not prevent the
	-- entire ChatMain GUI from starting.
	local settingsChanged = ChatSettings.SettingsChanged
	if settingsChanged and type(settingsChanged.Connect) == "function" then
		settingsChanged:Connect(function(setting, value)
			if setting == "ChatBarTextSize" then
				obj:SetTextSize(value)
			end
		end)
	else
		warn(
			"[Yeku ChatBar] DefaultClientChatModules.ChatSettings.SettingsChanged "
				.. "is missing; chat will continue without live text-size updates."
		)
	end

	coroutine.wrap(function()
		local success, canLocalUserChat = pcall(function()
			return Chat:CanUserChatAsync(LocalPlayer.UserId)
		end)

		local permitted = success
			and (RunService:IsStudio() or canLocalUserChat)

		if not permitted then
			obj.UserHasChatOff = true
			obj:DoLockChatBar()
		end
	end)()

	return obj
end

print("[Yeku Legacy ChatBar] source revision loaded: " .. YEKU_CHATBAR_SOURCE_REVISION)

return module

-- Patched legacy channel tab.

local module = {}

local Chat = game:GetService("Chat")
local ClientChatFolder = script:FindFirstAncestor("ClientChat")

local clientChatModules =
	ClientChatFolder:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
local modulesFolder = script.Parent

local ChatSettings = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatSettings", 10))
local CurveUtil = require(modulesFolder:WaitForChild("CurveUtil", 10))

local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods

local function CreateGuiObjects()
	local BaseFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	BaseFrame.Name = "BaseFrame"
	BaseFrame.Selectable = false
	BaseFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	BaseFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1

	local BackgroundFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	BackgroundFrame.Name = "BackgroundFrame"
	BackgroundFrame.Selectable = false
	BackgroundFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2)
	BackgroundFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 1)
	BackgroundFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	BackgroundFrame.Parent = BaseFrame

	local UnselectedFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	UnselectedFrame.Name = "UnselectedFrame"
	UnselectedFrame.Selectable = false
	UnselectedFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	UnselectedFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	UnselectedFrame.BackgroundColor3 = ChatSettings.ChannelsTabUnselectedColor
	UnselectedFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
	UnselectedFrame.Parent = BackgroundFrame

	local SelectedFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	SelectedFrame.Name = "SelectedFrame"
	SelectedFrame.Selectable = false
	SelectedFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	SelectedFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	SelectedFrame.BackgroundColor3 = ChatSettings.ChannelsTabSelectedColor
	SelectedFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	SelectedFrame.Visible = false
	SelectedFrame.Parent = BackgroundFrame

	local SelectedFrameBackgroundImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.Name = "BackgroundImage"
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.Selectable = false
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.BorderSizePixel = 0
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(94, 101, 115)
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.Parent = SelectedFrame

	local borderXOffset = 2
	local blueBarYSize = 4

	local BlueBarLeft = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	BlueBarLeft.Name = "BlueBarLeft"
	BlueBarLeft.Selectable = false
	BlueBarLeft.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -borderXOffset, 0, blueBarYSize)
	BlueBarLeft.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	BlueBarLeft.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
	BlueBarLeft.SliceCenter = Rect.new(3, 3, 32, 21)
	BlueBarLeft.Position = UDim2.new(0, borderXOffset, 1, -blueBarYSize)
	BlueBarLeft.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/SelectedBarLeft.png"
	BlueBarLeft.Parent = SelectedFrame

	local BlueBarRight = BlueBarLeft:Clone()
	BlueBarRight.Name = "BlueBarRight"
	BlueBarRight.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, blueBarYSize)
	BlueBarRight.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -blueBarYSize)
	BlueBarRight.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/SelectedBarRight.png"
	BlueBarRight.Parent = SelectedFrame

	local NameTag = Instance.new("TextButton")
	NameTag.Name = "NameTag"
	NameTag.Selectable = ChatSettings.GamepadNavigationEnabled
	NameTag.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	NameTag.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	NameTag.Font = ChatSettings.DefaultFont
	NameTag.TextSize = ChatSettings.ChatChannelsTabTextSize
	NameTag.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)

	NameTag.Text = ""

	NameTagNonSelect.Active = false

	NewMessageIconFrame.Name = "NewMessageIconFrame"
	NewMessageIconFrame.Selectable = false
	NewMessageIconFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
	NewMessageIconFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.8, -9, 0.5, -9)
	NewMessageIconFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	NewMessageIconFrame.Parent = BackgroundFrame

	local NewMessageIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	NewMessageIcon.Name = "NewMessageIcon"
	NewMessageIcon.Selectable = false
	NewMessageIcon.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	NewMessageIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	NewMessageIcon.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Chat/MessageCounter.png"
	NewMessageIcon.Visible = false
	NewMessageIcon.Parent = NewMessageIconFrame

	local NewMessageIconText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	NewMessageIconText.Name = "TextLabel"
	NewMessageIconText.Selectable = false
	NewMessageIconText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	NewMessageIconText.Size = UDim2.new(0, 13, 0, 9)
	NewMessageIconText.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -7, 0.5, -7)

		BaseFrame,

		NameTagNonSelect,

		NewMessageIcon,

function methods:Destroy()
	if self.GuiObject then
		self.GuiObject:Destroy()
	end
end

function methods:UpdateMessagePostedInChannel(ignoreActive)
	if self.Active and ignoreActive ~= true then
		return
	end

	self.UnreadMessageCount += 1

	local label = self.NewMessageIcon
	label.Visible = true

	local countLabel = label:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
	if countLabel then
		countLabel.Text = self.UnreadMessageCount &lt; 100
			and tostring(self.UnreadMessageCount)
			or "!"
	end

	local outPos = label.Position
	label.Position = UDim2.new(outPos.X.Scale, outPos.X.Offset, -0.15, 0)

	label:TweenPosition(
		outPos,
		Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
		Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
		0.15,
		true
	)
end

function methods:SetActive(active)
	self.Active = active
	self.UnselectedFrame.Visible = not active
	self.SelectedFrame.Visible = active

	if active then
		self.UnreadMessageCount = 0
		self.NewMessageIcon.Visible = false
		self.NameTag.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	else
		self.NameTag.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	end
end

function methods:SetTextSize(textSize)
	self.NameTag.TextSize = textSize
	self.NameTagNonSelect.TextSize = textSize
	self.NameTagSelect.TextSize = textSize
end

function methods:FadeOutBackground(duration)
	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 1
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:FadeInBackground(duration)
	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:FadeOutText(duration)
	self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency = 1
	self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)

	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_TargetTransparency = 1
	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:FadeInText(duration)
	self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency = 0
	self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)

	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_TargetTransparency = 0.75
	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:AnimGuiObjects()
	local backgroundTransparency = self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency
	local textTransparency = self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency
	local strokeTransparency = self.AnimParams.TextStroke_CurrentTransparency

	if self.UnselectedFrame then
		self.UnselectedFrame.BackgroundTransparency = backgroundTransparency
	end

	-- The tab can receive its first animation tick while SelectedFrame's
	-- image children are still being recreated. Resolve each child safely so
	-- an absent BackgroundImage cannot abort the chat/topbar startup thread.
	local selectedFrame = self.SelectedFrame
	if selectedFrame then
		local selectedBackground = selectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BackgroundImage")
		if selectedBackground and selectedBackground:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			selectedBackground.BackgroundTransparency = backgroundTransparency
			selectedBackground.ImageTransparency = backgroundTransparency
		end
		local blueBarLeft = selectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BlueBarLeft")
		if blueBarLeft and blueBarLeft:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			blueBarLeft.ImageTransparency = backgroundTransparency
		end
		local blueBarRight = selectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BlueBarRight")
		if blueBarRight and blueBarRight:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			blueBarRight.ImageTransparency = backgroundTransparency
		end
	end

	self.NameTagNonSelect.TextTransparency = backgroundTransparency
	self.NameTagNonSelect.TextStrokeTransparency = backgroundTransparency

	self.NameTag.TextTransparency = textTransparency
	self.NameTagSelect.TextTransparency = textTransparency
	self.NameTag.TextStrokeTransparency = strokeTransparency
	self.NameTagSelect.TextStrokeTransparency = strokeTransparency

	local countLabel = self.NewMessageIcon:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
	if countLabel then
		countLabel.TextTransparency = textTransparency
		countLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = strokeTransparency
	end

	self.NewMessageIcon.ImageTransparency = textTransparency
end

function methods:InitializeAnimParams()
	self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency = 0
	self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency = 0
	self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(0)

	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_TargetTransparency = 0.75
	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_CurrentTransparency = 0.75
	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(0)

	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(0)
end

function methods:Update(dtScale)
	self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency = CurveUtil:Expt(
		self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue,
		dtScale
	)

	self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency = CurveUtil:Expt(
		self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue,
		dtScale
	)

	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_CurrentTransparency = CurveUtil:Expt(
		self.AnimParams.TextStroke_CurrentTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.TextStroke_TargetTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.TextStroke_NormalizedExptValue,
		dtScale
	)

	self:AnimGuiObjects()
end

function module.new(channelName)
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)

	local BaseFrame, NameTag, NameTagNonSelect, NameTagSelect,
		NewMessageIcon, UnselectedFrame, SelectedFrame = CreateGuiObjects()

	obj.GuiObject = BaseFrame
	obj.NameTag = NameTag
	obj.NameTagNonSelect = NameTagNonSelect
	obj.NameTagSelect = NameTagSelect
	obj.NewMessageIcon = NewMessageIcon
	obj.UnselectedFrame = UnselectedFrame
	obj.SelectedFrame = SelectedFrame

	obj.BlueBarLeft = SelectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BlueBarLeft")
	obj.BlueBarRight = SelectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BlueBarRight")
	obj.BackgroundImage = SelectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BackgroundImage")
	obj.WhiteTextNewMessageNotification =
		NewMessageIcon:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")

	obj.ChannelName = channelName
	obj.UnreadMessageCount = 0
	obj.Active = false

	obj.GuiObject.Name = "Frame_" .. channelName

	local displayName = channelName
	if #displayName &gt; ChatSettings.MaxChannelNameLength then
		displayName = string.sub(
			displayName,
			1,
			math.max(1, ChatSettings.MaxChannelNameLength - 3)
		) .. "..."
	end

	obj.NameTag.Text = ""
	obj.NameTagNonSelect.Text = displayName
	obj.NameTagSelect.Text = displayName

	obj.AnimParams = {}
	obj:InitializeAnimParams()
	obj:AnimGuiObjects()
	obj:SetActive(false)

	return obj
end

			Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
			Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
			0.2,
			true
		)

	local scale = 0.7

	local pageButtonImage = "rbxassetid://471630199"

	end)

	PageRightButton.Activated:Connect(function()

	end)

	self:ScrollChannelsFrame(0)
end

function methods:UpdateMessagePostedInChannel(channelName)
	local tab = self:GetChannelTab(channelName)

	if tab then
		tab:UpdateMessagePostedInChannel()
	else
		warn("[LegacyChat] Channel tab does not exist: " .. tostring(channelName))
	end
end

function methods:AddChannelTab(channelName)
	if self:GetChannelTab(channelName) then
		warn("[LegacyChat] Duplicate channel tab ignored: " .. tostring(channelName))
		return self:GetChannelTab(channelName)
	end

	local tab = moduleChannelsTab.new(channelName)
	tab.GuiObject.Parent = self.GuiObjects.ScrollerFrame
	self.ChannelTabs[string.lower(channelName)] = tab

	self.NumTabs += 1
	self:OrganizeChannelTabs()

	if ChatSettings.RightClickToLeaveChannelEnabled then
		tab.NameTag.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
			self.LeaveConfirmationNotice.Text =
				string.format("Leave channel %s?", tab.ChannelName)

			self.GuiObjects.LeaveTarget.Value = tab.ChannelName

			self.GuiObjects.LeaveConfirmationFrame:TweenPosition(
				UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
				Enum.EasingDirection.In,
				Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
				0.2,
				true
			)
		end)
	end

	return tab
end

function methods:RemoveChannelTab(channelName)
	local indexName = string.lower(channelName)
	local tab = self.ChannelTabs[indexName]

	if not tab then
		warn("[LegacyChat] Cannot remove missing tab: " .. tostring(channelName))
		return
	end

	tab:Destroy()
	self.ChannelTabs[indexName] = nil

	self.NumTabs = math.max(0, self.NumTabs - 1)
	self.CurPageNum = math.min(
		self.CurPageNum,
		math.max(0, self.NumTabs - math.max(1, ChatSettings.ChannelsBarFullTabSize))
	)

	self:OrganizeChannelTabs()
end

function methods:GetChannelTab(channelName)
	if not channelName then
		return nil
	end

	return self.ChannelTabs[string.lower(channelName)]
end

function methods:OrganizeChannelTabs()
	local order = {}
	local added = {}

	local function appendTab(tab)
		if tab and not added[tab] then
			added[tab] = true
			table.insert(order, tab)
		end
	end

	appendTab(self:GetChannelTab(ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName))
	appendTab(self:GetChannelTab("System"))

	local otherTabs = {}

	for _, tab in pairs(self.ChannelTabs) do
		if not added[tab] then
			table.insert(otherTabs, tab)
		end
	end

	table.sort(otherTabs, function(a, b)
		return string.lower(a.ChannelName) &lt; string.lower(b.ChannelName)
	end)

	for _, tab in ipairs(otherTabs) do
		appendTab(tab)
	end

	for index, tab in ipairs(order) do
		tab.GuiObject.Position = UDim2.new(index - 1, 0, 0, 0)
	end

	local visibleTabCount = math.max(
		1,
		math.min(ChatSettings.ChannelsBarFullTabSize, self.NumTabs)
	)

	self.GuiObjects.ScrollerSizer.Size =
		UDim2.new(1 / visibleTabCount, 0, 1, 0)

	self:ScrollChannelsFrame(0)
end

function methods:ResizeChannelTabText(textSize)
	for _, tab in pairs(self.ChannelTabs) do
		tab:SetTextSize(textSize)
	end
end

function methods:ScrollChannelsFrame(dir)
	if self.ScrollChannelsFrameLock then
		return
	end

	if not self.GuiObjects.ScrollerFrame then
		return
	end

	self.ScrollChannelsFrameLock = true

	local tabNumber = math.max(1, ChatSettings.ChannelsBarFullTabSize)
	local maxPageNum = math.max(0, self.NumTabs - tabNumber)
	local newPageNum = math.clamp(self.CurPageNum + dir, 0, maxPageNum)

	self.CurPageNum = newPageNum

	local endPos = UDim2.new(-self.CurPageNum, 0, 0, 0)

	self.GuiObjects.PageLeftButton.Visible = self.CurPageNum &gt; 0
	self.GuiObjects.PageRightButton.Visible =
		self.CurPageNum &lt; maxPageNum

	if dir == 0 then
		self.GuiObjects.ScrollerFrame.Position = endPos
		self.ScrollChannelsFrameLock = false
		return
	end

	local function unlock()
		self.ScrollChannelsFrameLock = false
	end

	self:WaitUntilParentedCorrectly()

	self.GuiObjects.ScrollerFrame:TweenPosition(
		endPos,
		Enum.EasingDirection.InOut,
		Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
		0.15,
		true,
		unlock
	)
end

function methods:FadeOutBackground(duration)
	for _, channelObj in pairs(self.ChannelTabs) do
		channelObj:FadeOutBackground(duration)
	end

	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 1
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:FadeInBackground(duration)
	for _, channelObj in pairs(self.ChannelTabs) do
		channelObj:FadeInBackground(duration)
	end

	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:FadeOutText(duration)
	for _, channelObj in pairs(self.ChannelTabs) do
		channelObj:FadeOutText(duration)
	end
end

function methods:FadeInText(duration)
	for _, channelObj in pairs(self.ChannelTabs) do
		channelObj:FadeInText(duration)
	end
end

function methods:AnimGuiObjects()
	if not self.GuiObjects.PageLeftButton then
		return
	end

	local transparency = self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency

	self.GuiObjects.PageLeftButton.ImageTransparency = transparency
	self.GuiObjects.PageRightButton.ImageTransparency = transparency
	self.GuiObjects.PageLeftButtonArrow.ImageTransparency = transparency
	self.GuiObjects.PageRightButtonArrow.ImageTransparency = transparency
end

function methods:InitializeAnimParams()
	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(0)
end

function methods:Update(dtScale)
	for _, channelObj in pairs(self.ChannelTabs) do
		channelObj:Update(dtScale)
	end

	self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency = CurveUtil:Expt(
		self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue,
		dtScale
	)

	self:AnimGuiObjects()
end

function methods:WaitUntilParentedCorrectly()
	while self.GuiObject
		and not self.GuiObject:IsDescendantOf(LocalPlayer) do
		self.GuiObject.AncestryChanged:Wait()
	end
end

function module.new()
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)

	obj.GuiObject = nil
	obj.GuiObjects = {}
	obj.ChannelTabs = {}
	obj.NumTabs = 0
	obj.CurPageNum = 0
	obj.ScrollChannelsFrameLock = false
	obj.AnimParams = {}

	obj:InitializeAnimParams()

	ChatSettings.SettingsChanged:Connect(function(setting, value)
		if setting == "ChatChannelsTabTextSize" then
			obj:ResizeChannelTabText(value)
		end
	end)

	return obj
end

-- the ChatBar, and bubble rendering. Do not create game.Chat.ClientChatModules,

                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">-- FileName: MessageLogDisplay.lua
-- One copy only. Duplicate source removed.

local module = {}
module.ScrollBarThickness = 4

local Chat = game:GetService("Chat")
local ClientChatFolder = script:FindFirstAncestor("ClientChat")

local clientChatModules =
	ClientChatFolder:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
local modulesFolder = script.Parent

local moduleMessageLabelCreator = require(modulesFolder:WaitForChild("MessageLabelCreator", 10))
local CurveUtil = require(modulesFolder:WaitForChild("CurveUtil", 10))
local ChatSettings = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatSettings", 10))

local FlagFixChatMessageLogPerformance = false

pcall(function()
	FlagFixChatMessageLogPerformance =
		settings():IsUserFeatureEnabled("UserFixChatMessageLogPerformance")
end)

local MessageLabelCreator = moduleMessageLabelCreator.new()

local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods

local function CreateGuiObjects()
	local BaseFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	BaseFrame.Name = "MessageLogDisplay"
	BaseFrame.Selectable = false
	BaseFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	BaseFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1

	local Scroller = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	Scroller.Name = "Scroller"
	Scroller.Selectable = ChatSettings.GamepadNavigationEnabled
	Scroller.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	Scroller.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Scroller.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 3)
	Scroller.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -6)
	Scroller.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	Scroller.ScrollBarThickness = module.ScrollBarThickness
	Scroller.Active = false
	Scroller.Parent = BaseFrame

	local Layout

	if FlagFixChatMessageLogPerformance then
		Layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
		Layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
		Layout.Parent = Scroller
	end

	return BaseFrame, Scroller, Layout
end

function methods:Destroy()
	if self.GuiObject then
		self.GuiObject:Destroy()
	end

	self.Destroyed = true
end

function methods:SetActive(active)
	self.GuiObject.Visible = active
end

function methods:UpdateMessageFiltered(messageData)
	for _, messageObject in ipairs(self.MessageObjectLog) do
		if messageObject.ID == messageData.ID then
			local isScrolledDown = self:IsScrolledDown()
			messageObject.UpdateTextFunction(messageData)

			if FlagFixChatMessageLogPerformance then
				self:PositionMessageLabelInWindow(messageObject, isScrolledDown)
			else
				self:ReorderAllMessages()
			end

			return
		end
	end
end

function methods:AddMessage(messageData)
	self:WaitUntilParentedCorrectly()

	local messageObject = MessageLabelCreator:CreateMessageLabel(
		messageData,
		self.CurrentChannelName
	)

	if not messageObject then
		return
	end

	table.insert(self.MessageObjectLog, messageObject)
	self:PositionMessageLabelInWindow(messageObject)
end

function methods:AddMessageAtIndex(messageData, index)
	local messageObject = MessageLabelCreator:CreateMessageLabel(
		messageData,
		self.CurrentChannelName
	)

	if not messageObject then
		return
	end

	table.insert(self.MessageObjectLog, index, messageObject)

	local wasScrolledToBottom = self:IsScrolledDown()
	self:ReorderAllMessages()

	if wasScrolledToBottom then
		self.Scroller.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(
			0,
			math.max(0, self.Scroller.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - self.Scroller.AbsoluteSize.Y)
		)
	end
end

function methods:RemoveLastMessage()
	self:WaitUntilParentedCorrectly()

	local firstMessage = self.MessageObjectLog[1]
	if not firstMessage then
		return
	end

	local posOffset = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, firstMessage.BaseFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y)

	firstMessage:Destroy()
	table.remove(self.MessageObjectLog, 1)

	if not FlagFixChatMessageLogPerformance then
		for _, messageObject in ipairs(self.MessageObjectLog) do
			messageObject.BaseFrame.Position =
				messageObject.BaseFrame.Position - posOffset
		end

		self.Scroller.CanvasSize = self.Scroller.CanvasSize - posOffset
	end
end

function methods:IsScrolledDown()
	local yCanvasSize = self.Scroller.CanvasSize.Y.Offset
	local yContainerSize = self.Scroller.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y
	local yScrolledPosition = self.Scroller.CanvasPosition.Y

	return yCanvasSize &lt; yContainerSize
		or yCanvasSize - yScrolledPosition &lt;= yContainerSize + 5
end

function methods:PositionMessageLabelInWindow(messageObject, isScrolledDown)
	self:WaitUntilParentedCorrectly()

	local baseFrame = messageObject.BaseFrame

	if FlagFixChatMessageLogPerformance then
		if isScrolledDown == nil then
			isScrolledDown = self:IsScrolledDown()
		end

		baseFrame.LayoutOrder = messageObject.ID
	else
		baseFrame.Parent = self.Scroller
		baseFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, self.Scroller.CanvasSize.Y.Offset)
	end

	baseFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
		1,
		0,
		0,
		messageObject.GetHeightFunction(self.Scroller.AbsoluteSize.X)
	)

	if FlagFixChatMessageLogPerformance then
		baseFrame.Parent = self.Scroller
	end

	if messageObject.BaseMessage then
		if FlagFixChatMessageLogPerformance then
			for i = 1, 10 do
				if messageObject.BaseMessage.TextFits then
					break
				end

				local trySize = math.max(1, self.Scroller.AbsoluteSize.X - i)
				baseFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
					1,
					0,
					0,
					messageObject.GetHeightFunction(trySize)
				)
			end
		else
			local trySize = self.Scroller.AbsoluteSize.X
			local minTrySize = math.max(1, self.Scroller.AbsoluteSize.X - 10)

			while not messageObject.BaseMessage.TextFits and trySize &gt; minTrySize do
				trySize -= 1

				baseFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
					1,
					0,
					0,
					messageObject.GetHeightFunction(trySize)
				)
			end
		end
	end

	if FlagFixChatMessageLogPerformance then
		if isScrolledDown then
			local scrollValue =
				self.Scroller.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - self.Scroller.AbsoluteSize.Y

			self.Scroller.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, scrollValue))
		end
	else
		isScrolledDown = self:IsScrolledDown()

		self.Scroller.CanvasSize += UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, baseFrame.Size.Y.Offset)

		if isScrolledDown then
			self.Scroller.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(
				0,
				math.max(0, self.Scroller.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - self.Scroller.AbsoluteSize.Y)
			)
		end
	end
end

function methods:ReorderAllMessages()
	self:WaitUntilParentedCorrectly()

	if self.GuiObject.AbsoluteSize.Y &lt; 1 then
		return
	end

	local oldCanvasPosition = self.Scroller.CanvasPosition
	local wasScrolledDown = self:IsScrolledDown()

	self.Scroller.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

	for _, messageObject in ipairs(self.MessageObjectLog) do
		self:PositionMessageLabelInWindow(messageObject, wasScrolledDown)
	end

	if not wasScrolledDown then
		self.Scroller.CanvasPosition = oldCanvasPosition
	end
end

function methods:Clear()
	for _, messageObject in ipairs(self.MessageObjectLog) do
		messageObject:Destroy()
	end

	table.clear(self.MessageObjectLog)

	if not FlagFixChatMessageLogPerformance then
		self.Scroller.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	end
end

function methods:SetCurrentChannelName(name)
	self.CurrentChannelName = name
end

function methods:FadeOutBackground(_duration)
	-- Intentionally handled by the containing ChatWindow.
end

function methods:FadeInBackground(_duration)
	-- Intentionally handled by the containing ChatWindow.
end

function methods:FadeOutText(duration)
	for _, messageObject in ipairs(self.MessageObjectLog) do
		if messageObject.FadeOutFunction then
			messageObject.FadeOutFunction(duration, CurveUtil)
		end
	end
end

function methods:FadeInText(duration)
	for _, messageObject in ipairs(self.MessageObjectLog) do
		if messageObject.FadeInFunction then
			messageObject.FadeInFunction(duration, CurveUtil)
		end
	end
end

function methods:Update(dtScale)
	for _, messageObject in ipairs(self.MessageObjectLog) do
		if messageObject.UpdateAnimFunction then
			messageObject.UpdateAnimFunction(dtScale, CurveUtil)
		end
	end
end

function methods:WaitUntilParentedCorrectly()
	while self.GuiObject
		and not self.GuiObject:IsDescendantOf(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer) do
		self.GuiObject.AncestryChanged:Wait()
	end
end

function module.new()
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)

	obj.Destroyed = false

	local BaseFrame, Scroller, Layout = CreateGuiObjects()
	obj.GuiObject = BaseFrame
	obj.Scroller = Scroller
	obj.Layout = Layout

	obj.MessageObjectLog = {}
	obj.Name = "MessageLogDisplay"
	obj.GuiObject.Name = "Frame_" .. obj.Name
	obj.CurrentChannelName = ""

	obj.GuiObject:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
		task.spawn(function()
			obj:ReorderAllMessages()
		end)
	end)

	if FlagFixChatMessageLogPerformance and obj.Layout then
		local wasScrolledDown = true

		obj.Layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
			local size = obj.Layout.AbsoluteContentSize
			obj.Scroller.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, size.Y)

			if wasScrolledDown then
				local windowSize = obj.Scroller.AbsoluteWindowSize
				obj.Scroller.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(
					0,
					math.max(0, size.Y - windowSize.Y)
				)
			end
		end)

		obj.Scroller:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
			wasScrolledDown = obj:IsScrolledDown()
		end)
	end

	return obj
end

                                        <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-115</int64>
                                        <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                                    </Properties>
                                </Item>
                                <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX145">
                                    <Properties>
                                        <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                        <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                        <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                                        <ContentId name="LinkedSource">
                                            <null />
                                        </ContentId>
                                        <string name="Name">CommandProcessor</string>
                                        <string name="ScriptGuid">{f507664d-a07c-4e94-8cb0-41fee724ff99}</string>
                                        <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    FileName: CommandProcessor.lua
    Yeku Legacy CoreGui compatibility rewrite.

    Fixes:
    - Resolves the bundled ClientChat hierarchy.
    - Uses the bundled DefaultClientChatModules only.
    - Never creates or waits for a global Chat.ClientChatModules alias.
    - Does not wait indefinitely for missing module folders.
    - Loads command modules independently and preserves processing order.
    - Protects message processors from throwing errors.
    - Does not create fake network RemoteEvents.
]]

local module = {}

local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods


local function findChild(parent, name)
	if not parent then
		return nil
	end

	return parent:FindFirstChild(name)
end

local function hasBundledModules(instance)
	local defaults = instance and instance:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
	return defaults ~= nil and defaults:IsA("Folder")
end

local function findClientChatFolder()
	local namedAncestor = script:FindFirstAncestor("ClientChat")

	if namedAncestor and hasBundledModules(namedAncestor) then
		return namedAncestor
	end

	local current = script.Parent

	while current and current ~= game do
		if hasBundledModules(current) then
			return current
		end

		current = current.Parent
	end

	return nil
end

local function requireModule(instance, description)
	if not instance then
		error(
			"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Missing " .. description,
			2
		)
	end

	if not instance:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		error(
			"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Expected a ModuleScript for "
				.. description
				.. ", found "
				.. instance.ClassName
				.. " at "
				.. instance:GetFullName(),
			2
		)
	end

	local success, result = pcall(require, instance)

	if not success then
		error(
			"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Failed requiring "
				.. instance:GetFullName()
				.. ": "
				.. tostring(result),
			2
		)
	end

	return result
end

local ClientChatFolder = findClientChatFolder()

if not ClientChatFolder then
	error(
		"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Could not locate the bundled "
			.. "ClientChat folder from "
			.. script:GetFullName()
	)
end

local clientChatModules =
	findChild(ClientChatFolder, "DefaultClientChatModules")

if not clientChatModules or not clientChatModules:IsA("Folder") then
	error(
		"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Missing bundled DefaultClientChatModules under "
			.. ClientChatFolder:GetFullName()
	)
end

local commandModules = findChild(clientChatModules, "CommandModules")

if not commandModules or not commandModules:IsA("Folder") then
	error(
		"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Missing CommandModules under "
			.. clientChatModules:GetFullName()
	)
end

local commandUtilModule = findChild(commandModules, "Util")
local chatSettingsModule = findChild(clientChatModules, "ChatSettings")

local commandUtil = requireModule(
	commandUtilModule,
	commandModules:GetFullName() .. ".Util"
)

local ChatSettings = requireModule(
	chatSettingsModule,
	clientChatModules:GetFullName() .. ".ChatSettings"
)

if type(commandUtil) ~= "table" then
	error(
		"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] CommandModules.Util "
			.. "did not return a table."
	)
end

local requiredConstants = {
	"KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE",
	"KEY_PROCESSOR_FUNCTION",
	"IN_PROGRESS_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR",
	"COMPLETED_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR",
}

for _, constantName in ipairs(requiredConstants) do
	if commandUtil[constantName] == nil then
		error(
			"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] CommandModules.Util is missing "
				.. constantName
		)
	end
end

function methods:SetupCommandProcessors()
	for _, commandModule in ipairs(commandModules:GetChildren()) do
		if commandModule:IsA("ModuleScript")
			and commandModule.Name ~= "Util" then


			-- Require only package-owned command modules; no Chat service alias is used.
			local success, commandProcessor = pcall(require, commandModule)

			if not success then
				warn(
					"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Failed loading "
						.. commandModule:GetFullName()
						.. ": "
						.. tostring(commandProcessor)
				)

				continue
			end

			if type(commandProcessor) ~= "table" then
				warn(
					"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] "
						.. commandModule:GetFullName()
						.. " did not return a table."
				)

				continue
			end

			local processorType =
				commandProcessor[commandUtil.KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE]

			local processorFunction =
				commandProcessor[commandUtil.KEY_PROCESSOR_FUNCTION]

			if type(processorFunction) ~= "function" then
				warn(
					"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Ignoring "
						.. commandModule:GetFullName()
						.. " because its processor function is missing."
				)

				continue
			end

			if processorType == commandUtil.IN_PROGRESS_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR then
				table.insert(
					self.InProgressMessageProcessors,
					processorFunction
				)
			elseif processorType == commandUtil.COMPLETED_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR then
				table.insert(
					self.CompletedMessageProcessors,
					processorFunction
				)
			end
		end
	end
end

function methods:ProcessCompletedChatMessage(message, ChatWindow)
	for _, processor in ipairs(self.CompletedMessageProcessors) do
		local success, result = pcall(
			processor,
			message,
			ChatWindow,
			ChatSettings
		)

		if not success then
			warn(
				"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Completed-message processor "
					.. "failed: "
					.. tostring(result)
			)
		elseif result then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

function methods:ProcessInProgressChatMessage(
	message,
	ChatWindow,
	ChatBar
)
	for _, processor in ipairs(self.InProgressMessageProcessors) do
		local success, customState = pcall(
			processor,
			message,
			ChatWindow,
			ChatBar,
			ChatSettings
		)

		if not success then
			warn(
				"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] In-progress processor "
					.. "failed: "
					.. tostring(customState)
			)
		elseif customState then
			return customState
		end
	end

	return nil
end

function module.new()
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)

	obj.CompletedMessageProcessors = {}
	obj.InProgressMessageProcessors = {}

	obj:SetupCommandProcessors()

	print(
		"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Initialized: "
			.. tostring(#obj.CompletedMessageProcessors)
			.. " completed processor(s), "
			.. tostring(#obj.InProgressMessageProcessors)
			.. " in-progress processor(s)."
	)

	return obj
end

return module</ProtectedString>
                                        <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-116</int64>
                                        <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                                    </Properties>
                                </Item>
                                <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX146">
                                    <Properties>
                                        <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                        <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                        <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                                        <ContentId name="LinkedSource">
                                            <null />
                                        </ContentId>
                                        <string name="Name">MessageLabelCreator</string>
                                        <string name="ScriptGuid">{f16bb412-dbbc-4e32-9f84-613be8cea428}</string>
                                        <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">-- FileName: MessageLabelCreator.lua

local OBJECT_POOL_SIZE = 50

local module = {}

local Chat = game:GetService("Chat")
local ClientChatFolder = script:FindFirstAncestor("ClientChat")

assert(
	ClientChatFolder and ClientChatFolder:IsA("Folder"),
	"[LegacyChat][MessageLabelCreator] Expected the bundled ClientChat folder."
)
local clientChatModules = ClientChatFolder:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
assert(
	clientChatModules and clientChatModules:IsA("Folder"),
	"[LegacyChat][MessageLabelCreator] Missing bundled DefaultClientChatModules under "
		.. ClientChatFolder:GetFullName()
)
local modulesFolder = script.Parent
local messageCreatorModules = clientChatModules:WaitForChild("MessageCreatorModules", 10)
assert(
	messageCreatorModules and messageCreatorModules:IsA("Folder"),
	"[LegacyChat][MessageLabelCreator] Missing DefaultClientChatModules.MessageCreatorModules."
)
local messageCreatorUtilModule = messageCreatorModules:WaitForChild("Util", 10)
assert(
	messageCreatorUtilModule and messageCreatorUtilModule:IsA("ModuleScript"),
	"[LegacyChat][MessageLabelCreator] Missing MessageCreatorModules.Util."
)
local messageCreatorUtil = require(messageCreatorUtilModule)

local modulesFolder = script.Parent
local commonFolder = script.Parent.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("Common")
local objectPoolModule = commonFolder and commonFolder:WaitForChild("ObjectPool", 10)
assert(objectPoolModule and objectPoolModule:IsA("ModuleScript"),
	"[LegacyChat][MessageLabelCreator] Missing bundled Common.ObjectPool.")
local moduleObjectPool = require(objectPoolModule)

local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods

local function ReturnToObjectPoolRecursive(instance, objectPool)
	for _, child in ipairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
		ReturnToObjectPoolRecursive(child, objectPool)
	end

	instance.Parent = nil
	objectPool:ReturnInstance(instance)
end

local function GetMessageCreators()
	local typeToFunction = {}

	for _, creatorModule in ipairs(messageCreatorModules:GetChildren()) do
		if creatorModule:IsA("ModuleScript") and creatorModule.Name ~= "Util" then
			local ok, creator = pcall(require, creatorModule)

			if ok and type(creator) == "table" then
				local messageType = creator[messageCreatorUtil.KEY_MESSAGE_TYPE]
				local createFunction = creator[messageCreatorUtil.KEY_CREATOR_FUNCTION]

				if messageType ~= nil and type(createFunction) == "function" then
					typeToFunction[messageType] = createFunction
				end
			else
				warn(
					"[LegacyChat] Failed loading message creator "
						.. creatorModule.Name
						.. ": "
						.. tostring(creator)
				)
			end
		end
	end

	return typeToFunction
end

function methods:WrapIntoMessageObject(messageData, createdMessageObject)
	local BaseFrame = createdMessageObject[messageCreatorUtil.KEY_BASE_FRAME]
	local BaseMessage

	if messageCreatorUtil.KEY_BASE_MESSAGE then
		BaseMessage = createdMessageObject[messageCreatorUtil.KEY_BASE_MESSAGE]
	end

	local obj = {
		ID = messageData.ID,
		BaseFrame = BaseFrame,
		BaseMessage = BaseMessage,
		UpdateTextFunction = createdMessageObject[messageCreatorUtil.KEY_UPDATE_TEXT_FUNC],
		GetHeightFunction = createdMessageObject[messageCreatorUtil.KEY_GET_HEIGHT],
		FadeInFunction = createdMessageObject[messageCreatorUtil.KEY_FADE_IN],
		FadeOutFunction = createdMessageObject[messageCreatorUtil.KEY_FADE_OUT],
		UpdateAnimFunction = createdMessageObject[messageCreatorUtil.KEY_UPDATE_ANIMATION],
		ObjectPool = self.ObjectPool,
		Destroyed = false,
	}

	if type(obj.UpdateTextFunction) ~= "function" then
		obj.UpdateTextFunction = function()
			warn("[LegacyChat] Message creator has no text update function.")
		end
	end

	function obj:Destroy()
		if self.Destroyed then
			return
		end

		if self.BaseFrame then
			ReturnToObjectPoolRecursive(self.BaseFrame, self.ObjectPool)
		end

		self.Destroyed = true
	end

	return obj
end

function methods:CreateMessageLabel(messageData, currentChannelName)
	local messageType = messageData.MessageType
	local creatorFunction = self.MessageCreators[messageType]

	if not creatorFunction and self.DefaultCreatorType then
		creatorFunction = self.MessageCreators[self.DefaultCreatorType]
	end

	if not creatorFunction then
		warn("[LegacyChat] No message creator for message type: " .. tostring(messageType))
		return nil
	end

	local ok, createdMessageObject = pcall(
		creatorFunction,
		messageData,
		currentChannelName
	)

	if not ok then
		warn("[LegacyChat] Message creator failed: " .. tostring(createdMessageObject))
		return nil
	end

	if not createdMessageObject then
		return nil
	end

	return self:WrapIntoMessageObject(messageData, createdMessageObject)
end

function module.new()
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)

	obj.ObjectPool = moduleObjectPool.new(OBJECT_POOL_SIZE)
	obj.MessageCreators = GetMessageCreators()
	obj.DefaultCreatorType = messageCreatorUtil.DEFAULT_MESSAGE_CREATOR

	messageCreatorUtil:RegisterObjectPool(obj.ObjectPool)

	return obj
end

function module:GetStringTextBounds(text, font, textSize, sizeBounds)
	return messageCreatorUtil:GetStringTextBounds(text, font, textSize, sizeBounds)
end

                                        <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-117</int64>

                                        <string name="ScriptGuid">{0100ef28-657b-4b5e-a345-970a7186d0b5}</string>
                                        <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">-- FileName: CurveUtil.lua

local CurveUtil = {}

local DEFAULT_THRESHOLD = 0.01

function CurveUtil:Expt(startValue, targetValue, pct, dtScale)
	if math.abs(targetValue - startValue) &lt; DEFAULT_THRESHOLD then
		return targetValue
	end

	local amount = self:Expty(startValue, targetValue, pct, dtScale)
	return startValue + ((targetValue - startValue) * amount)
end

function CurveUtil:Expty(_, _, pct, dtScale)
	local friction = math.clamp(1 - pct, 0.000001, 1)
	local a = -math.log(friction)

	return 1 - math.exp(-a * dtScale)
end

function CurveUtil:Sign(value)
	if value &gt; 0 then
		return 1
	elseif value &lt; 0 then
		return -1
	end

	return 0
end

function CurveUtil:BezierValForT(p0, p1, p2, p3, t)
	local cp0 = (1 - t) ^ 3
	local cp1 = 3 * t * (1 - t) ^ 2
	local cp2 = 3 * t ^ 2 * (1 - t)
	local cp3 = t ^ 3

	return cp0 * p0 + cp1 * p1 + cp2 * p2 + cp3 * p3
end

CurveUtil._BezierPt2ForT = { x = 0, y = 0 }

function CurveUtil:BezierPt2ForT(
	p0x, p0y,
	p1x, p1y,
	p2x, p2y,
	p3x, p3y,
	t
)
	self._BezierPt2ForT.x = self:BezierValForT(p0x, p1x, p2x, p3x, t)
	self._BezierPt2ForT.y = self:BezierValForT(p0y, p1y, p2y, p3y, t)

	return self._BezierPt2ForT
end

function CurveUtil:YForPointOf2PtLine(pt1, pt2, x)
	if pt1.x == pt2.x then
		return pt1.y
	end

	local m = (pt1.y - pt2.y) / (pt1.x - pt2.x)
	local b = pt1.y - m * pt1.x

	return m * x + b
end

function CurveUtil:DeltaTimeToTimescale(frameDeltaTime)
	return frameDeltaTime / (1 / 60)
end

function CurveUtil:SecondsToTick(seconds)
	if seconds &lt;= 0 then
		return math.huge
	end

	return (1 / 60) / seconds
end

function CurveUtil:ExptValueInSeconds(threshold, startValue, seconds)
	if seconds &lt;= 0 then
		return 1
	end

	return 1 - math.pow(threshold / startValue, 1 / (60 * seconds))
end

function CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(seconds)
	return self:ExptValueInSeconds(DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, 1, seconds)
end

return CurveUtil</ProtectedString>
                                        <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-118</int64>
                                        <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                                    </Properties>
                                </Item>
                                <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX148">
                                    <Properties>
                                        <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                        <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                        <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                                        <ContentId name="LinkedSource">
                                            <null />
                                        </ContentId>
                                        <string name="Name">ProcessCommands</string>
                                        <string name="ScriptGuid">{0f8d949a-70c8-4101-ab05-ff708030172a}</string>
                                        <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    FileName: CommandProcessor.lua
    Yeku Legacy CoreGui compatibility rewrite.

    Fixes:
    - Resolves the bundled ClientChat hierarchy.
    - Uses the bundled DefaultClientChatModules only.
    - Never creates or waits for a global Chat.ClientChatModules alias.
    - Does not wait indefinitely for missing module folders.
    - Loads command modules independently and preserves processing order.
    - Protects message processors from throwing errors.
    - Does not create fake network RemoteEvents.
]]

local module = {}

local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods


local function findChild(parent, name)
	if not parent then
		return nil
	end

	return parent:FindFirstChild(name)
end

local function hasBundledModules(instance)
	local defaults = instance and instance:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
	return defaults ~= nil and defaults:IsA("Folder")
end

local function findClientChatFolder()
	local namedAncestor = script:FindFirstAncestor("ClientChat")

	if namedAncestor and hasBundledModules(namedAncestor) then
		return namedAncestor
	end

	local current = script.Parent

	while current and current ~= game do
		if hasBundledModules(current) then
			return current
		end

		current = current.Parent
	end

	return nil
end

local function requireModule(instance, description)
	if not instance then
		error(
			"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Missing " .. description,
			2
		)
	end

	if not instance:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		error(
			"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Expected a ModuleScript for "
				.. description
				.. ", found "
				.. instance.ClassName
				.. " at "
				.. instance:GetFullName(),
			2
		)
	end

	local success, result = pcall(require, instance)

	if not success then
		error(
			"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Failed requiring "
				.. instance:GetFullName()
				.. ": "
				.. tostring(result),
			2
		)
	end

	return result
end

local ClientChatFolder = findClientChatFolder()

if not ClientChatFolder then
	error(
		"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Could not locate the bundled "
			.. "ClientChat folder from "
			.. script:GetFullName()
	)
end

local clientChatModules =
	findChild(ClientChatFolder, "DefaultClientChatModules")

if not clientChatModules or not clientChatModules:IsA("Folder") then
	error(
		"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Missing bundled DefaultClientChatModules under "
			.. ClientChatFolder:GetFullName()
	)
end

local commandModules = findChild(clientChatModules, "CommandModules")

if not commandModules or not commandModules:IsA("Folder") then
	error(
		"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Missing CommandModules under "
			.. clientChatModules:GetFullName()
	)
end

local commandUtilModule = findChild(commandModules, "Util")
local chatSettingsModule = findChild(clientChatModules, "ChatSettings")

local commandUtil = requireModule(
	commandUtilModule,
	commandModules:GetFullName() .. ".Util"
)

local ChatSettings = requireModule(
	chatSettingsModule,
	clientChatModules:GetFullName() .. ".ChatSettings"
)

if type(commandUtil) ~= "table" then
	error(
		"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] CommandModules.Util "
			.. "did not return a table."
	)
end

local requiredConstants = {
	"KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE",
	"KEY_PROCESSOR_FUNCTION",
	"IN_PROGRESS_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR",
	"COMPLETED_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR",
}

for _, constantName in ipairs(requiredConstants) do
	if commandUtil[constantName] == nil then
		error(
			"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] CommandModules.Util is missing "
				.. constantName
		)
	end
end

function methods:SetupCommandProcessors()
	for _, commandModule in ipairs(commandModules:GetChildren()) do
		if commandModule:IsA("ModuleScript")
			and commandModule.Name ~= "Util" then


			-- Require only package-owned command modules; no Chat service alias is used.
			local success, commandProcessor = pcall(require, commandModule)

			if not success then
				warn(
					"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Failed loading "
						.. commandModule:GetFullName()
						.. ": "
						.. tostring(commandProcessor)
				)

				continue
			end

			if type(commandProcessor) ~= "table" then
				warn(
					"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] "
						.. commandModule:GetFullName()
						.. " did not return a table."
				)

				continue
			end

			local processorType =
				commandProcessor[commandUtil.KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE]

			local processorFunction =
				commandProcessor[commandUtil.KEY_PROCESSOR_FUNCTION]

			if type(processorFunction) ~= "function" then
				warn(
					"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Ignoring "
						.. commandModule:GetFullName()
						.. " because its processor function is missing."
				)

				continue
			end

			if processorType == commandUtil.IN_PROGRESS_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR then
				table.insert(
					self.InProgressMessageProcessors,
					processorFunction
				)
			elseif processorType == commandUtil.COMPLETED_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR then
				table.insert(
					self.CompletedMessageProcessors,
					processorFunction
				)
			end
		end
	end
end

function methods:ProcessCompletedChatMessage(message, ChatWindow)
	for _, processor in ipairs(self.CompletedMessageProcessors) do
		local success, result = pcall(
			processor,
			message,
			ChatWindow,
			ChatSettings
		)

		if not success then
			warn(
				"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Completed-message processor "
					.. "failed: "
					.. tostring(result)
			)
		elseif result then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

function methods:ProcessInProgressChatMessage(
	message,
	ChatWindow,
	ChatBar
)
	for _, processor in ipairs(self.InProgressMessageProcessors) do
		local success, customState = pcall(
			processor,
			message,
			ChatWindow,
			ChatBar,
			ChatSettings
		)

		if not success then
			warn(
				"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] In-progress processor "
					.. "failed: "
					.. tostring(customState)
			)
		elseif customState then
			return customState
		end
	end

	return nil
end

function module.new()
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)

	obj.CompletedMessageProcessors = {}
	obj.InProgressMessageProcessors = {}

	obj:SetupCommandProcessors()

	print(
		"[LegacyChat][CommandProcessor] Initialized: "
			.. tostring(#obj.CompletedMessageProcessors)
			.. " completed processor(s), "
			.. tostring(#obj.InProgressMessageProcessors)
			.. " in-progress processor(s)."
	)

	return obj
end

local module = {}

local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods

function methods:SendMessage(message, toChannel)
	if not self.SayMessageRequest then
		warn("[LegacyChat] SayMessageRequest was not registered.")
		return false
	end

	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		self.SayMessageRequest:FireServer(message, toChannel)
	end)

	if not ok then
		warn("[LegacyChat] Failed sending message: " .. tostring(err))
		return false
	end

	return true
end

function methods:RegisterSayMessageFunction(func)
	self.SayMessageRequest = func
end

function module.new()
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)
	obj.SayMessageRequest = nil
	return obj
end

                                            <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                            <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    FileName: BubbleChat.lua
    Yeku Legacy Bubble Chat renderer.

    Restores the original jeditkacheff / TheGamer101 bubble layout while
    retaining Yeku's legacy-chat and TextChatService message adapters.

    Historical behavior:
      - BubbleChat ScreenGui -&gt; BillboardGui -&gt; BillboardFrame hierarchy.
      - Original dialog_white/blue/red/green nine-slice assets and tails.
      - SourceSans 24px text, 34px line height, original width/height math.
      - Per-character FIFO, elastic entrance, stacked older bubbles, fade order.
      - Near/distant/very-far LOD at 65/100 studs.
      - Distant "..." uses the original scaled dialog asset.

    Fixes compared with the newer Yeku version:
      - Removes stale untagged billboards left by earlier renderer versions.
      - Never rounds a one-line bubble up to two full lines.
      - Restores correct distant-placeholder asset and reliably swaps LOD.
      - Recalculates bubble stacking after creation/removal.
      - Prevents expired entries removed from the queue from leaving cleanup
        tasks waiting forever.
      - Keeps modern native BubbleChat disabled while this renderer is active.
      - Replaces TextChatService:DisplayBubble() NPC/dialog bubbles through
        TextChatService.BubbleDisplayed, not just player chat messages.
]]

local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ChatService = game:GetService("Chat")
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed = true
local bubbleChatAllAllowed = true
local bubbleGuiAllowed = true

local function getNewChatBackend()
	local root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	local modules = root and root:FindFirstChild("Modules")
	local backendModule = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("NewChat")
	if not backendModule or not backendModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return nil
	end
	local ok, backend = pcall(require, backendModule)
	if ok and type(backend) == "table" then
		return backend
	end
	return nil
end

local NewChatBackend = getNewChatBackend()

local LocalPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
local localPlayerDeadline = os.clock() + 20
while not LocalPlayer and os.clock() &lt; localPlayerDeadline do
	task.wait(0.1)
	LocalPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
end
assert(LocalPlayer, "[Yeku Legacy BubbleChat] LocalPlayer was not available within 20 seconds.")

local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 15)
assert(PlayerGui, "[Yeku Legacy BubbleChat] PlayerGui was not available within 15 seconds.")

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHAT SETTINGS / CONSTANTS
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function findModule(folder, name)
	if not folder then return nil end
	local object = folder:FindFirstChild(name)
	if object and object:IsA("ModuleScript") then return object end
	return nil
end

local function findClientChatModules()
	-- This renderer is nested under the bundled Modules.Server.ClientChat tree.
	-- Never select the unrelated runtime game.Chat.ClientChatModules folder.
	local clientChat = script:FindFirstAncestor("ClientChat")
	local defaults = clientChat and clientChat:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
	if defaults and defaults:IsA("Folder") then return defaults end
	return nil
end

local ClientChatModules = findClientChatModules()
local ChatSettings = {}
local ChatConstants = {}

local settingsModule = findModule(ClientChatModules, "ChatSettings")
if settingsModule then
	local ok, result = pcall(require, settingsModule)
	if ok and type(result) == "table" then
		ChatSettings = result
	else
		warn("[Yeku Legacy BubbleChat] ChatSettings failed: " .. tostring(result))
	end
end

local constantsModule = findModule(ClientChatModules, "ChatConstants")
if constantsModule then
	local ok, result = pcall(require, constantsModule)
	if ok and type(result) == "table" then
		ChatConstants = result
	else
		warn("[Yeku Legacy BubbleChat] ChatConstants failed: " .. tostring(result))
	end
end

if not ChatConstants.MessageTypeDefault then ChatConstants.MessageTypeDefault = "Message" end
if not ChatConstants.MessageTypeWhisper then ChatConstants.MessageTypeWhisper = "Whisper" end

local function getSetting(name, fallback)
	local value = ChatSettings[name]
	if value == nil then return fallback end
	return value
end

local function bubbleChatEnabled()
	local chatOK, chatEnabled = pcall(function() return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat) end)
	if chatOK then bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed = chatEnabled ~= false end
	if not bubbleGuiAllowed or not bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed or not bubbleChatAllAllowed then return false end
	if ChatSettings.BubbleChatEnabled ~= nil then
		return ChatSettings.BubbleChatEnabled == true
	end
	local ok, enabled = pcall(function() return PlayersService.BubbleChat end)
	if ok then return enabled == true end
	-- Some newer clients no longer expose Players.BubbleChat. In that case,
	-- ChatSettings (when supplied) is authoritative; otherwise keep rendering.
	return true
end

local function showOwnFilteredMessage()
	return getSetting("ShowUserOwnFilteredMessage", false) == true
end

local allowedMessageTypes = getSetting("BubbleChatMessageTypes", nil)
if type(allowedMessageTypes) ~= "table" then
	allowedMessageTypes = {
		ChatConstants.MessageTypeDefault,
		ChatConstants.MessageTypeWhisper,
	}
end

local function messageTypeAllowed(messageData)
	if not messageData or messageData.MessageType == nil then return true end
	for _, allowedType in ipairs(allowedMessageTypes) do
		if allowedType == messageData.MessageType then return true end
	end
	return false
end

-- HISTORICAL VISUAL CONSTANTS
