local CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT = Enum.Font.SourceSans
local CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT_SIZE_INT = 24
local CHAT_BUBBLE_LINE_HEIGHT = CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT_SIZE_INT + 10
local CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_HEIGHT = 14
local CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING = 30
local CHAT_BUBBLE_FADE_SPEED = 1.5
local BILLBOARD_MAX_WIDTH = 400
local BILLBOARD_MAX_HEIGHT = 250
local ELLIPSES = "..."
local MAX_CHAT_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 128
local MAX_CHAT_MESSAGE_LENGTH_EXCLUSIVE = MAX_CHAT_MESSAGE_LENGTH - #ELLIPSES - 1
local NEAR_BUBBLE_DISTANCE = 65
local MAX_BUBBLE_DISTANCE = 100

-- These are the original fixed visual values. In particular, the line count
-- below is intentionally fractional; rounding it up made short bubbles taller.
local okShouldClipInGameChat, valueShouldClipInGameChat = pcall(function()
	return UserSettings():IsUserFeatureEnabled("UserShouldClipInGameChat")
end)
local shouldClipInGameChat = okShouldClipInGameChat and valueShouldClipInGameChat

local BubbleColor = {
	WHITE = "dub",
	BLUE = "blu",
	GREEN = "gre",
	RED = "red",
}

local BubbleAssets = {
	[BubbleColor.WHITE] = {
		image = "ui/dialog_white",
		notify = "ui/chatBubble_white_notify_bkg",
		slice = Rect.new(5, 5, 15, 15),
		inset = false,
	},
	[BubbleColor.BLUE] = {
		image = "ui/dialog_blue",
		notify = "ui/chatBubble_blue_notify_bkg",
		slice = Rect.new(7, 7, 33, 33),
		inset = true,
	},
	[BubbleColor.RED] = {
		image = "ui/dialog_red",
		notify = "ui/chatBubble_red_notify_bkg",
		slice = Rect.new(7, 7, 33, 33),
		inset = true,
	},
	[BubbleColor.GREEN] = {
		image = "ui/dialog_green",
		notify = "ui/chatBubble_green_notify_bkg",
		slice = Rect.new(7, 7, 33, 33),
		inset = true,
	},
}

local function getYekuChatLocalization()
	local modulesRoot = script:FindFirstAncestor("Modules")
	local server = modulesRoot and modulesRoot:FindFirstChild("Server")
	local clientChat = server and server:FindFirstChild("ClientChat")
	local defaults = clientChat and clientChat:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
	local moduleScript = defaults and defaults:FindFirstChild("ChatLocalization")
	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then return nil end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if ok and type(result) == "table" and type(result.Get) == "function" then return result end
	return nil
end

local function resolveLocalizationMarkup(value)
	local text = tostring(value or "")
	local marker = "{RBX_LOCALIZATION_KEY}"
	local first = string.find(text, marker, 1, true)
	if not first then return text end
	local second = string.find(text, marker, first + #marker, true)
	if not second then return string.gsub(text, marker, "") end
	local keyPart = string.sub(text, first + #marker, second - 1)
	local fallbackPart = string.sub(text, second + #marker)
	local key = string.match(keyPart, "^%s*%((.-)%)%s*$") or string.match(keyPart, "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
	local fallback = string.match(fallbackPart, "^%s*%((.*)%)%s*$") or fallbackPart
	key = string.gsub(tostring(key or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
	fallback = string.gsub(tostring(fallback or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
	if key == "" then return fallback end
	local localization = getYekuChatLocalization()
	if localization then
		local ok, translated = pcall(function() return localization:Get(key, fallback) end)
		if ok and type(translated) == "string" and translated ~= "" then return translated end
	end
	return fallback
end

local function sanitizeChatLine(message)
	message = resolveLocalizationMarkup(message)
	if #message &gt; MAX_CHAT_MESSAGE_LENGTH_EXCLUSIVE then
		return string.sub(message, 1, MAX_CHAT_MESSAGE_LENGTH_EXCLUSIVE + #ELLIPSES)
	end
	return message
end

local function lerpLength(message, minimum, maximum)
	return minimum + (maximum - minimum) * math.min(#message / 75.0, 1.0)
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- SCREEN GUI: THIS RENDERER OWNS THE NAMED BUBBLECHAT ROOT
----------------------------------------------------------------

-- Use a private, tagged root so the loader can suppress Roblox's native
-- BubbleChat ScreenGui without deleting this historical renderer.
local BubbleChatScreenGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("YekuLegacyBubbleChat")
if BubbleChatScreenGui and (not BubbleChatScreenGui:IsA("ScreenGui")
	or BubbleChatScreenGui:GetAttribute("YekuLegacyBubbleRoot") ~= true) then
	pcall(function() BubbleChatScreenGui:Destroy() end)
	BubbleChatScreenGui = nil
end
if not BubbleChatScreenGui then
	BubbleChatScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	BubbleChatScreenGui.Name = "YekuLegacyBubbleChat"
	BubbleChatScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	BubbleChatScreenGui.Parent = PlayerGui
end
BubbleChatScreenGui.Name = "YekuLegacyBubbleChat"
BubbleChatScreenGui:SetAttribute("YekuLegacyBubbleRoot", true)
BubbleChatScreenGui:SetAttribute("YekuLegacyBubbleVersion", 12)
BubbleChatScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
BubbleChatScreenGui.Enabled = true

-- Delete only untagged Roblox/native BubbleChat roots. Never remove the
-- tagged Yeku renderer, even during reloads.
for _, child in ipairs(PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
	if child ~= BubbleChatScreenGui and child.Name == "BubbleChat" and child:IsA("ScreenGui") then
		pcall(function() child.Enabled = false; child:Destroy() end)
	end
end

-- IMPORTANT: old versions created plain, untagged BillboardGuis. Filtering
-- cleanup by a newer attribute left those old billboards on screen, where their
-- distant "..." could remain large even after the live renderer moved nearby.
-- BubbleChatScreenGui is dedicated to this renderer, so remove every old
-- BillboardGui child, tagged or not.
for _, child in ipairs(BubbleChatScreenGui:GetChildren()) do
	if child:IsA("BillboardGui") then child:Destroy() end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHARACTER / ORIGIN HELPERS
----------------------------------------------------------------

local characterChats = {}
local globalConnections = {}
local messageConnections = {}
local destroyed = false

local function getOriginPart(origin)
	if not origin then return nil end
	if origin:IsA("BasePart") then return origin end
	if origin:IsA("Model") then
		-- Keep the historical Head-first placement, but support NPC rigs that
		-- do not set Model.PrimaryPart and use different body-part names.
		local head = origin:FindFirstChild("Head")
		if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then return head end

		local primary = origin.PrimaryPart
		if primary and primary:IsA("BasePart") then return primary end

		for _, partName in ipairs({"HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "Torso", "LowerTorso"}) do
			local part = origin:FindFirstChild(partName)
			if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return part end
		end

		for _, descendant in ipairs(origin:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then return descendant end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function getOriginContainer(origin)
	if not origin then return nil end
	if origin:IsA("Model") then return origin end
	if origin:IsA("BasePart") then
		return origin:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or origin
	end
	return nil
end

local function isLocalOrigin(origin)
	local character = LocalPlayer.Character
	if not character or not origin then return false end
	return origin == character or origin:IsDescendantOf(character)
end

local function getDistanceFromCamera(origin)
	local part = getOriginPart(origin)
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not part or not camera then return math.huge end
	return (part.Position - camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- LEGACY BUBBLE ART
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function createChatBubbleMain(filePrefix, sliceRect)
	local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	image.Name = "ChatBubble"
	image.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
	image.SliceCenter = sliceRect
	image.Image = "rbxasset://textures/" .. tostring(filePrefix) .. ".png"
	image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	image.BorderSizePixel = 0
	image.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	image.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	-- Do not hard-code Visible=false in the reusable art template. The LOD
	-- controller owns visibility, and will reveal every live bubble when near.
	return image
end

local function createChatBubbleTail(position, size)
	local tail = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	tail.Name = "ChatBubbleTail"
	tail.ZIndex = 2
	tail.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/dialog_tail.png"
	tail.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	tail.BorderSizePixel = 0
	tail.Position = position
	tail.Size = size
	return tail
end

local function createChatBubbleWithTail(filePrefix, position, size, sliceRect)
	local main = createChatBubbleMain(filePrefix, sliceRect)
	createChatBubbleTail(position, size).Parent = main
	return main
end

local function createScaledChatBubbleWithTail(filePrefix, frameScaleSize, position, sliceRect)
	local main = createChatBubbleMain(filePrefix, sliceRect)
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Name = "ChatBubbleTailFrame"
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	frame.SizeConstraint = Enum.SizeConstraint.RelativeXX
	frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(frameScaleSize, 0, frameScaleSize, 0)
	frame.Parent = main
	createChatBubbleTail(position, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)).Parent = frame
	return main
end

local function tweenGuiObject(object, duration, style, direction, goals, onComplete)
	if not object or not object.Parent then
		if onComplete then pcall(onComplete) end
		return nil
	end
	local tween
	local ok = pcall(function()
		tween = TweenService:Create(object, TweenInfo.new(duration, style, direction), goals)
		if not tween then error("TweenService returned no tween") end
		if onComplete then
			tween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
				if playbackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
					pcall(onComplete)
				end
			end)
		end
		tween:Play()
	end)
	if ok and tween then return tween end

	-- Some executor/client combinations reject TweenService:Create or Play for
	-- PlayerGui billboards. Never let that error abort bubble construction.
	if tween then pcall(function() tween:Cancel() end) end
	for property, value in pairs(goals) do
		pcall(function() object[property] = value end)
	end
	if onComplete then pcall(onComplete) end
	return nil
end

local function createBubbleText(message)
	local text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	text.Name = "BubbleText"
	text.ZIndex = 5
	text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	text.BorderSizePixel = 0
	text.Position = UDim2.new(0, CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING / 2, 0, 0)
	text.Size = UDim2.new(1, -CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING, 1, 0)
	text.Font = CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT
	text.TextSize = CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT_SIZE_INT
	text.ClipsDescendants = false
	text.TextWrapped = true
	text.Text = message
	text.Visible = false
	return text
end

local function createSmallTalkBubble()
	-- The historical renderer built this from ScalingChatBubbleWithTail, which
	-- uses ui/dialog_white (not chatBubble_white_notify_bkg). Using the notify
	-- texture here changed its proportions and made the distant dots look wrong.
	local small = createScaledChatBubbleWithTail(
		BubbleAssets[BubbleColor.WHITE].image,
		0.5,
		UDim2.new(-0.5, 0, 0, 0),
		BubbleAssets[BubbleColor.WHITE].slice
	)
	small.Name = "SmallTalkBubble"
	small.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	small.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
	small.Visible = false

	local text = createBubbleText("...")
	text.Name = "BubbleText"
	text.ZIndex = 5
	text.TextScaled = true
	text.TextWrapped = false
	text.Visible = true
	text.Parent = small
	return small
end

local function createBillboardInstance(origin)
	local adornee = getOriginPart(origin)
	if not adornee then return nil end

	local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
	billboard.Name = "YekuLegacyBubbleBillboard"
	billboard:SetAttribute("YekuLegacyBubble", true)
	billboard.Adornee = adornee
	billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, BILLBOARD_MAX_WIDTH + CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING + 24, 0, BILLBOARD_MAX_HEIGHT)
	billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.0, 0)
	billboard.Enabled = bubbleGuiAllowed and bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed and bubbleChatAllAllowed
	billboard.Parent = PlayerGui

	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Name = "BillboardFrame"
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, BILLBOARD_MAX_WIDTH, 1, 0)
	frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -BILLBOARD_MAX_WIDTH / 2, -0.5, 0)
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	frame.ClipsDescendants = false
	frame.Parent = billboard

	createSmallTalkBubble().Parent = frame
	return billboard
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- FIFO / STATE CLEANUP
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function cleanCharacterState(origin, state)
	if not state or state.Destroyed then return end
	state.Destroyed = true
	if state.AncestryConnection then
		state.AncestryConnection:Disconnect()
		state.AncestryConnection = nil
	end
	if state.BillboardGui then
		state.BillboardGui:Destroy()
		state.BillboardGui = nil
	end
	if characterChats[origin] == state then characterChats[origin] = nil end
end

local function getCharacterState(origin)
	local existing = characterChats[origin]
	if existing and not existing.Destroyed and existing.BillboardGui and existing.BillboardGui.Parent then
		return existing
	end

	local billboard = createBillboardInstance(origin)
	if not billboard then return nil end

	local state = {
		Origin = origin,
		BillboardGui = billboard,
		Queue = {},
		Destroyed = false,
		RemovalProcessing = false,
		AncestryConnection = nil,
	}
	characterChats[origin] = state

	state.AncestryConnection = origin.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
		if parent == nil then cleanCharacterState(origin, state) end
	end)
	return state
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- DISTANCE LOD
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function setBillboardLODNear(billboard)
	local adornee = billboard.Adornee
	local localCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
	local isLocalPlayer = localCharacter ~= nil and adornee ~= nil
		and (adornee == localCharacter or adornee:IsDescendantOf(localCharacter))

	billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, BILLBOARD_MAX_WIDTH + CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING + 24, 0, BILLBOARD_MAX_HEIGHT)
	billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, isLocalPlayer and 1.75 or 2.5, 0)
	billboard.Enabled = bubbleGuiAllowed and bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed and bubbleChatAllAllowed

	local frame = billboard:FindFirstChild("BillboardFrame")
	if not frame then return end
	for _, child in ipairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name ~= "SmallTalkBubble" then child.Visible = true end
	end
	local small = frame:FindFirstChild("SmallTalkBubble")
	if small then small.Visible = false end
end

local function setBillboardLODDistant(billboard)
	local adornee = billboard.Adornee
	local localCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
	local isLocal = localCharacter ~= nil and adornee ~= nil
		and (adornee == localCharacter or adornee:IsDescendantOf(localCharacter))

	billboard.Size = UDim2.new(4, 0, 2.24, 0)
	billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
	billboard.Enabled = bubbleGuiAllowed and bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed and bubbleChatAllAllowed

	local frame = billboard:FindFirstChild("BillboardFrame")
	if not frame then return end
	for _, child in ipairs(frame:GetChildren()) do child.Visible = false end
	local small = frame:FindFirstChild("SmallTalkBubble")
	if small then small.Visible = true end
end

local function setBillboardLODVeryFar(billboard)
	billboard.Enabled = false
end

local function updateBillboardLOD(billboard, origin)
	if not billboard or not billboard.Parent or not origin then return end
	local part = getOriginPart(origin)
	if not part or not part.Parent then
		billboard.Enabled = false
		return
	end

	-- Always follow the currently valid head/primary part. Measuring the model's
	-- stale PrimaryPart while the billboard is attached to Head caused LOD mode
	-- and visible position to disagree on some avatar rigs.
	if billboard.Adornee ~= part then billboard.Adornee = part end
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not camera then
		billboard.Enabled = false
		return
	end
	local distance = (part.Position - camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude

	if distance &lt; NEAR_BUBBLE_DISTANCE then
		setBillboardLODNear(billboard)
	elseif distance &lt; MAX_BUBBLE_DISTANCE then
		setBillboardLODDistant(billboard)
	else
		setBillboardLODVeryFar(billboard)
	end
end

local function updateAllBillboards()
	if destroyed then return end
	for origin, state in pairs(characterChats) do
		if not origin.Parent or not state.BillboardGui or not state.BillboardGui.Parent then
			cleanCharacterState(origin, state)
		else
			updateBillboardLOD(state.BillboardGui, origin)
		end
	end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- BUBBLE STACKING / REMOVAL
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function repositionOlderBubbles(state)
	if not state or state.Destroyed then return end
	local queue = state.Queue
	local count = #queue
	if count &lt;= 1 then return end

	local newest = queue[count]
	local currentY = -(newest.Height or (newest.RenderBubble and newest.RenderBubble.Size.Y.Offset) or 0)

	-- Start immediately above the newest bubble and stack older ones upwards,
	-- matching the historical FIFO layout. Use target heights, not mid-tween
	-- Size values (which can still be zero while the elastic entrance runs).
	for index = count - 1, 1, -1 do
		local entry = queue[index]
		local bubble = entry and entry.RenderBubble
		if bubble and bubble.Parent then
			local height = entry.Height or bubble.Size.Y.Offset
			local target = UDim2.new(
				bubble.Position.X.Scale,
				bubble.Position.X.Offset,
				1,
				currentY - height - CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_HEIGHT
			)
			tweenGuiObject(
				bubble,
				0.1,
				Enum.EasingStyle.Bounce,
				Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
				{ Position = target }
			)
			local tail = bubble:FindFirstChild("ChatBubbleTail")
			if tail then tail:Destroy() end
			local text = bubble:FindFirstChild("BubbleText")
			if text then text.TextTransparency = 0.5 end
			currentY -= height + CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_HEIGHT
		end
	end
end

local function fadeBubble(entry)
	local bubble = entry and entry.RenderBubble
	if not bubble or not bubble.Parent then return end

	local text = bubble:FindFirstChild("BubbleText")
	local tail = bubble:FindFirstChild("ChatBubbleTail")
	local elapsed = 0
	while bubble.Parent and bubble.ImageTransparency &lt; 1 and elapsed &lt; 2 do
		local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		elapsed += dt
		local fadeAmount = dt * CHAT_BUBBLE_FADE_SPEED
		bubble.ImageTransparency = math.min(1, bubble.ImageTransparency + fadeAmount)
		if text and text.Parent then
			text.TextTransparency = math.min(1, text.TextTransparency + fadeAmount)
		end
		if tail and tail.Parent then
			tail.ImageTransparency = math.min(1, tail.ImageTransparency + fadeAmount)
		end
	end
	if bubble.Parent then bubble:Destroy() end
end

local function processExpiredBubbles(state)
	if not state or state.Destroyed or state.RemovalProcessing then return end
	state.RemovalProcessing = true

	task.spawn(function()
		while not state.Destroyed do
			local first = state.Queue[1]
			if not first or not first.Expired then break end

			fadeBubble(first)
			if state.Destroyed then break end

			-- A queue cap can have removed this entry while the fade yielded.
			-- Only pop it if it is still the front item.
			if state.Queue[1] == first then
				table.remove(state.Queue, 1)
				first.RenderBubble = nil
				repositionOlderBubbles(state)
			end
		end

		state.RemovalProcessing = false
		if not state.Destroyed and #state.Queue == 0 then
			cleanCharacterState(state.Origin, state)
		elseif not state.Destroyed then
			-- If the remaining front became expired during a yield, continue.
			local first = state.Queue[1]
			if first and first.Expired then processExpiredBubbles(state) end
		end
	end)
end

local function requestBubbleRemoval(state, entry)
	if not state or state.Destroyed or not entry then return end
	local stillQueued = false
	for _, queued in ipairs(state.Queue) do
		if queued == entry then stillQueued = true break end
	end
	if not stillQueued then return end
	entry.Expired = true
	processExpiredBubbles(state)
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- BUBBLE RENDERING
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function renderChatLine(origin, message, bubbleColor, isLocalPlayer)
	if destroyed or not bubbleChatEnabled() then return end
	if not origin or not origin.Parent then return end

	message = sanitizeChatLine(message)
	if message == "" then return end

	local state = getCharacterState(origin)
	if not state then return end

	local asset = BubbleAssets[bubbleColor] or BubbleAssets[BubbleColor.WHITE]
	local entry = {
		Message = message,
		BubbleColor = bubbleColor,
		Origin = origin,
		IsLocalPlayer = isLocalPlayer,
		BubbleDieDelay = isLocalPlayer and lerpLength(message, 8, 15) or lerpLength(message, 12, 20),
		RenderBubble = nil,
		Height = 0,
		Expired = false,
	}
	table.insert(state.Queue, entry)

	local billboard = state.BillboardGui
	local frame = billboard and billboard:FindFirstChild("BillboardFrame")
	if not frame then
		table.remove(state.Queue, #state.Queue)
		if #state.Queue == 0 then cleanCharacterState(origin, state) end
		return
	end

	local bubble = createChatBubbleWithTail(
		asset.image,
		UDim2.new(0.5, -CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_HEIGHT, 1, asset.inset and -1 or 0),
		UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_HEIGHT),
		asset.slice
	)
	bubble.Visible = true
	local text = createBubbleText(message)
	text.Parent = bubble
	bubble.Parent = frame
	entry.RenderBubble = bubble

	-- Measure against the actual text area of the main nine-slice. The label is
	-- inset by half the 30px art padding on each side, so its true width is
	-- 400 - 30 = 370px. The former extra 32px safety subtraction wrapped text
	-- early and then added those same pixels back into the art width.
	local BUBBLE_MAIN_ASSET_WIDTH = BILLBOARD_MAX_WIDTH
	local TEXT_INSET_TOTAL = CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING
	local maxTextWidth = math.max(1, BUBBLE_MAIN_ASSET_WIDTH - TEXT_INSET_TOTAL)
	local textBounds = TextService:GetTextSize(
		text.Text,
		CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT_SIZE_INT,
		CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT,
		Vector2.new(maxTextWidth, BILLBOARD_MAX_HEIGHT)
	)
	local widthScale = math.clamp(
		(textBounds.X + TEXT_INSET_TOTAL) / BUBBLE_MAIN_ASSET_WIDTH,
		0.1,
		1
	)
	local numberOfLines = math.max(textBounds.Y / CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT_SIZE_INT, 1)
	local height = math.clamp(numberOfLines * CHAT_BUBBLE_LINE_HEIGHT, CHAT_BUBBLE_LINE_HEIGHT, BILLBOARD_MAX_HEIGHT)
	entry.Height = height

	bubble.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	bubble.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
	tweenGuiObject(
		bubble,
		0.1,
		Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic,
		Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
		{
			Size = UDim2.new(widthScale, 0, 0, height),
			Position = UDim2.new((1 - widthScale) / 2, 0, 1, -height),
		},
		function()
			if bubble.Parent and text.Parent == bubble then text.Visible = true end
		end
	)

	-- Apply distance mode after adding the new bubble so the new item is not
	-- left invisible when the character is close to the camera.
	updateBillboardLOD(billboard, origin)
	-- The original renderer did not delete bubbles at an arbitrary queue size.
	-- Keep the full FIFO and let the historical billboard height/stack layout
	-- determine how much of the older stack remains visible.
	repositionOlderBubbles(state)

	task.delay(entry.BubbleDieDelay, function()
		if not destroyed and not state.Destroyed then requestBubbleRemoval(state, entry) end
	end)
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- MESSAGE ADAPTERS
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function findPlayer(playerName)
	if type(playerName) ~= "string" then return nil end
	for _, player in ipairs(PlayersService:GetPlayers()) do
		if player.Name == playerName then return player end
	end
	return nil
end

local function legacyBubbleColor(messageData)
	local extra = type(messageData.ExtraData) == "table" and messageData.ExtraData or {}
	local color = extra.BubbleColor or extra.ChatColor
	if color == Enum.ChatColor.Blue then return BubbleColor.BLUE end
	if color == Enum.ChatColor.Red then return BubbleColor.RED end
	if color == Enum.ChatColor.Green then return BubbleColor.GREEN end
	return BubbleColor.WHITE
end

local function onUnfilteredLegacyMessage(messageData)
	if destroyed or type(messageData) ~= "table" then return end
	if not bubbleChatEnabled() or not messageTypeAllowed(messageData) then return end
	if type(messageData.Message) ~= "string" then return end

	local player = findPlayer(messageData.FromSpeaker)
	if not player or not player.Character then return end

	-- Historical behavior: for the local player, show the unfiltered local
	-- echo only when ShowUserOwnFilteredMessage is false. For everyone else,
	-- wait for OnMessageDoneFiltering to avoid displaying an unfiltered string.
	if not messageData.IsFiltered or messageData.FromSpeaker == LocalPlayer.Name then
		if messageData.FromSpeaker ~= LocalPlayer.Name or showOwnFilteredMessage() then return end
	end

	renderChatLine(player.Character, messageData.Message, BubbleColor.WHITE, player == LocalPlayer)
end

local function onFilteredLegacyMessage(messageData)
	if destroyed or type(messageData) ~= "table" then return end
	if not bubbleChatEnabled() or not messageTypeAllowed(messageData) then return end
	if type(messageData.Message) ~= "string" then return end

	local player = findPlayer(messageData.FromSpeaker)
	if not player or not player.Character then return end
	if player == LocalPlayer and not showOwnFilteredMessage() then return end

	renderChatLine(player.Character, messageData.Message, legacyBubbleColor(messageData), player == LocalPlayer)
end

local function onGameChatMessage(origin, message, color)
	if destroyed or not bubbleChatEnabled() then return end
	if typeof(origin) ~= "Instance" then return end
	if not (origin:IsA("Model") or origin:IsA("BasePart")) then return end

	local normalizedOrigin = getOriginContainer(origin) or origin
	local bubbleColor = BubbleColor.WHITE
	if color == Enum.ChatColor.Blue then
		bubbleColor = BubbleColor.BLUE
	elseif color == Enum.ChatColor.Red then
		bubbleColor = BubbleColor.RED
	elseif color == Enum.ChatColor.Green then
		bubbleColor = BubbleColor.GREEN
	end

	renderChatLine(normalizedOrigin, message, bubbleColor, isLocalOrigin(normalizedOrigin))
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- SELECT MESSAGE SOURCE
----------------------------------------------------------------

local textChatServiceActive = false
pcall(function()
	textChatServiceActive = TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService
end)

local nativeBubbleConnection
local nativeBubbleConfiguration
local function disableNativeBubbles()
	local config = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("BubbleChatConfiguration")
		or TextChatService:FindFirstChildOfClass("BubbleChatConfiguration")
	if not config then return end

	if nativeBubbleConfiguration ~= config then
		if nativeBubbleConnection then nativeBubbleConnection:Disconnect() end
		nativeBubbleConfiguration = config
		nativeBubbleConnection = config:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
			if nativeBubbleConfiguration == config and config.Enabled then
				pcall(function() config.Enabled = false end)
			end
		end)
	end
	pcall(function() config.Enabled = false end)
end

-- TextChatService:DisplayBubble() is how modern NPC dialogue commonly creates
-- bubbles. BubbleDisplayed fires for that API call and supplies both the NPC's
-- part/character and its TextChatMessage. Route it into the same historical
-- renderer while the native BubbleChatConfiguration is disabled, so the NPC
-- does not get a modern bubble on top of the Yeku bubble.
local function onTextChatBubbleDisplayed(adornee, textChatMessage)
	if destroyed or not bubbleChatEnabled() then return end
	if typeof(adornee) ~= "Instance" then return end
	if not (adornee:IsA("Model") or adornee:IsA("BasePart")) then return end

	local messageText
	local ok = pcall(function()
		messageText = textChatMessage and textChatMessage.Text
	end)
	if not ok or type(messageText) ~= "string" or messageText == "" then return end

	-- DisplayBubble accepts either a character Model or a part such as Head.
	-- Normalize parts to their character/NPC Model so all its lines share one
	-- FIFO queue and stack above the same head rather than making separate stacks.
	local origin = getOriginContainer(adornee) or adornee
	if not (origin:IsA("Model") or origin:IsA("BasePart")) then return end
	renderChatLine(origin, messageText, BubbleColor.WHITE, isLocalOrigin(origin))
end

-- Keep the legacy renderer in charge of all bubble visuals whenever Roblox has
-- a modern BubbleChatConfiguration, even if the experience's main text chat
-- backend is set to Legacy. This also lets DisplayBubble() NPC calls be replaced.
disableNativeBubbles()
table.insert(globalConnections, TextChatService.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
	if child.Name == "BubbleChatConfiguration" then task.defer(disableNativeBubbles) end
end))

local bubbleDisplayedOk, bubbleDisplayedSignal = pcall(function()
	return TextChatService.BubbleDisplayed
end)
if bubbleDisplayedOk and bubbleDisplayedSignal then
	table.insert(messageConnections, bubbleDisplayedSignal:Connect(onTextChatBubbleDisplayed))
else
	warn("[Yeku Legacy BubbleChat] TextChatService.BubbleDisplayed is unavailable; modern DisplayBubble() NPC bubbles cannot be intercepted.")
end

local function onTextChatPlayerMessage(message)
	if destroyed or not message or not bubbleChatEnabled() then return end
	if message.Status and message.Status ~= Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success then return end
	local source = message.TextSource
	if not source then return end
	local player = PlayersService:GetPlayerByUserId(source.UserId)
	if not player or not player.Character then return end
	if player == LocalPlayer and not showOwnFilteredMessage() then return end
	if type(message.Text) ~= "string" or message.Text == "" then return end
	renderChatLine(player.Character, message.Text, BubbleColor.WHITE, player == LocalPlayer)
end

local backendConnected = false
if textChatServiceActive then
	if NewChatBackend and NewChatBackend.IncomingMessage then
		local incomingSignal = NewChatBackend.IncomingMessage
		local connect = incomingSignal.Connect or incomingSignal.connect
		if type(connect) == "function" then
			local ok, connection = pcall(connect, incomingSignal, onTextChatPlayerMessage)
			if ok and connection then
				table.insert(messageConnections, connection)
				backendConnected = true
			end
		end
	end
	if not backendConnected then
		local ok, connection = pcall(function()
			return TextChatService.MessageReceived:Connect(onTextChatPlayerMessage)
		end)
		if ok and connection then
			table.insert(messageConnections, connection)
			backendConnected = true
		end
	end
else
	local connectedFolders = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local function connectLegacyFolder(events)
		if not events or connectedFolders[events] then return end
		connectedFolders[events] = true
		local connectedRemotes = {}
		local function connectRemote(remote)
			if not remote or not remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") or connectedRemotes[remote] then return end
			if remote:GetAttribute("YekuClientPlaceholder") == true then return end
			local callback
			if remote.Name == "OnNewMessage" then
				callback = onUnfilteredLegacyMessage
			elseif remote.Name == "OnMessageDoneFiltering" then
				callback = onFilteredLegacyMessage
			end
			if callback then
				connectedRemotes[remote] = true
				table.insert(messageConnections, remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(callback))
			end
		end
		connectRemote(events:FindFirstChild("OnNewMessage"))
		connectRemote(events:FindFirstChild("OnMessageDoneFiltering"))
		table.insert(messageConnections, events.ChildAdded:Connect(connectRemote))
	end
	local events = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
		or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents", 10)
	if events then connectLegacyFolder(events) end
	table.insert(messageConnections, ReplicatedStorage.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == "DefaultChatSystemChatEvents" then connectLegacyFolder(child) end
	end))
end

-- The legacy renderer always listened for game-originated Chat:Chat() bubbles.
-- Keep that path in either chat backend; it is separate from player chat remotes.
local chattedOk, chattedSignal = pcall(function() return ChatService.Chatted end)
if chattedOk and chattedSignal then
	table.insert(messageConnections, chattedSignal:Connect(onGameChatMessage))
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- LIFECYCLE
----------------------------------------------------------------

table.insert(globalConnections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(updateAllBillboards))
table.insert(globalConnections, PlayersService.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
	local character = player.Character
	local state = character and characterChats[character]
	if state then cleanCharacterState(character, state) end
end))

local function cleanupAllStates()
	for origin, state in pairs(characterChats) do
		cleanCharacterState(origin, state)
	end
end

local function updateBubbleCoreGuiPermission(changedType, changedEnabled)
	local chatOK, chatEnabled = pcall(function() return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat) end)
	if chatOK then bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed = chatEnabled ~= false end
	if changedType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat then bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed = changedEnabled == true end
	if changedType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All then bubbleChatAllAllowed = changedEnabled == true end
	bubbleGuiAllowed = bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed and bubbleChatAllAllowed
	local allowed = bubbleGuiAllowed
	if BubbleChatScreenGui then BubbleChatScreenGui.Enabled = allowed end
	if not allowed then cleanupAllStates() end
end
updateBubbleCoreGuiPermission()
local coreGuiSignalOK, coreGuiSignal = pcall(function() return StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal end)
if coreGuiSignalOK and coreGuiSignal then
	table.insert(globalConnections, coreGuiSignal:Connect(function(coreGuiType, enabled)
		if coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat or coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All then
			updateBubbleCoreGuiPermission(coreGuiType, enabled)
		end
	end))
end
task.spawn(function()
	while not destroyed do
		updateBubbleCoreGuiPermission()
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
end)

script.Destroying:Connect(function()
	destroyed = true
	for _, connection in ipairs(messageConnections) do
		pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
	end
	for _, connection in ipairs(globalConnections) do
		pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
	end
	if nativeBubbleConnection then
		pcall(function() nativeBubbleConnection:Disconnect() end)
		nativeBubbleConnection = nil
	end
	cleanupAllStates()
end)

print("[Yeku Legacy BubbleChat] Started; renderer=historical, backend=" .. (textChatServiceActive and "TextChatService" or "legacy"))

return {
	Enabled = true,
	Renderer = "Legacy",
	ScreenGui = BubbleChatScreenGui,
}

local function ProcessMessage(message, ChatWindow, _ChatSettings)

	if command ~= "/cls" and command ~= "/clear" then
		return false
	end

	local currentChannel = ChatWindow:GetCurrentChannel()

	if currentChannel then

	end

	return true
end

                                                <ProtectedString name="Source">-- FileName: Whisper.lua
-- Patched legacy whisper input state.
-- Supports /w and /whisper, autocomplete, preserving an already-entered message,
-- and sending the finished command through the selected chat backend.

local util = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Util", 10))
local ChatSettings = require(script.Parent.Parent:WaitForChild("ChatSettings", 10))

local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
local localPlayerDeadline = os.clock() + 15
while not LocalPlayer and os.clock() &lt; localPlayerDeadline do
	task.wait(0.1)
	LocalPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
end
if not LocalPlayer then
	error("[Yeku Legacy Chat] LocalPlayer was not available within 15 seconds; refusing an infinite PlayerAdded wait.")
end

local whisperStateMethods = {}
whisperStateMethods.__index = whisperStateMethods

local WhisperCustomState = {}

function whisperStateMethods:TrimWhisperCommand(text)
	text = tostring(text or "")

	local lowered = string.lower(text)

	if lowered:sub(1, 3) == "/w " then
		return text:sub(4)
	end

	if lowered:sub(1, 9) == "/whisper " then
		return text:sub(10)
	end

	return nil
end

function whisperStateMethods:ShouldAutoCompleteNames()
	if ChatSettings.WhisperCommandAutoCompletePlayerNames ~= nil then
		return ChatSettings.WhisperCommandAutoCompletePlayerNames
	end

	return true
end

function whisperStateMethods:GetWhisperChanneNameColor()
	return self.ChatSettings.WhisperChannelNameColor
		or Color3.fromRGB(102, 14, 102)
end

function whisperStateMethods:ParseWhisperInput(text)
	local remaining = self:TrimWhisperCommand(text)

	if remaining == nil then
		return nil
	end

	-- Target is the first non-whitespace token. Everything after it is retained
	-- as the draft message, including the possibility of multiple words.
	local targetName = remaining:match("^(%S+)")

	if not targetName then
		return nil
	end

	local remainder = remaining:sub(#targetName + 1)
	local hasSeparator = remainder:match("^%s") ~= nil
	local body = remainder:gsub("^%s+", "")

	return targetName, body, hasSeparator
end

function whisperStateMethods:GetWhisperingPlayer(enteredText)
	local targetName, body, hasSeparator =
		self:ParseWhisperInput(enteredText)

	if not targetName or targetName == "" then
		return nil
	end

	local loweredTarget = string.lower(targetName)
	local possibleMatches = {}
	local exactMatch = nil

	for _, player in ipairs(PlayersService:GetPlayers()) do
		if player ~= LocalPlayer then
			local loweredPlayerName = string.lower(player.Name)

			if loweredPlayerName == loweredTarget then
				exactMatch = player
			end

			if loweredPlayerName:sub(1, #loweredTarget) == loweredTarget then
				table.insert(possibleMatches, player)
			end
		end
	end

	-- An exact username followed by a space is unambiguous even when
	-- autocomplete is disabled.
	if exactMatch and hasSeparator then
		return exactMatch, body
	end

	if #possibleMatches == 1 then
		local onlyMatch = possibleMatches[1]

		if self:ShouldAutoCompleteNames()
			or string.lower(onlyMatch.Name) == loweredTarget then
			return onlyMatch, body
		end
	end

	return nil
end

function whisperStateMethods:EnterWhisperMode(player, body)
	if self.Destroyed or not player then
		return
	end

	self.PlayerNameEntered = true
	self.PlayerName = player.Name

	self.MessageModeButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1000, 1, 0)
	self.MessageModeButton.Text = string.format("[To %s]", player.Name)
	self.MessageModeButton.TextColor3 = self:GetWhisperChanneNameColor()

	local xSize = self.MessageModeButton.TextBounds.X

	self.MessageModeButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, xSize, 1, 0)
	self.TextBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -xSize, 1, 0)
	self.TextBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, xSize, 0, 0)

	-- Preserve any message already typed after the target username.
	if body and body ~= "" then
		self.TextBox.Text = " " .. body
	else
		self.TextBox.Text = " "
	end
end

function whisperStateMethods:TextUpdated()
	if self.Destroyed then
		return
	end

	local newText = self.TextBox.Text

	if not self.PlayerNameEntered then
		local player, body = self:GetWhisperingPlayer(newText)

		if player then
			self:EnterWhisperMode(player, body)
		end

		return
	end

	-- Backspace to an empty field exits whisper mode without sending.
	if newText == "" then
		self.MessageModeButton.Text = ""
		self.MessageModeButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

		self.TextBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		self.TextBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

		self.PlayerNameEntered = false
		self.PlayerName = nil

		self.ChatBar:ResetCustomState()
		self.ChatBar:CaptureFocus()
	end
end

function whisperStateMethods:GetMessage()
	if not self.PlayerNameEntered or not self.PlayerName then
		return self.TextBox.Text
	end

	local body = tostring(self.TextBox.Text or ""):gsub("^%s+", "")

	return "/w " .. self.PlayerName .. " " .. body
end

function whisperStateMethods:ProcessCompletedMessage(_message)
	-- Let the existing chat backend process the completed /w command.
	return false
end

function whisperStateMethods:Destroy()
	if self.Destroyed then
		return
	end

	self.Destroyed = true

	if self.MessageModeConnection then
		self.MessageModeConnection:Disconnect()
		self.MessageModeConnection = nil
	end
end

function WhisperCustomState.new(ChatWindow, ChatBar, Settings)
	local obj = setmetatable({}, whisperStateMethods)

	obj.Destroyed = false
	obj.ChatWindow = ChatWindow
	obj.ChatBar = ChatBar
	obj.ChatSettings = Settings

	obj.TextBox = ChatBar:GetTextBox()
	obj.MessageModeButton = ChatBar:GetMessageModeTextButton()

	obj.OriginalWhisperText = ""
	obj.PlayerNameEntered = false
	obj.PlayerName = nil

	obj.MessageModeConnection = obj.MessageModeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		local currentText = obj.TextBox.Text:gsub("^%s+", "")

		obj:Destroy()
		obj.ChatBar:ResetCustomState()
		obj.ChatBar:SetTextBoxText(currentText)
		obj.ChatBar:CaptureFocus()
	end)

	-- Defer initial processing until ChatBar has installed this state as its
	-- active custom state. This avoids re-entering the TextBox.Changed handler
	-- before ChatBar finishes assigning obj.CustomState.
	task.defer(function()
		if not obj.Destroyed then
			obj:TextUpdated()
		end
	end)

	return obj
end

local function ProcessMessage(message, ChatWindow, ChatBar, Settings)
	local lowered = string.lower(message)

	if lowered:sub(1, 3) ~= "/w "
		and lowered:sub(1, 9) ~= "/whisper " then
		return nil
	end

	if ChatBar.TargetChannel == "Team" then
		-- Do not replace a team-chat editing state with a whisper state.
		return nil
	end

	return WhisperCustomState.new(ChatWindow, ChatBar, Settings)
end

return {
	[util.KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE] = util.IN_PROGRESS_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR,
	[util.KEY_PROCESSOR_FUNCTION] = ProcessMessage,
}</ProtectedString>

                                                <ProtectedString name="Source">-- FileName: SwitchChannel.lua
-- Switches legacy channels using /c &lt;channel name&gt;.

local util = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Util", 10))

local ChatLocalization
local bundledDefaults = script.Parent and script.Parent.Parent
local localizationModule = bundledDefaults and bundledDefaults:FindFirstChild("ChatLocalization")
if localizationModule and localizationModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
	local localizationOK, localizationResult = pcall(require, localizationModule)
	if localizationOK and type(localizationResult) == "table" then
		ChatLocalization = localizationResult
	end
end

if not ChatLocalization then
	ChatLocalization = {
		Get = function(_, _, defaultText)
			return defaultText
		end,
	}
end

local function ProcessMessage(message, ChatWindow, ChatSettings)
	local command, channelName = string.match(
		message,
		"^%s*(%S+)%s*(.-)%s*$"
	)

	if not command or string.lower(command) ~= "/c" then
		return false
	end

	if not channelName or channelName == "" then
		return true
	end

	local targetChannel = ChatWindow:GetChannel(channelName)

	if targetChannel then
		ChatWindow:SwitchCurrentChannel(channelName)

		if not ChatSettings.ShowChannelsBar then
			local currentChannel = ChatWindow:GetCurrentChannel()

			if currentChannel then
				util:SendSystemMessageToSelf(
					string.gsub(
						ChatLocalization:Get(
							"GameChat_SwitchChannel_NowInChannel",
							string.format(
								"You are now chatting in channel: '%s'",
								channelName
							)
						),
						"{RBX_NAME}",
						channelName
					),
					targetChannel,
					{}
				)
			end
		end
	else
		local currentChannel = ChatWindow:GetCurrentChannel()

		if currentChannel then
			util:SendSystemMessageToSelf(
				string.gsub(
					ChatLocalization:Get(
						"GameChat_SwitchChannel_NotInChannel",
						string.format(
							"You are not in channel: '%s'",
							channelName
						)
					),
					"{RBX_NAME}",
					channelName
				),
				currentChannel,
				{
					ChatColor = ChatSettings.ErrorMessageTextColor,
					TextColor3 = ChatSettings.ErrorMessageTextColor,
				}
			)
		end
	end

	return true
end

return {
	[util.KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE] = util.COMPLETED_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR,
	[util.KEY_PROCESSOR_FUNCTION] = ProcessMessage,
}</ProtectedString>

                                            <Properties>
                                                <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />

                                                <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                                <ProtectedString name="Source">-- FileName: SwallowGuestChat.lua
-- Preserves the legacy guest-chat restriction where applicable.

local util = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Util", 10))
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local ChatLocalization
local bundledDefaults = script.Parent and script.Parent.Parent
local localizationModule = bundledDefaults and bundledDefaults:FindFirstChild("ChatLocalization")
if localizationModule and localizationModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
	local localizationOK, localizationResult = pcall(require, localizationModule)
	if localizationOK and type(localizationResult) == "table" then
		ChatLocalization = localizationResult
	end
end

if not ChatLocalization then
	ChatLocalization = {
		Get = function(_, _, defaultText)
			return defaultText
		end,
	}
end

local function ProcessMessage(_message, ChatWindow, _ChatSettings)
	local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

	if not localPlayer then
		return false
	end

	if localPlayer.UserId &lt; 0 and not RunService:IsStudio() then
		local currentChannel = ChatWindow:GetCurrentChannel()

		if currentChannel then
			util:SendSystemMessageToSelf(
				ChatLocalization:Get(
					"GameChat_SwallowGuestChat_Message",
					"Create a free account to get access to chat permissions!"
				),
				currentChannel,
				{}
			)
		end

		return true
	end

	return false
end

return {
	[util.KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE] = util.COMPLETED_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR,

                                                <ProtectedString name="Source">-- FileName: DeveloperConsole.lua
-- Toggles the developer console through supported StarterGui Core calls.

local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local util = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Util", 10))

local function toggleCoreProperty(propertyName)
	local ok, visible = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui:GetCore(propertyName)
	end)

	if not ok then
		return false
	end

	local setOk, err = pcall(function()
		StarterGui:SetCore(propertyName, not visible)
	end)

	if not setOk then
		warn(
			"[LegacyChat] Could not toggle "
				.. propertyName
				.. ": "
				.. tostring(err)
		)

		return false
	end

	return true
end

local function ProcessMessage(message, _ChatWindow, _ChatSettings)
	local command = string.match(string.lower(message), "^%s*(%S+)")

	if command == "/newconsole" then
		toggleCoreProperty("DevConsoleVisible")
		return true
	elseif command == "/console" then
		if not toggleCoreProperty("DeveloperConsoleVisible") then
			toggleCoreProperty("DevConsoleVisible")
		end

		return true
	end

	return false
end

return {
	[util.KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE] = util.COMPLETED_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR,
	[util.KEY_PROCESSOR_FUNCTION] = ProcessMessage,

-- Patched in-progress team chat input state.

local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
local util = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Util", 10))

local TEAM_COMMANDS = { "/team ", "/t ", "% " }

local function IsTeamCommand(message)
	local lowered = string.lower(message)

	for _, command in ipairs(TEAM_COMMANDS) do
		if lowered:sub(1, #command) == command then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local teamStateMethods = {}
teamStateMethods.__index = teamStateMethods

local TeamCustomState = {}

function teamStateMethods:GetTeamChatColor()
	local localPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer

	if localPlayer and localPlayer.Team then
		return localPlayer.Team.TeamColor.Color
	end

	return self.ChatSettings.DefaultChannelNameColor
		or Color3.fromRGB(35, 76, 142)
end

function teamStateMethods:EnterTeamChat()
	self.TeamChatEntered = true

	self.MessageModeButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1000, 1, 0)
	self.MessageModeButton.Text = "[Team]"
	self.MessageModeButton.TextColor3 = self:GetTeamChatColor()

	local xSize = self.MessageModeButton.TextBounds.X

	self.MessageModeButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, xSize, 1, 0)
	self.TextBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -xSize, 1, 0)
	self.TextBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, xSize, 0, 0)

	if self.TextBox.Text == "" then
		self.TextBox.Text = " "
	end
end

function teamStateMethods:TextUpdated()
	if not self.TeamChatEntered then
		if IsTeamCommand(self.TextBox.Text) then
			self:EnterTeamChat()
		end

		return
	end

	if self.TextBox.Text == "" then
		self:ResetEntry()
	end
end

function teamStateMethods:ResetEntry()
	self.MessageModeButton.Text = ""
	self.MessageModeButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

	self.TextBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	self.TextBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	self.TextBox.Text = ""

	self.TeamChatEntered = false

	self.ChatBar:ResetCustomState()
	self.ChatBar:CaptureFocus()
end

function teamStateMethods:GetMessage()
	if not self.TeamChatEntered then
		return self.TextBox.Text
	end

	local body = self.TextBox.Text:gsub("^%s+", "")
	return "/t " .. body
end

function teamStateMethods:ProcessCompletedMessage()
	return false
end

function teamStateMethods:Destroy()
	if self.MessageModeConnection then
		self.MessageModeConnection:Disconnect()
		self.MessageModeConnection = nil
	end

	self.Destroyed = true
end

function TeamCustomState.new(ChatWindow, ChatBar, ChatSettings)
	local obj = setmetatable({}, teamStateMethods)

	obj.Destroyed = false
	obj.ChatWindow = ChatWindow
	obj.ChatBar = ChatBar
	obj.ChatSettings = ChatSettings
	obj.TextBox = ChatBar:GetTextBox()
	obj.MessageModeButton = ChatBar:GetMessageModeTextButton()
	obj.TeamChatEntered = false

	obj.MessageModeConnection = obj.MessageModeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		local text = obj.TextBox.Text:gsub("^%s+", "")

		obj:Destroy()
		obj.ChatBar:ResetCustomState()
		obj.ChatBar:SetTextBoxText(text)
		obj.ChatBar:CaptureFocus()
	end)

	task.defer(function()
		if not obj.Destroyed then
			obj:TextUpdated()
		end
	end)

	return obj
end

local function ProcessMessage(message, ChatWindow, ChatBar, ChatSettings)
	if ChatBar.TargetChannel == "Team" then
		return nil
	end

	if IsTeamCommand(message) then
		return TeamCustomState.new(ChatWindow, ChatBar, ChatSettings)
	end

	return nil
end

return {
	[util.KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE] = util.IN_PROGRESS_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR,
	[util.KEY_PROCESSOR_FUNCTION] = ProcessMessage,
}</ProtectedString>

-- Prints the chat version to the active legacy chat channel.

local util = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Util", 10))
local ChatConstants = require(script.Parent.Parent:WaitForChild("ChatConstants", 10))

local function ProcessMessage(message, ChatWindow, _ChatSettings)
	local command = string.match(string.lower(message), "^%s*(%S+)")

	if command ~= "/version" then
		return false
	end

	local currentChannel = ChatWindow:GetCurrentChannel()
	if not currentChannel then
		return true
	end

	util:SendSystemMessageToSelf(
		string.format(
			"This game is running chat version [%d.%d.%s].",
			ChatConstants.MajorVersion,
			ChatConstants.MinorVersion,
			ChatConstants.BuildVersion
		),
		currentChannel,
		{}
	)

	return true
end

-- Patched utilities shared between legacy command modules.

local clientChatModules = script.Parent.Parent
local ChatConstants = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatConstants", 10))

local COMMAND_MODULES_VERSION = 1

local KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE = "ProcessorType"
local KEY_PROCESSOR_FUNCTION = "ProcessorFunction"

local IN_PROGRESS_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR = 0
local COMPLETED_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR = 1

local module = {}
local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods

local nextSystemMessageId = 0

function methods:SendSystemMessageToSelf(message, channelObj, extraData)
	if not channelObj then
		return false
	end

	nextSystemMessageId -= 1

	local text = tostring(message or "")

	channelObj:AddMessageToChannel({
		ID = nextSystemMessageId,
		FromSpeaker = nil,
		SpeakerUserId = 0,
		OriginalChannel = channelObj.Name,
		IsFiltered = true,
		MessageLength = #text,
		Message = text,
		MessageType = ChatConstants.MessageTypeSystem,
		Time = os.time(),
		ExtraData = type(extraData) == "table" and extraData or {},
	})

	return true
end

function module.new()
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)

	obj.COMMAND_MODULES_VERSION = COMMAND_MODULES_VERSION

	obj.KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE = KEY_COMMAND_PROCESSOR_TYPE
	obj.KEY_PROCESSOR_FUNCTION = KEY_PROCESSOR_FUNCTION

	obj.IN_PROGRESS_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR = IN_PROGRESS_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR
	obj.COMPLETED_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR = COMPLETED_MESSAGE_PROCESSOR

	return obj
end

-- Patched /me message creator.

local clientChatModules = script.Parent.Parent
local ChatSettings = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatSettings", 10))
local ChatConstants = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatConstants", 10))
local util = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Util", 10))

local function CreateMeCommandMessageLabel(messageData, channelName)
	local extraData = type(messageData.ExtraData) == "table"
		and messageData.ExtraData
		or {}

	local useFont = extraData.Font or Enum.Font.SourceSansItalic
	-- /me messages use the same configured size as every other chat message.
	local useTextSize = ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize
	local useChatColor = extraData.TextColor3
		or extraData.ChatColor
		or extraData.Color
		or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	local useChannelColor = extraData.ChannelColor or useChatColor

	local BaseFrame, BaseMessage =
		util:CreateBaseMessage("", useFont, useTextSize, useChatColor)

	util:ApplyMessageStyle(
		BaseMessage,
		extraData,
		useFont,
		useTextSize,
		useChatColor
	)

	local ChannelButton
	local numNeededSpaces = 0

	if channelName ~= messageData.OriginalChannel then
		local formatChannelName = string.format(
			"{%s}",
			tostring(messageData.OriginalChannel or "")
		)

		ChannelButton = util:AddChannelButtonToBaseMessage(
			BaseMessage,
			useChannelColor,
			formatChannelName,
			messageData.OriginalChannel
		)

		numNeededSpaces =
			util:GetNumberOfSpaces(formatChannelName, useFont, useTextSize) + 1
	end

	local function UpdateTextFunction(messageObject)
		local speaker = tostring(messageObject.FromSpeaker or "Someone")
		local message = tostring(messageObject.Message or "")
		local actionText = message

		if messageObject.IsFiltered then
			-- The original legacy format is "/me action".
			if actionText:sub(1, 4):lower() == "/me " then
				actionText = actionText:sub(5)
			end

			BaseMessage.Text =
				string.rep(" ", numNeededSpaces)
				.. speaker
				.. " "
				.. actionText
		else
			local estimatedLength = #speaker + math.max(0, #message - 4)
			BaseMessage.Text =
				string.rep(" ", numNeededSpaces)
				.. string.rep("_", estimatedLength)
		end
	end

	UpdateTextFunction(messageData)

	local function GetHeightFunction(xSize)
		return util:GetMessageHeight(BaseMessage, BaseFrame, xSize)
	end

	local fadeParameters = {
		[BaseMessage] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		},
	}

	if ChannelButton then
		fadeParameters[ChannelButton] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		}
	end

	local FadeInFunction, FadeOutFunction, UpdateAnimFunction =
		util:CreateFadeFunctions(fadeParameters)

	return {
		[util.KEY_BASE_FRAME] = BaseFrame,
		[util.KEY_BASE_MESSAGE] = BaseMessage,
		[util.KEY_UPDATE_TEXT_FUNC] = UpdateTextFunction,
		[util.KEY_GET_HEIGHT] = GetHeightFunction,
		[util.KEY_FADE_IN] = FadeInFunction,
		[util.KEY_FADE_OUT] = FadeOutFunction,
		[util.KEY_UPDATE_ANIMATION] = UpdateAnimFunction,
	}
end

return {
	[util.KEY_MESSAGE_TYPE] = ChatConstants.MessageTypeMeCommand,
	[util.KEY_CREATOR_FUNCTION] = CreateMeCommandMessageLabel,
}</ProtectedString>

-- Patched system messages with font, font face, size, colour and opt-in RichText.

local clientChatModules = script.Parent.Parent
local ChatSettings = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatSettings", 10))
local ChatConstants = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatConstants", 10))
local util = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Util", 10))

local function CreateSystemMessageLabel(messageData, channelName)
	local extraData = type(messageData.ExtraData) == "table"
		and messageData.ExtraData
		or {}

	local useFont = extraData.Font or ChatSettings.DefaultFont
	local useTextSize = util:ResolveMessageTextSize(extraData, ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize)

	-- Supports the original ChatColor property and standard text-style aliases.
	local useChatColor = extraData.TextColor3
		or extraData.ChatColor
		or extraData.Color
		or ChatSettings.DefaultMessageColor

	local useChannelColor = extraData.ChannelColor or useChatColor

	local BaseFrame, BaseMessage = util:CreateBaseMessage(
		tostring(messageData.Message or ""),
		useFont,
		useTextSize,
		useChatColor
	)

	util:ApplyMessageStyle(
		BaseMessage,
		extraData,
		useFont,
		useTextSize,
		useChatColor
	)

	local ChannelButton

	if channelName ~= messageData.OriginalChannel then
		local formatChannelName = string.format(
			"{%s}",
			tostring(messageData.OriginalChannel or "")
		)

		ChannelButton = util:AddChannelButtonToBaseMessage(
			BaseMessage,
			useChannelColor,
			formatChannelName,
			messageData.OriginalChannel
		)

		local spaces = util:GetNumberOfSpaces(
			formatChannelName,
			useFont,
			useTextSize
		) + 1

		BaseMessage.Text = string.rep(" ", spaces)
			.. tostring(messageData.Message or "")
	end

	local function GetHeightFunction(xSize)
		return util:GetMessageHeight(BaseMessage, BaseFrame, xSize)
	end

	local fadeParameters = {
		[BaseMessage] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		},
	}

	if ChannelButton then
		fadeParameters[ChannelButton] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		}
	end

	local FadeInFunction, FadeOutFunction, UpdateAnimFunction =
		util:CreateFadeFunctions(fadeParameters)

	return {
		[util.KEY_BASE_FRAME] = BaseFrame,
		[util.KEY_BASE_MESSAGE] = BaseMessage,
		[util.KEY_UPDATE_TEXT_FUNC] = function(messageObject)
			BaseMessage.Text = tostring(messageObject.Message or "")
		end,
		[util.KEY_GET_HEIGHT] = GetHeightFunction,
		[util.KEY_FADE_IN] = FadeInFunction,
		[util.KEY_FADE_OUT] = FadeOutFunction,
		[util.KEY_UPDATE_ANIMATION] = UpdateAnimFunction,
	}
end

	[util.KEY_CREATOR_FUNCTION] = CreateSystemMessageLabel,

	local extraData = type(messageData.ExtraData) == "table"
		and messageData.ExtraData
		or {}

	local useFont = extraData.Font or ChatSettings.DefaultFont
	local useTextSize = util:ResolveMessageTextSize(extraData, ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize)
	local useChatColor = extraData.TextColor3
		or extraData.ChatColor
		or extraData.Color
		or ChatSettings.DefaultMessageColor
	local useChannelColor = extraData.ChannelColor or useChatColor

	local BaseFrame, BaseMessage = util:CreateBaseMessage(
		tostring(messageData.Message or ""),
		useFont,
		useTextSize,
		useChatColor
	)

		BaseMessage,
		extraData,
		useFont,
		useTextSize,
		useChatColor
	)

		local formatChannelName = string.format(
			"{%s}",
			tostring(messageData.OriginalChannel or "")
		)

			useChannelColor,
			formatChannelName,
			messageData.OriginalChannel
		)

			formatChannelName,
			useFont,
			useTextSize
		) + 1

		BaseMessage.Text = string.rep(" ", spaces)

	end

			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		},
	}

	if ChannelButton then
		fadeParameters[ChannelButton] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		}
	end

	local FadeInFunction, FadeOutFunction, UpdateAnimFunction =
		util:CreateFadeFunctions(fadeParameters)

	return {
		[util.KEY_BASE_FRAME] = BaseFrame,
		[util.KEY_BASE_MESSAGE] = BaseMessage,
		[util.KEY_UPDATE_TEXT_FUNC] = nil,
		[util.KEY_GET_HEIGHT] = GetHeightFunction,
		[util.KEY_FADE_IN] = FadeInFunction,
		[util.KEY_FADE_OUT] = FadeOutFunction,
		[util.KEY_UPDATE_ANIMATION] = UpdateAnimFunction,
	}

local function CreateMessageLabel(messageData, channelName)
	local fromSpeaker = tostring(messageData.FromSpeaker or "Unknown")
	local extraData = type(messageData.ExtraData) == "table"
		and messageData.ExtraData
		or {}

	local useFont = extraData.Font or ChatSettings.DefaultFont
	local useTextSize = util:ResolveMessageTextSize(extraData, ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize)
	local useNameColor = extraData.NameColor or ChatSettings.DefaultNameColor
	local useChatColor = extraData.TextColor3
		or extraData.ChatColor
		or ChatSettings.DefaultMessageColor
	local useChannelColor = extraData.ChannelColor or useChatColor

	local formatUseName = string.format("[%s]:", fromSpeaker)
	local numNeededSpaces =
		util:GetNumberOfSpaces(formatUseName, useFont, useTextSize) + 1

	local BaseFrame, BaseMessage =
		util:CreateBaseMessage("", useFont, useTextSize, useChatColor)

	util:ApplyMessageStyle(
		BaseMessage,
		extraData,
		useFont,
		useTextSize,
		useChatColor
	)

	local NameButton = util:AddNameButtonToBaseMessage(
		BaseMessage,
		useNameColor,
		formatUseName,
		fromSpeaker
	)

	local ChannelButton

	if channelName ~= messageData.OriginalChannel then
		local whisperString = tostring(messageData.OriginalChannel or "")

		if fromSpeaker ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
			whisperString = string.format("From %s", fromSpeaker)
		end

		local formatChannelName = string.format("{%s}", whisperString)

		ChannelButton = util:AddChannelButtonToBaseMessage(
			BaseMessage,
			useChannelColor,
			formatChannelName,
			messageData.OriginalChannel
		)

		NameButton.Position = UDim2.new(
			0,
			ChannelButton.Size.X.Offset
				+ util:GetStringTextBounds(" ", useFont, useTextSize).X,
			0,
			0
		)

		numNeededSpaces +=
			util:GetNumberOfSpaces(formatChannelName, useFont, useTextSize) + 1
	end

	local function UpdateTextFunction(messageObject)
		if messageObject.IsFiltered then
			BaseMessage.Text =
				string.rep(" ", numNeededSpaces)
				.. tostring(messageObject.Message or "")
		else
			BaseMessage.Text =
				string.rep(" ", numNeededSpaces)
				.. string.rep("_", math.max(0, messageObject.MessageLength or 0))
		end
	end

	UpdateTextFunction(messageData)

	local function GetHeightFunction(xSize)
		return util:GetMessageHeight(BaseMessage, BaseFrame, xSize)
	end

	local fadeParameters = {
		[NameButton] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		},

		[BaseMessage] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		},
	}

	if ChannelButton then
		fadeParameters[ChannelButton] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		}
	end

	local FadeInFunction, FadeOutFunction, UpdateAnimFunction =
		util:CreateFadeFunctions(fadeParameters)

	return {
		[util.KEY_BASE_FRAME] = BaseFrame,
		[util.KEY_BASE_MESSAGE] = BaseMessage,
		[util.KEY_UPDATE_TEXT_FUNC] = UpdateTextFunction,
		[util.KEY_GET_HEIGHT] = GetHeightFunction,
		[util.KEY_FADE_IN] = FadeInFunction,
		[util.KEY_FADE_OUT] = FadeOutFunction,
		[util.KEY_UPDATE_ANIMATION] = UpdateAnimFunction,
	}

-- Patched standard message creator.

local clientChatModules = script.Parent.Parent
local ChatSettings = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatSettings", 10))
local ChatConstants = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatConstants", 10))

local function CreateMessageLabel(messageData, channelName)
	local fromSpeaker = tostring(messageData.FromSpeaker or "Unknown")
	local extraData = type(messageData.ExtraData) == "table"
		and messageData.ExtraData
		or {}

	local useFont = extraData.Font or ChatSettings.DefaultFont
	local useTextSize = util:ResolveMessageTextSize(extraData, ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize)
	local useNameColor = extraData.NameColor or ChatSettings.DefaultNameColor
	local useChatColor = extraData.TextColor3
		or extraData.ChatColor
		or ChatSettings.DefaultMessageColor
	local useChannelColor = extraData.ChannelColor or useChatColor
	local tags = type(extraData.Tags) == "table" and extraData.Tags or {}

	local formatUseName = string.format("[%s]:", fromSpeaker)
	local numNeededSpaces =
		util:GetNumberOfSpaces(formatUseName, useFont, useTextSize) + 1

	local BaseFrame, BaseMessage =
		util:CreateBaseMessage("", useFont, useTextSize, useChatColor)

	util:ApplyMessageStyle(
		BaseMessage,
		extraData,
		useFont,
		useTextSize,
		useChatColor
	)

	local NameButton = util:AddNameButtonToBaseMessage(
		BaseMessage,
		useNameColor,
		formatUseName,
		fromSpeaker
	)

	local ChannelButton
	local guiObjectSpacing = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

	if channelName ~= messageData.OriginalChannel then
		local formatChannelName =
			string.format("{%s}", tostring(messageData.OriginalChannel or ""))

		ChannelButton = util:AddChannelButtonToBaseMessage(
			BaseMessage,
			useChannelColor,
			formatChannelName,
			messageData.OriginalChannel
		)

		guiObjectSpacing = UDim2.new(
			0,
			ChannelButton.Size.X.Offset
				+ util:GetStringTextBounds(" ", useFont, useTextSize).X,
			0,
			0
		)

		numNeededSpaces +=
			util:GetNumberOfSpaces(formatChannelName, useFont, useTextSize) + 1
	end

	local tagLabels = {}

	for _, tag in ipairs(tags) do
		if type(tag) == "table" then
			local tagColor = tag.TagColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)
			local tagText = tostring(tag.TagText or "???")
			local formatTagText = string.format("[%s] ", tagText)

			local label = util:AddTagLabelToBaseMessage(
				BaseMessage,
				tagColor,
				formatTagText
			)

			label.Position = guiObjectSpacing
			numNeededSpaces +=
				util:GetNumberOfSpaces(formatTagText, useFont, useTextSize)

			guiObjectSpacing += UDim2.new(0, label.Size.X.Offset, 0, 0)
			table.insert(tagLabels, label)
		end
	end

	NameButton.Position = guiObjectSpacing

	local function UpdateTextFunction(messageObject)
		local message = tostring(messageObject.Message or "")

		if messageObject.IsFiltered then
			BaseMessage.Text = string.rep(" ", numNeededSpaces) .. message
		else
			BaseMessage.Text = string.rep(
				" ",
				numNeededSpaces
			) .. string.rep("_", math.max(0, messageObject.MessageLength or #message))
		end
	end

	UpdateTextFunction(messageData)

	local function GetHeightFunction(xSize)
		return util:GetMessageHeight(BaseMessage, BaseFrame, xSize)
	end

	local fadeParameters = {
		[NameButton] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		},

		[BaseMessage] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		},
	}

	for _, tagLabel in ipairs(tagLabels) do
		fadeParameters[tagLabel] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		}
	end

	if ChannelButton then
		fadeParameters[ChannelButton] = {
			TextTransparency = { FadedIn = 0, FadedOut = 1 },
			TextStrokeTransparency = { FadedIn = 0.75, FadedOut = 1 },
		}
	end

	local FadeInFunction, FadeOutFunction, UpdateAnimFunction =
		util:CreateFadeFunctions(fadeParameters)

	return {
		[util.KEY_BASE_FRAME] = BaseFrame,
		[util.KEY_BASE_MESSAGE] = BaseMessage,
		[util.KEY_UPDATE_TEXT_FUNC] = UpdateTextFunction,
		[util.KEY_GET_HEIGHT] = GetHeightFunction,
		[util.KEY_FADE_IN] = FadeInFunction,
		[util.KEY_FADE_OUT] = FadeOutFunction,
		[util.KEY_UPDATE_ANIMATION] = UpdateAnimFunction,
	}
end

	[util.KEY_MESSAGE_TYPE] = ChatConstants.MessageTypeDefault,
	[util.KEY_CREATOR_FUNCTION] = CreateMessageLabel,
