                                                <ProtectedString name="Source">-- FileName: Util.lua
-- Patched utilities shared by legacy message creators.
-- Supports opt-in RichText and ExtraData font/size/colour styling.

local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local localPlayerDeadline = os.clock() + 15
while not LocalPlayer and os.clock() &lt; localPlayerDeadline do
	task.wait(0.1)
	LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
end
if not LocalPlayer then
	error("[Yeku Legacy Chat] LocalPlayer was not available within 15 seconds; refusing an infinite PlayerAdded wait.")
end

local clientChatModules = script.Parent.Parent
local ChatSettings = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatSettings", 10))
local ChatConstants = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatConstants", 10))

local okClip, shouldClipInGameChat = pcall(function()
	return UserSettings():IsUserFeatureEnabled("UserShouldClipInGameChat")
end)

shouldClipInGameChat = okClip and shouldClipInGameChat or false

local DEFAULT_MESSAGE_CREATOR = "UnknownMessage"
local MESSAGE_CREATOR_MODULES_VERSION = 1

local KEY_MESSAGE_TYPE = "MessageType"
local KEY_CREATOR_FUNCTION = "MessageCreatorFunc"

local KEY_BASE_FRAME = "BaseFrame"
local KEY_BASE_MESSAGE = "BaseMessage"
local KEY_UPDATE_TEXT_FUNC = "UpdateTextFunction"
local KEY_GET_HEIGHT = "GetHeightFunction"
local KEY_FADE_IN = "FadeInFunction"
local KEY_FADE_OUT = "FadeOutFunction"
local KEY_UPDATE_ANIMATION = "UpdateAnimFunction"

local module = {}
local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods

local function safeColor(value, fallback)
	if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
		return value
	end

	return fallback
end

local function stripRichTextForMeasurement(text)
	text = tostring(text or "")
	text = text:gsub("&lt;[^&lt;&gt;]-&gt;", "")
	text = text:gsub("&amp;lt;", "&lt;")
	text = text:gsub("&amp;gt;", "&gt;")
	text = text:gsub("&amp;quot;", "\"")
	text = text:gsub("&amp;apos;", "'")
	text = text:gsub("&amp;amp;", "&amp;")
	return text
end

function methods:GetStringTextBounds(text, font, textSize, sizeBounds)
	sizeBounds = sizeBounds or Vector2.new(10000, 10000)

	local safeText = stripRichTextForMeasurement(text)
	local safeFont = font or ChatSettings.DefaultFont
	local safeSize = tonumber(textSize) or ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize

	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return TextService:GetTextSize(
			safeText,
			safeSize,
			safeFont,
			sizeBounds
		)
	end)

	if ok then
		return result
	end

	return Vector2.new(0, math.max(1, safeSize))
end

function methods:GetMessageHeight(BaseMessage, BaseFrame, xSize)
	xSize = xSize or BaseFrame.AbsoluteSize.X

	local textBoundsSize = self:GetStringTextBounds(
		BaseMessage.Text,
		BaseMessage.Font,
		BaseMessage.TextSize,
		Vector2.new(math.max(1, xSize), 1000)
	)

	return math.max(BaseMessage.TextSize, textBoundsSize.Y)
end

function methods:GetNumberOfSpaces(str, font, textSize)
	local strSize = self:GetStringTextBounds(str, font, textSize)
	local singleSpaceSize = self:GetStringTextBounds(" ", font, textSize)

	if singleSpaceSize.X &lt;= 0 then
		return 0
	end

	return math.ceil(strSize.X / singleSpaceSize.X)
end

function methods:ResolveMessageTextSize(extraData, defaultTextSize)
	-- All legacy chat message types share the same configured size. In
	-- particular, SystemMessage/SetCoreMessage must not become smaller merely
	-- because an incoming ExtraData table contains a TextSize/FontSize value.
	return ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize
end

function methods:ApplyMessageStyle(textObject, extraData, defaultFont, defaultTextSize, defaultColor)
	extraData = type(extraData) == "table" and extraData or {}

	local font = extraData.Font or defaultFont or ChatSettings.DefaultFont
	-- Keep every chat message type at the same size as player messages.
	local textSize = ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize

	local color = safeColor(
		extraData.TextColor3 or extraData.ChatColor or extraData.Color,
		defaultColor or ChatSettings.DefaultMessageColor
	)

	textObject.Font = font
	textObject.TextSize = textSize
	textObject.TextColor3 = color

	-- ExtraData.RichText must be explicitly true; ordinary chat content is plain.
	textObject.RichText = extraData.RichText == true

	if extraData.TextStrokeColor3
		and typeof(extraData.TextStrokeColor3) == "Color3" then
		textObject.TextStrokeColor3 = extraData.TextStrokeColor3
	end

	if tonumber(extraData.TextStrokeTransparency) then
		textObject.TextStrokeTransparency =
			math.clamp(tonumber(extraData.TextStrokeTransparency), 0, 1)
	end

	if extraData.FontFace then
		pcall(function()
			textObject.FontFace = extraData.FontFace
		end)
	end

	return font, textSize, color
end

function methods:CreateBaseMessage(message, font, textSize, chatColor)
	local BaseFrame = self:GetFromObjectPool("Frame")
	BaseFrame.Name = "LegacyChatMessage"
	BaseFrame.Selectable = false
	BaseFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
	BaseFrame.Visible = true
	BaseFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	BaseFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	BaseFrame.ClipsDescendants = false

	local messageBorder = 8

	local BaseMessage = self:GetFromObjectPool("TextLabel")
	BaseMessage.Name = "MessageText"
	BaseMessage.Selectable = false
	BaseMessage.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(messageBorder + 6), 1, 0)
	BaseMessage.Position = UDim2.new(0, messageBorder, 0, 0)
	BaseMessage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	BaseMessage.BorderSizePixel = 0
	BaseMessage.Font = font or ChatSettings.DefaultFont
	BaseMessage.TextSize = ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize
	BaseMessage.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	BaseMessage.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
	BaseMessage.TextTransparency = 0
	BaseMessage.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.75
	BaseMessage.TextColor3 = chatColor or ChatSettings.DefaultMessageColor
	BaseMessage.TextWrapped = true
	BaseMessage.RichText = false

	if shouldClipInGameChat then
		BaseMessage.ClipsDescendants = true
	end

	BaseMessage.Text = tostring(message or "")
	BaseMessage.Visible = true
	BaseMessage.Parent = BaseFrame

	return BaseFrame, BaseMessage
end

local function disconnectOnReparent(instance, clickedConnection)
	local changedConnection

	changedConnection = instance:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
		if instance.Parent == nil then
			if clickedConnection then
				clickedConnection:Disconnect()
			end

			if changedConnection then
				changedConnection:Disconnect()
			end
		end
	end)
end

function methods:AddNameButtonToBaseMessage(BaseMessage, nameColor, formatName, playerName)
	local speakerNameSize = self:GetStringTextBounds(
		formatName,
		BaseMessage.Font,
		BaseMessage.TextSize
	)

	local NameButton = self:GetFromObjectPool("TextButton")
	NameButton.Name = "SpeakerName"
	NameButton.Selectable = false
	NameButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, speakerNameSize.X, 0, speakerNameSize.Y)
	NameButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	NameButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	NameButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	NameButton.Font = BaseMessage.Font
	NameButton.TextSize = BaseMessage.TextSize
	NameButton.TextXAlignment = BaseMessage.TextXAlignment
	NameButton.TextYAlignment = BaseMessage.TextYAlignment
	NameButton.TextTransparency = BaseMessage.TextTransparency
	NameButton.TextStrokeTransparency = BaseMessage.TextStrokeTransparency
	NameButton.TextColor3 = safeColor(nameColor, ChatSettings.DefaultNameColor)
	NameButton.Text = tostring(formatName or "")
	NameButton.RichText = false
	NameButton.Visible = true
	NameButton.Parent = BaseMessage

	local clickedConnection = NameButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		self:NameButtonClicked(NameButton, playerName)
	end)

	disconnectOnReparent(NameButton, clickedConnection)

	return NameButton
end

function methods:AddChannelButtonToBaseMessage(
	BaseMessage,
	channelColor,
	formatChannelName,
	channelName
)
	local channelNameSize = self:GetStringTextBounds(
		formatChannelName,
		BaseMessage.Font,
		BaseMessage.TextSize
	)

	local ChannelButton = self:GetFromObjectPool("TextButton")
	ChannelButton.Name = "ChannelName"
	ChannelButton.Selectable = false
	ChannelButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, channelNameSize.X, 0, channelNameSize.Y)
	ChannelButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	ChannelButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ChannelButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ChannelButton.Font = BaseMessage.Font
	ChannelButton.TextSize = BaseMessage.TextSize
	ChannelButton.TextXAlignment = BaseMessage.TextXAlignment
	ChannelButton.TextYAlignment = BaseMessage.TextYAlignment
	ChannelButton.TextTransparency = BaseMessage.TextTransparency
	ChannelButton.TextStrokeTransparency = BaseMessage.TextStrokeTransparency
	ChannelButton.TextColor3 = safeColor(channelColor, ChatSettings.DefaultMessageColor)
	ChannelButton.Text = tostring(formatChannelName or "")
	ChannelButton.RichText = false
	ChannelButton.Visible = true
	ChannelButton.Parent = BaseMessage

	local clickedConnection = ChannelButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		self:ChannelButtonClicked(ChannelButton, channelName)
	end)

	disconnectOnReparent(ChannelButton, clickedConnection)

	return ChannelButton
end

function methods:AddTagLabelToBaseMessage(BaseMessage, tagColor, formatTagText)
	local tagNameSize = self:GetStringTextBounds(
		formatTagText,
		BaseMessage.Font,
		BaseMessage.TextSize
	)

	local TagLabel = self:GetFromObjectPool("TextLabel")
	TagLabel.Name = "ChatTag"
	TagLabel.Selectable = false
	TagLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, tagNameSize.X, 0, tagNameSize.Y)
	TagLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	TagLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	TagLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
	TagLabel.Font = BaseMessage.Font
	TagLabel.TextSize = BaseMessage.TextSize
	TagLabel.TextXAlignment = BaseMessage.TextXAlignment
	TagLabel.TextYAlignment = BaseMessage.TextYAlignment
	TagLabel.TextTransparency = BaseMessage.TextTransparency
	TagLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = BaseMessage.TextStrokeTransparency
	TagLabel.TextColor3 = safeColor(tagColor, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255))
	TagLabel.Text = tostring(formatTagText or "")
	TagLabel.RichText = false
	TagLabel.Visible = true
	TagLabel.Parent = BaseMessage

	return TagLabel
end

local function GetWhisperChannelPrefix()
	return ChatConstants.WhisperChannelPrefix or "To "
end

function methods:NameButtonClicked(_, playerName)
	if not self.ChatWindow or not self.ChatBar then
		return
	end

	if not ChatSettings.ClickOnPlayerNameToWhisper then
		return
	end

	playerName = tostring(playerName or "")
	local player = Players:FindFirstChild(playerName)

	if not player or player == LocalPlayer then
		return
	end

	local whisperChannel = GetWhisperChannelPrefix() .. playerName

	if self.ChatWindow:GetChannel(whisperChannel) then
		self.ChatBar:ResetCustomState()

		if self.ChatWindow:GetTargetMessageChannel() ~= whisperChannel then
			self.ChatWindow:SwitchCurrentChannel(whisperChannel)
		end
	end

	-- The Whisper in-progress command processor turns this into a proper
	-- "/w username message" when the user types and submits the message.
	self.ChatBar:SetText("/w " .. playerName)
	self.ChatBar:CaptureFocus()
end

function methods:ChannelButtonClicked(_, channelName)
	if not self.ChatWindow or not self.ChatBar then
		return
	end

	if not ChatSettings.ClickOnChannelNameToSetMainChannel then
		return
	end

	if self.ChatWindow:GetChannel(channelName) then
		self.ChatBar:ResetCustomState()

		if self.ChatWindow:GetTargetMessageChannel() ~= channelName then
			self.ChatWindow:SwitchCurrentChannel(channelName)
		end

		self.ChatBar:ResetText()
		self.ChatBar:CaptureFocus()
	end
end

function methods:RegisterChatWindow(chatWindow)
	self.ChatWindow = chatWindow
	self.ChatBar = chatWindow:GetChatBar()
end

function methods:GetFromObjectPool(className)
	if not self.ObjectPool then
		return Instance.new(className)
	end

	return self.ObjectPool:GetInstance(className)
end

function methods:RegisterObjectPool(objectPool)
	self.ObjectPool = objectPool
end

function methods:CreateFadeFunctions(fadeObjects)
	local animParams = {}

	for object, properties in pairs(fadeObjects) do
		animParams[object] = {}

		for property, values in pairs(properties) do
			animParams[object][property] = {
				Target = values.FadedIn,
				Current = object[property],
				NormalizedExptValue = 1,
			}
		end
	end

	local function setTargets(which, duration, CurveUtil)
		for object, properties in pairs(animParams) do
			if object.Parent then
				for property, values in pairs(properties) do
					local target = fadeObjects[object][property][which]

					values.Target = target
					values.NormalizedExptValue =
						CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function FadeInFunction(duration, CurveUtil)
		setTargets("FadedIn", duration, CurveUtil)
	end

	local function FadeOutFunction(duration, CurveUtil)
		setTargets("FadedOut", duration, CurveUtil)
	end

	local function UpdateAnimFunction(dtScale, CurveUtil)
		for object, properties in pairs(animParams) do
			if object.Parent then
				for property, values in pairs(properties) do
					values.Current = CurveUtil:Expt(
						values.Current,
						values.Target,
						values.NormalizedExptValue,
						dtScale
					)

					object[property] = values.Current
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return FadeInFunction, FadeOutFunction, UpdateAnimFunction
end

function methods:NewBindableEvent(name)
	local bindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	bindable.Name = name
	return bindable
end

function methods:RegisterGuiRoot()
	-- Kept for compatibility with older chat modules.
end

local function sendSelfMessage(message, channelObj, extraData)
	if not channelObj then
		return
	end

	local nextId = (module._systemMessageId or 0) - 1
	module._systemMessageId = nextId

	channelObj:AddMessageToChannel({
		ID = nextId,
		FromSpeaker = nil,
		SpeakerUserId = 0,
		OriginalChannel = channelObj.Name,
		IsFiltered = true,
		MessageLength = #tostring(message or ""),
		Message = tostring(message or ""),
		MessageType = ChatConstants.MessageTypeSystem,
		Time = os.time(),
		ExtraData = extraData or {},
	})
end

function methods:SendSystemMessageToSelf(message, channelObj, extraData)
	sendSelfMessage(message, channelObj, extraData)
end

function module.new()
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)

	obj.ObjectPool = nil
	obj.ChatWindow = nil

	obj.DEFAULT_MESSAGE_CREATOR = DEFAULT_MESSAGE_CREATOR
	obj.MESSAGE_CREATOR_MODULES_VERSION = MESSAGE_CREATOR_MODULES_VERSION

	obj.KEY_MESSAGE_TYPE = KEY_MESSAGE_TYPE
	obj.KEY_CREATOR_FUNCTION = KEY_CREATOR_FUNCTION

	obj.KEY_BASE_FRAME = KEY_BASE_FRAME
	obj.KEY_BASE_MESSAGE = KEY_BASE_MESSAGE
	obj.KEY_UPDATE_TEXT_FUNC = KEY_UPDATE_TEXT_FUNC
	obj.KEY_GET_HEIGHT = KEY_GET_HEIGHT
	obj.KEY_FADE_IN = KEY_FADE_IN
	obj.KEY_FADE_OUT = KEY_FADE_OUT
	obj.KEY_UPDATE_ANIMATION = KEY_UPDATE_ANIMATION

	return obj
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- STRICT LEGACY CHAT SETTINGS LOOKUP
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- This helper is called only after ChatConstants finishes loading. Since
-- ChatSettings requires ChatConstants, do not call it at module top-level.
-- ChatConstants is inside DefaultClientChatModules, so its sibling is the
-- only authoritative settings module for this bundled chat implementation.

function module.GetLegacyChatSettings()
	local parent = script.Parent
	local settingsModule = parent and parent:FindFirstChild("ChatSettings")

	if not settingsModule or not settingsModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		warn(
			"[Yeku ChatConstants] Bundled sibling ChatSettings is missing under "
				.. (parent and parent:GetFullName() or "&lt;nil&gt;")
		)
		return nil
	end

	local success, settings = pcall(require, settingsModule)
	if not success then
		warn("[Yeku ChatConstants] Bundled ChatSettings failed: " .. tostring(settings))
		return nil
	end

	if type(settings) ~= "table" then
		warn(
			"[Yeku ChatConstants] Bundled ChatSettings returned "
				.. typeof(settings)
				.. ", expected table"
		)
		return nil
	end

	return settings
end

setmetatable(proxyTable, {
	__index = function(_, key)
		return values[key]
	end,
	__newindex = function(_, key, newValue)
		local oldValue = values[key]
		values[key] = newValue
		if oldValue ~= newValue then
			ChangedEvent:Fire(key, newValue)
		end
	end,
	__pairs = function()
		return next, values, nil
	end,
})

rawset(proxyTable, "SettingsChanged", ChangedEvent.Event)

-- Validate the exact setting implicated in the startup error before returning.

assert(proxyTable.WindowDraggable ~= nil, "[Yeku ChatSettings] WindowDraggable was not initialized")
assert(proxyTable.WindowResizable ~= nil, "[Yeku ChatSettings] WindowResizable was not initialized")
assert(proxyTable.ShowChannelsBar ~= nil, "[Yeku ChatSettings] ShowChannelsBar was not initialized")

                                            <ProtectedString name="Source">-- FileName: ChatLocalization.lua
-- Uses the experience's ChatLocalization table first, then Roblox CoreScript
-- localization for any keys/text that are also present in the CoreScript table.

local LocalizationService = game:GetService("LocalizationService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ChatService = game:GetService("Chat")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local function getExperienceLocaleId()
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local localeId
    pcall(function()
        if localPlayer then localeId = localPlayer.LocaleId end
    end)
    if type(localeId) == "string" and localeId ~= "" then return localeId end
    return LocalizationService.RobloxLocaleId
end

local ChatLocalization = {
    _translator = nil,
    _hasFetchedLocalization = false,
    _localeConnection = nil,
    _coreTranslatorModule = nil,
}

local function getCoreTranslatorModule()
    if ChatLocalization._coreTranslatorModule then
        return ChatLocalization._coreTranslatorModule
    end
    local root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
    local modules = root and root:FindFirstChild("Modules")
    local module = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("RobloxTranslator")
    if module and module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
        local ok, result = pcall(require, module)
        if ok and type(result) == "table" then
            ChatLocalization._coreTranslatorModule = result
            return result
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function invalidateExperienceTranslator()
    ChatLocalization._translator = nil
    ChatLocalization._hasFetchedLocalization = false
end

local function watchExperienceLocale()
    if ChatLocalization._localeConnection then
        ChatLocalization._localeConnection:Disconnect()
        ChatLocalization._localeConnection = nil
    end
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    if localPlayer then
        ChatLocalization._localeConnection = localPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocaleId"):Connect(invalidateExperienceTranslator)
    else
        ChatLocalization._localeConnection = LocalizationService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("RobloxLocaleId"):Connect(invalidateExperienceTranslator)
    end
    invalidateExperienceTranslator()
end

watchExperienceLocale()
pcall(function()
    Players:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocalPlayer"):Connect(watchExperienceLocale)
end)

function ChatLocalization:_getTranslator()
    if self._hasFetchedLocalization then
        return self._translator
    end
    self._hasFetchedLocalization = true

    -- The live Chat service table is optional. Never wait for it during
    -- CoreScript startup; use the bundled fallback when it is not already present.
    local localizationTable = ChatService:FindFirstChild("ChatLocalization")

    if localizationTable and localizationTable:IsA("LocalizationTable") then
        local ok, translator = pcall(function()
            return localizationTable:GetTranslator(getExperienceLocaleId())
        end)
        if ok then self._translator = translator end
    end

    if not self._localeConnection then watchExperienceLocale() end
    return self._translator
end

function ChatLocalization:Get(key, defaultText)
    local fallback = tostring(defaultText or key or "")
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        local translator = self:_getTranslator()
        if translator then
            local translated = translator:FormatByKey(key)
            if type(translated) == "string" and translated ~= "" and translated ~= tostring(key) then
                return translated
            end
        end

        local core = getCoreTranslatorModule()
        if core then
            if type(core.FormatByKey) == "function" then
                local translated = core:FormatByKey(key, nil, fallback)
                if type(translated) == "string" and translated ~= "" then
                    return translated
                end
            end
            if type(core.TranslateText) == "function" then
                return core:TranslateText(ChatService, fallback, fallback)
            end
        end
        return fallback
    end)
    if ok and type(result) == "string" then return result end
    return fallback
end

return ChatLocalization

                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    FileName: ChatWindow.lua
    Written by: Xsitsu
    Yeku compatibility patch

    Main GUI window piece.
    Manages ChatBar, ChannelsBar, and ChatChannels.

    Compatibility fixes:
        - Supports Yeku's bundled DefaultClientChatModules.ChatSettings.
        - Supports ChatWindow as a sibling of ChatMain in ClientChat.
        - Resolves historical sibling modules without downloading scripts.
        - Resolves the real Chat.ClientChatModules folder (no invalid script.p lookup).
        - Prevents the resize lock getting stuck.
        - Handles missing ScreenGui ancestors safely.
        - Preserves historical channel, visibility and animation APIs.
        - Uses a safe input signal for historical TextLabel channel tabs.
        - Gives the root Frame a stable ChatWindow name for Topbar lookup.
]]

local module = {}

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local ChatService = game:GetService("Chat")
local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local localPlayerDeadline = os.clock() + 20
while not LocalPlayer and os.clock() &lt; localPlayerDeadline do
	task.wait(0.1)
	LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
end
assert(LocalPlayer, "[Yeku ChatWindow] LocalPlayer was not available within 20 seconds; aborting instead of waiting forever.")

local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 15)
assert(PlayerGui, "[ChatWindow] PlayerGui was not available within 15 seconds.")

local PHONE_SCREEN_WIDTH = 640
local TABLET_SCREEN_WIDTH = 1024

local DEVICE_PHONE = 1
local DEVICE_TABLET = 2
local DEVICE_DESKTOP = 3

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- DEPENDENCY RESOLUTION
----------------------------------------------------------------

local NewRobloxGui = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
local NewModules = NewRobloxGui and NewRobloxGui:FindFirstChild("Modules")
local ClientChatFolder = script.Parent
local DefaultClientChatModules =
	ClientChatFolder and ClientChatFolder:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")

assert(
	ClientChatFolder and ClientChatFolder:IsA("Folder"),
	"[ChatWindow] Expected script.Parent to be the bundled ClientChat folder."
)
assert(
	DefaultClientChatModules and DefaultClientChatModules:IsA("Folder"),
	"[ChatWindow] Missing script.Parent.DefaultClientChatModules under "
		.. ClientChatFolder:GetFullName()
)

local function addUniqueRoot(list, root)
	if not root then return end
	for _, existing in ipairs(list) do
		if existing == root then return end
	end
	table.insert(list, root)
end

local function getDependencyRoots()
	local roots = {}
	-- Resolve non-settings support modules from this legacy package first.
	addUniqueRoot(roots, ClientChatFolder)
	addUniqueRoot(roots, ClientChatFolder.Parent)
	addUniqueRoot(roots, ClientChatFolder.Parent and ClientChatFolder.Parent.Parent)
	addUniqueRoot(roots, NewModules)
	return roots
end

local function findModuleScript(name, preferChatSettings)
	-- ChatSettings has one legal location for this package. Never search
	-- game.Chat.ClientChatModules or recursively select another implementation.
	if preferChatSettings then
		local found = DefaultClientChatModules:FindFirstChild(name)
		if found and found:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			return found
		end
		return nil
	end

	local roots = getDependencyRoots()
	for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
		local found = root:FindFirstChild(name)
		if found and found:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			return found
		end
	end
	for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
		local found = root:FindFirstChild(name, true)
		if found and found:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			return found
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function requireDependency(name, preferChatSettings)
	local dependency = findModuleScript(name, preferChatSettings)
	if not dependency then
		error(
			"[ChatWindow] Missing required ModuleScript '" .. tostring(name) .. "'. "
				.. (preferChatSettings
					and ("Expected it under " .. DefaultClientChatModules:GetFullName())
					or "Checked the bundled ClientChat package hierarchy"),
			2
		)
	end

	local success, result = pcall(require, dependency)
	if not success then
		error(
			"[ChatWindow] Failed to require " .. dependency:GetFullName() .. ": " .. tostring(result),
			2
		)
	end

	if preferChatSettings and type(result) ~= "table" then
		error(
			"[ChatWindow] " .. dependency:GetFullName()
				.. " returned " .. typeof(result) .. ", expected a settings table",
			2
		)
	end
	return result
end

local moduleChatChannel = requireDependency("ChatChannel", false)
local ChatSettings = requireDependency("ChatSettings", true)
local CurveUtil = requireDependency("CurveUtil", false)

-- The bundled settings contract includes these values. Supply safe defaults if
-- an older settings revision omitted optional keys, rather than indexing nil
-- during CreateGuiObjects. The actual table itself is still required above.
local requiredSettingDefaults = {
	WindowDraggable = false,
	WindowResizable = false,
	ShowChannelsBar = false,
	BackGroundColor = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
	ChatWindowTextSize = 18,
	ChatChannelsTabTextSize = 18,
	ChatBarTextSize = 19,
	DefaultWindowPosition = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
	DefaultWindowSizeDesktop = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0.25, 24),
	MinimumWindowSize = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0.25, 0),
	MaximumWindowSize = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
}
for settingName, defaultValue in pairs(requiredSettingDefaults) do
	if ChatSettings[settingName] == nil then
		warn("[ChatWindow] Bundled ChatSettings is missing " .. settingName .. "; using a safe default.")
		ChatSettings[settingName] = defaultValue
	end
end
assert(ChatSettings.WindowDraggable ~= nil, "[ChatWindow] WindowDraggable must be defined")

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHAT MODE HELPERS
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function chatBarOnlyMode()
	local classicOK, classicEnabled = pcall(function()
		return Players.ClassicChat
	end)

	if classicOK
		and type(classicEnabled) == "boolean"
		and classicEnabled == false then
		return true
	end

	local windowConfig = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("ChatWindowConfiguration")
	local inputConfig = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("ChatInputBarConfiguration")

	if windowConfig and inputConfig then
		local windowOK, windowEnabled = pcall(function()
			return windowConfig.Enabled
		end)
		local inputOK, inputEnabled = pcall(function()
			return inputConfig.Enabled
		end)

		if windowOK and inputOK
			and type(windowEnabled) == "boolean"
			and type(inputEnabled) == "boolean"
			and windowEnabled == false
			and inputEnabled == true then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function connectSignal(signal, callback)
	if not signal then
		return false
	end

	local success = pcall(function()
		signal:Connect(callback)
	end)

	if success then
		return true
	end

	local legacySuccess = pcall(function()
		signal:connect(callback)
	end)

	return legacySuccess
end

local function getScreenGuiParent(baseFrame)
	local current = baseFrame

	while current do
		if current:IsA("ScreenGui") then
			return current
		end

		current = current.Parent
	end

	return nil
end

local function getViewportSize(screenGuiParent)
	if screenGuiParent
		and screenGuiParent.AbsoluteSize.X &gt; 0
		and screenGuiParent.AbsoluteSize.Y &gt; 0 then
		return screenGuiParent.AbsoluteSize
	end

	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		local size = camera.ViewportSize
		if size.X &gt; 0 and size.Y &gt; 0 then
			return size
		end
	end

	return Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
end

local function getDeviceType(screenGuiParent)
	local viewport = getViewportSize(screenGuiParent)

	if viewport.X &lt;= PHONE_SCREEN_WIDTH then
		return DEVICE_PHONE
	elseif viewport.X &lt;= TABLET_SCREEN_WIDTH then
		return DEVICE_TABLET
	end

	return DEVICE_DESKTOP
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- GUI CONSTRUCTION
----------------------------------------------------------------

local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods

local function isLegacyChatWindowRoot(child)
	if not child or not child:IsA("GuiObject") then
		return false
	end

	return child:FindFirstChild("ChatBarParentFrame", true) ~= nil
		and child:FindFirstChild("ChannelsBarParentFrame", true) ~= nil
		and child:FindFirstChild("ChatChannelParentFrame", true) ~= nil
end

function methods:CreateGuiObjects(targetParent)
	assert(
		targetParent and targetParent.Parent ~= nil,
		"[ChatWindow] CreateGuiObjects needs a parent that is already in the GUI tree."
	)

	self.GuiObjects = self.GuiObjects or {}

	-- Make CreateGuiObjects safe if another initialization path calls it twice.
	-- ChatMain normally performs the same cleanup before calling this method.
	for _, child in ipairs(targetParent:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("GuiObject")
			and (child.Name == "ChatWindow" or isLegacyChatWindowRoot(child)) then
			child:Destroy()
		end
	end

	local BaseFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	BaseFrame.Name = "ChatWindow"
	BaseFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	BaseFrame.Active = ChatSettings.WindowDraggable == true
	BaseFrame.AutoLocalize = false
	BaseFrame.Visible = true
	BaseFrame.ZIndex = 1
	BaseFrame.Parent = targetParent

	local ChatBarParentFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	ChatBarParentFrame.Name = "ChatBarParentFrame"
	ChatBarParentFrame.Selectable = false
	ChatBarParentFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ChatBarParentFrame.Parent = BaseFrame

	local ChannelsBarParentFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	ChannelsBarParentFrame.Name = "ChannelsBarParentFrame"
	ChannelsBarParentFrame.Selectable = false
	ChannelsBarParentFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ChannelsBarParentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	ChannelsBarParentFrame.Parent = BaseFrame

	local ChatChannelParentFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	ChatChannelParentFrame.Name = "ChatChannelParentFrame"
	ChatChannelParentFrame.Selectable = false
	ChatChannelParentFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
	ChatChannelParentFrame.BackgroundColor3 = ChatSettings.BackGroundColor
	ChatChannelParentFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ChatChannelParentFrame.Parent = BaseFrame

	local ChatResizerFrame = Instance.new("ImageButton")
	ChatResizerFrame.Name = "ChatResizerFrame"
	ChatResizerFrame.Selectable = false
	ChatResizerFrame.Image = ""
	ChatResizerFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
	ChatResizerFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ChatResizerFrame.Visible = false
	ChatResizerFrame.BackgroundColor3 = ChatSettings.BackGroundColor
	ChatResizerFrame.Active = true
	ChatResizerFrame.Parent = BaseFrame

	local ResizeIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	ResizeIcon.Name = "ResizeIcon"
	ResizeIcon.Selectable = false
	ResizeIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0.8, 0)
	ResizeIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.2, 0, 0.2, 0)
	ResizeIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ResizeIcon.Image = "rbxassetid://261880743"
	ResizeIcon.Parent = ChatResizerFrame

	local deviceType = getDeviceType(getScreenGuiParent(BaseFrame))

	----------------------------------------------------------------
	-- SIZE ENFORCEMENT
	----------------------------------------------------------------

	local checkSizeLock = false

	local function doCheckSizeBounds()
		if checkSizeLock then
			return
		end

		checkSizeLock = true

		local success, err = pcall(function()
			if not BaseFrame:IsDescendantOf(PlayerGui) then
				return
			end

			local screenGuiParent = getScreenGuiParent(BaseFrame)
			local viewport = getViewportSize(screenGuiParent)

			local minWinSize = ChatSettings.MinimumWindowSize or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
			local maxWinSize = ChatSettings.MaximumWindowSize or UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)

			local forceMinY = ChatBarParentFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y
			if ChannelsBarParentFrame.Visible then
				forceMinY += ChannelsBarParentFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y
			end

			local minSizePixelX = minWinSize.X.Scale * viewport.X + minWinSize.X.Offset
			local minSizePixelY

			if chatBarOnlyMode() then
				minSizePixelY = forceMinY
			else
				minSizePixelY = math.max(
					minWinSize.Y.Scale * viewport.Y + minWinSize.Y.Offset,
					forceMinY
				)
			end

			local maxSizePixelX = maxWinSize.X.Scale * viewport.X + maxWinSize.X.Offset
			local maxSizePixelY = maxWinSize.Y.Scale * viewport.Y + maxWinSize.Y.Offset

			maxSizePixelX = math.max(maxSizePixelX, minSizePixelX)
			maxSizePixelY = math.max(maxSizePixelY, minSizePixelY)

			local absSizeX = BaseFrame.AbsoluteSize.X
			local absSizeY = BaseFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y

			local wantedX = math.clamp(absSizeX, minSizePixelX, maxSizePixelX)
			local wantedY = math.clamp(absSizeY, minSizePixelY, maxSizePixelY)

			if viewport.X &gt; 0 and viewport.Y &gt; 0 then
				BaseFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
					wantedX / viewport.X, 0,
					wantedY / viewport.Y, 0
				)
			end
		end)

		checkSizeLock = false

		if not success then
			warn("[ChatWindow] Size-bound update failed: " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end

	BaseFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(doCheckSizeBounds)

	local screenGuiParent = getScreenGuiParent(BaseFrame)
	if screenGuiParent then
		screenGuiParent:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(doCheckSizeBounds)
	end

	----------------------------------------------------------------
	-- DRAG / RESIZE BEHAVIOR
	----------------------------------------------------------------

	local function updatePositionFromDrag(atPos)
		if ChatSettings.WindowDraggable == false
			and ChatSettings.WindowResizable == false then
			return
		end

		local newSize =
			atPos - BaseFrame.AbsolutePosition + ChatResizerFrame.AbsoluteSize

		BaseFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, newSize.X, 0, newSize.Y)

		if chatBarOnlyMode() then
			ChatResizerFrame.Position =
				UDim2.new(1, -ChatResizerFrame.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 0)
		else
			ChatResizerFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
				1, -ChatResizerFrame.AbsoluteSize.X,
				1, -ChatResizerFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y
			)
		end
	end

	connectSignal(ChatResizerFrame.DragBegin, function()
		BaseFrame.Draggable = false
	end)

	connectSignal(ChatResizerFrame.DragStopped, function()
		BaseFrame.Draggable = ChatSettings.WindowDraggable == true
	end)

	local resizeLock = false

	ChatResizerFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsolutePosition"):Connect(function()
		if BaseFrame.Draggable or resizeLock then
			return
		end

		resizeLock = true
		local success, err = pcall(function()
			updatePositionFromDrag(ChatResizerFrame.AbsolutePosition)
		end)
		resizeLock = false

		if not success then
			warn("[ChatWindow] Resize update failed: " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end)

	----------------------------------------------------------------
	-- LAYOUT CALCULATIONS
	----------------------------------------------------------------

	local function calculateChannelsBarPixelSize(textSize)
		if deviceType == DEVICE_PHONE then
			textSize = textSize or ChatSettings.ChatChannelsTabTextSizePhone
		else
			textSize = textSize or ChatSettings.ChatChannelsTabTextSize
		end

		textSize = textSize or 14
		return math.max(32, textSize + 8) + 2
	end

	local function calculateChatBarPixelSize(textSize)
		if deviceType == DEVICE_PHONE then
			textSize = textSize or ChatSettings.ChatBarTextSizePhone
		else
			textSize = textSize or ChatSettings.ChatBarTextSize
		end

		textSize = textSize or 14
		return textSize + (7 * 2) + (5 * 2)
	end

	local function updateChatChannelParentFrameSize()
		local channelsBarSize = calculateChannelsBarPixelSize()
		local chatBarSize = calculateChatBarPixelSize()

		if chatBarOnlyMode() then
			ChatChannelParentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
			ChatChannelParentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		elseif ChatSettings.ShowChannelsBar then
			ChatChannelParentFrame.Size =
				UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -(channelsBarSize + chatBarSize + 4))
			ChatChannelParentFrame.Position =
				UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, channelsBarSize + 2)
		else
			ChatChannelParentFrame.Size =
				UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -(chatBarSize + 4))
			ChatChannelParentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2)
		end
	end

	local function updateChatChannelsTabTextSize(size)
		local channelsBarSize = calculateChannelsBarPixelSize(size)
		ChannelsBarParentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, channelsBarSize)
		updateChatChannelParentFrameSize()
	end

	local function updateResizable(enabled)
		enabled = enabled == true and not chatBarOnlyMode()

		ChatResizerFrame.Visible = enabled
		ChatResizerFrame.Active = enabled
		ChatResizerFrame.Draggable = enabled

		local frameSizeY = ChatBarParentFrame.Size.Y.Offset
		if enabled then
			ChatBarParentFrame.Size =
				UDim2.new(1, -frameSizeY - 2, 0, frameSizeY)
		else
			ChatBarParentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, frameSizeY)
		end

		if not chatBarOnlyMode() then
			ChatBarParentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -frameSizeY)
		else
			ChatBarParentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		end
	end

	local function updateChatBarTextSize(size)
		local chatBarSize = calculateChatBarPixelSize(size)
		ChatBarParentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, chatBarSize)

		if chatBarOnlyMode() then
			ChatBarParentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		else
			ChatBarParentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -chatBarSize)
		end

		ChatResizerFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, chatBarSize, 0, chatBarSize)

		if chatBarOnlyMode() then
			ChatResizerFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -chatBarSize, 0, 0)
		else
			ChatResizerFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -chatBarSize, 1, -chatBarSize)
		end

		updateChatChannelParentFrameSize()
		updateResizable(ChatSettings.WindowResizable)
	end

	local function updateDraggable(enabled)
		enabled = enabled == true
		BaseFrame.Active = enabled
		BaseFrame.Draggable = enabled
	end

	local function updateShowChannelsBar(enabled)
		enabled = enabled == true and not chatBarOnlyMode()
		ChannelsBarParentFrame.Visible = enabled
		ChannelsBarParentFrame.Active = enabled
		updateChatChannelParentFrameSize()
	end

	----------------------------------------------------------------
	-- INITIAL AND LIVE CHAT-MODE LAYOUT
	----------------------------------------------------------------

	if deviceType == DEVICE_PHONE then
		ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize =
			ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSizePhone or ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize
		ChatSettings.ChatChannelsTabTextSize =
			ChatSettings.ChatChannelsTabTextSizePhone or ChatSettings.ChatChannelsTabTextSize
		ChatSettings.ChatBarTextSize =
			ChatSettings.ChatBarTextSizePhone or ChatSettings.ChatBarTextSize
	end

	local defaultClassicWindowSize
	if deviceType == DEVICE_PHONE then
		defaultClassicWindowSize = ChatSettings.DefaultWindowSizePhone
	elseif deviceType == DEVICE_TABLET then
		defaultClassicWindowSize = ChatSettings.DefaultWindowSizeTablet
	else
		defaultClassicWindowSize = ChatSettings.DefaultWindowSizeDesktop
	end
	defaultClassicWindowSize = defaultClassicWindowSize or UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0.3, 0)

	local defaultBarOnlySize
	if deviceType == DEVICE_PHONE then
		defaultBarOnlySize = ChatSettings.DefaultWindowSizePhone
	else
		defaultBarOnlySize = ChatSettings.DefaultWindowSizeTablet
	end
	defaultBarOnlySize = defaultBarOnlySize or UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0, 50)

	local refreshingChatMode = false

	local function applyChatModeLayout()
		if refreshingChatMode or not BaseFrame.Parent then
			return
		end
		refreshingChatMode = true

		local barOnly = chatBarOnlyMode()

		ChatChannelParentFrame.Visible = not barOnly
		ChatChannelParentFrame.Active = not barOnly
		ChannelsBarParentFrame.Visible =
			not barOnly and ChatSettings.ShowChannelsBar == true
		ChannelsBarParentFrame.Active =
			not barOnly and ChatSettings.ShowChannelsBar == true

		if barOnly then
			local barHeight = calculateChatBarPixelSize()
			BaseFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
				defaultBarOnlySize.X.Scale,
				defaultBarOnlySize.X.Offset,
				0,
				barHeight
			)
		else
			BaseFrame.Size = defaultClassicWindowSize
		end

		BaseFrame.Position = ChatSettings.DefaultWindowPosition
			or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

		updateChatChannelsTabTextSize(ChatSettings.ChatChannelsTabTextSize)
		updateChatBarTextSize(ChatSettings.ChatBarTextSize)
		updateDraggable(ChatSettings.WindowDraggable)
		updateResizable(ChatSettings.WindowResizable)
		updateShowChannelsBar(ChatSettings.ShowChannelsBar)
		task.defer(doCheckSizeBounds)

		refreshingChatMode = false
	end

	applyChatModeLayout()

	----------------------------------------------------------------
	-- LIVE SETTINGS CHANGES
	----------------------------------------------------------------

	local settingsChanged = ChatSettings.SettingsChanged
	if settingsChanged then
		connectSignal(settingsChanged, function(setting, value)
			if setting == "WindowDraggable" then
				updateDraggable(value)
			elseif setting == "WindowResizable" then
				updateResizable(value)
			elseif setting == "ChatChannelsTabTextSize" then
				updateChatChannelsTabTextSize(value)
			elseif setting == "ChatBarTextSize" then
				updateChatBarTextSize(value)
			elseif setting == "ShowChannelsBar" then
				updateShowChannelsBar(value)
			elseif setting == "BubbleChatEnabled" then
				applyChatModeLayout()
			end
		end)
	end

	pcall(function()
		Players:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ClassicChat"):Connect(applyChatModeLayout)
	end)

	local function watchModernConfig(configName)
		local observedConfig

		local function bindConfig(instance)
			if not instance
				or instance.Name ~= configName
				or observedConfig == instance then
				return
			end

			observedConfig = instance
			pcall(function()
				instance:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(applyChatModeLayout)
			end)
			applyChatModeLayout()
		end

		bindConfig(TextChatService:FindFirstChild(configName))

		TextChatService.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child.Name == configName then
				bindConfig(child)
			end
		end)

		TextChatService.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
			if child == observedConfig then
				observedConfig = nil
				applyChatModeLayout()
			end
		end)
	end

	watchModernConfig("ChatWindowConfiguration")
	watchModernConfig("ChatInputBarConfiguration")

	pcall(function()
		TextChatService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ChatVersion"):Connect(applyChatModeLayout)
	end)

	self.GuiObject = BaseFrame
	self.GuiObjects.BaseFrame = BaseFrame
	self.GuiObjects.ChatBarParentFrame = ChatBarParentFrame
	self.GuiObjects.ChannelsBarParentFrame = ChannelsBarParentFrame
	self.GuiObjects.ChatChannelParentFrame = ChatChannelParentFrame
	self.GuiObjects.ChatResizerFrame = ChatResizerFrame
	self.GuiObjects.ResizeIcon = ResizeIcon

	self:AnimGuiObjects()
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHILD GUI REGISTRATION
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:GetChatBar()
	return self.ChatBar
end

function methods:RegisterChatBar(ChatBar)
	assert(
		self.GuiObjects.ChatBarParentFrame,
		"[ChatWindow] CreateGuiObjects must run before RegisterChatBar."
	)
	self.ChatBar = ChatBar
	self.ChatBar:CreateGuiObjects(self.GuiObjects.ChatBarParentFrame)
end

function methods:RegisterChannelsBar(ChannelsBar)
	assert(
		self.GuiObjects.ChannelsBarParentFrame,
		"[ChatWindow] CreateGuiObjects must run before RegisterChannelsBar."
	)
	self.ChannelsBar = ChannelsBar
	self.ChannelsBar:CreateGuiObjects(self.GuiObjects.ChannelsBarParentFrame)
end

function methods:RegisterMessageLogDisplay(MessageLogDisplay)
	assert(
		self.GuiObjects.ChatChannelParentFrame,
		"[ChatWindow] CreateGuiObjects must run before RegisterMessageLogDisplay."
	)
	self.MessageLogDisplay = MessageLogDisplay
	if self.MessageLogDisplay.GuiObject then
		self.MessageLogDisplay.GuiObject.Parent = self.GuiObjects.ChatChannelParentFrame
	end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHANNEL MANAGEMENT
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:AddChannel(channelName)
	assert(
		type(channelName) == "string" and channelName ~= "",
		"[ChatWindow] AddChannel requires a non-empty channel name."
	)
	if self:GetChannel(channelName) then
		error("Channel '" .. channelName .. "' already exists!")
	end
	assert(self.MessageLogDisplay,
		"[ChatWindow] RegisterMessageLogDisplay must run before AddChannel.")
	assert(self.ChannelsBar,
		"[ChatWindow] RegisterChannelsBar must run before AddChannel.")

	local channel = moduleChatChannel.new(channelName, self.MessageLogDisplay)
	self.Channels[channelName:lower()] = channel
	channel:SetActive(false)

	local tab = self.ChannelsBar:AddChannelTab(channelName)
	if tab then
		if tab.NameTag and tab.NameTag:IsA("GuiButton") then
			tab.NameTag.Activated:Connect(function()
				self:SwitchCurrentChannel(channelName)
			end)
		elseif tab.NameTag and tab.NameTag:IsA("TextLabel") then
			tab.NameTag.Active = true
			connectSignal(tab.NameTag.InputBegan, function(input)
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
					or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
					self:SwitchCurrentChannel(channelName)
				end
			end)
		end
		channel:RegisterChannelTab(tab)
	end

	return channel
end

function methods:GetFirstChannel()
	for _, channel in pairs(self.Channels) do
		return channel
	end
	return nil
end

function methods:RemoveChannel(channelName)
	if not self:GetChannel(channelName) then
		error("Channel '" .. tostring(channelName) .. "' does not exist!")
	end

	local indexName = channelName:lower()
	local removedChannel = self.Channels[indexName]
	local needsChannelSwitch = removedChannel == self:GetCurrentChannel()

	if needsChannelSwitch then
		self:SwitchCurrentChannel(nil)
	end

	removedChannel:Destroy()
	self.Channels[indexName] = nil
	if self.ChannelsBar then
		self.ChannelsBar:RemoveChannelTab(channelName)
	end

	if needsChannelSwitch then
		local generalName = ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName or "All"
		local generalChannelExists = self:GetChannel(generalName) ~= nil
		local removingGeneralChannel = indexName == string.lower(generalName)
		local targetSwitchChannel

		if generalChannelExists and not removingGeneralChannel then
			targetSwitchChannel = generalName
		else
			local firstChannel = self:GetFirstChannel()
			targetSwitchChannel = firstChannel and firstChannel.Name or nil
		end

		if targetSwitchChannel then
			self:SwitchCurrentChannel(targetSwitchChannel)
		end
	end

	if not ChatSettings.ShowChannelsBar and self.ChatBar then
		if self.ChatBar.TargetChannel == channelName then
			self.ChatBar:SetChannelTarget(ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName or "All")
		end
	end
end

function methods:GetChannel(channelName)
	if type(channelName) ~= "string" then
		return nil
	end
	return self.Channels[channelName:lower()]
end

function methods:GetTargetMessageChannel()
	if not ChatSettings.ShowChannelsBar then
		return self.ChatBar and self.ChatBar.TargetChannel or nil
	end
	local currentChannel = self:GetCurrentChannel()
	return currentChannel and currentChannel.Name or nil
end

function methods:GetCurrentChannel()
	return self.CurrentChannel
end

function methods:SwitchCurrentChannel(channelName)
	local generalName = ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName or "All"
	if not ChatSettings.ShowChannelsBar then
		local target = self:GetChannel(channelName)
		if target and self.ChatBar then
			self.ChatBar:SetChannelTarget(target.Name)
		end
		channelName = generalName
	end

	local currentChannel = self:GetCurrentChannel()
	local newChannel = self:GetChannel(channelName)

	if not newChannel then
		if channelName == nil and currentChannel == nil then
			return
		end
		error(string.format(
			"[ChatWindow] Cannot switch to missing channel '%s'.",
			tostring(channelName)
			))
	end

	if newChannel == currentChannel then
		return
	end

	if currentChannel then
		currentChannel:SetActive(false)
		if self.ChannelsBar then
			local oldTab = self.ChannelsBar:GetChannelTab(currentChannel.Name)
			if oldTab then
				oldTab:SetActive(false)
			end
		end
	end

	newChannel:SetActive(true)
	if self.ChannelsBar then
		local newTab = self.ChannelsBar:GetChannelTab(newChannel.Name)
		if newTab then
			newTab:SetActive(true)
		end
	end
	self.CurrentChannel = newChannel
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- VISIBILITY AND RESIZING API
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:UpdateFrameVisibility()
	if self.GuiObject then
		self.GuiObject.Visible = self.Visible == true and self.CoreGuiEnabled == true
	end
end

function methods:GetVisible()
	if not self.GuiObject or not self.GuiObject.Parent then
		return false
	end

	if self.Visible ~= true or self.CoreGuiEnabled ~= true
		or self.GuiObject.Visible ~= true then
		return false
	end

	local screenGui = getScreenGuiParent(self.GuiObject)
	return screenGui ~= nil and screenGui.Enabled == true
end

function methods:SetVisible(visible)
	self.Visible = visible == true
	self:UpdateFrameVisibility()
	return self.Visible == true
		and self.GuiObject ~= nil
		and self.GuiObject.Visible == true
end

function methods:GetCoreGuiEnabled()
	return self.CoreGuiEnabled
end

function methods:SetCoreGuiEnabled(enabled)
	self.CoreGuiEnabled = enabled == true
	self:UpdateFrameVisibility()
	return self.CoreGuiEnabled
end

function methods:EnableResizable()
	if self.GuiObjects.ChatResizerFrame then
		self.GuiObjects.ChatResizerFrame.Active = true
	end
end

function methods:DisableResizable()
	if self.GuiObjects.ChatResizerFrame then
		self.GuiObjects.ChatResizerFrame.Active = false
	end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- ANIMATIONS
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:FadeOutBackground(duration)
	if self.ChannelsBar then
		self.ChannelsBar:FadeOutBackground(duration)
	end
	if self.MessageLogDisplay then
		self.MessageLogDisplay:FadeOutBackground(duration)
	end
	if self.ChatBar then
		self.ChatBar:FadeOutBackground(duration)
	end
	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 1
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:FadeInBackground(duration)
	if self.ChannelsBar then
		self.ChannelsBar:FadeInBackground(duration)
	end
	if self.MessageLogDisplay then
		self.MessageLogDisplay:FadeInBackground(duration)
	end
	if self.ChatBar then
		self.ChatBar:FadeInBackground(duration)
	end
	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:FadeOutText(duration)
	-- ChatMain owns the separate ChatBar text fade; do not apply it twice.
	if self.MessageLogDisplay then
		self.MessageLogDisplay:FadeOutText(duration)
	end
	if self.ChannelsBar then
		self.ChannelsBar:FadeOutText(duration)
	end
end

function methods:FadeInText(duration)
	-- ChatMain owns the separate ChatBar text fade; do not apply it twice.
	if self.MessageLogDisplay then
		self.MessageLogDisplay:FadeInText(duration)
	end
	if self.ChannelsBar then
		self.ChannelsBar:FadeInText(duration)
	end
end

function methods:AnimGuiObjects()
	if not self.GuiObjects then
		return
	end
	local backgroundTransparency = self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency or 0.6
	local channelFrame = self.GuiObjects.ChatChannelParentFrame
	local resizerFrame = self.GuiObjects.ChatResizerFrame
	local resizeIcon = self.GuiObjects.ResizeIcon
	if channelFrame then
		channelFrame.BackgroundTransparency = backgroundTransparency
	end
	if resizerFrame then
		resizerFrame.BackgroundTransparency = backgroundTransparency
	end
	if resizeIcon then
		resizeIcon.ImageTransparency = backgroundTransparency
	end
end

function methods:InitializeAnimParams()
	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(0)
end

function methods:Update(dtScale)
	if self.ChatBar and self.ChatBar.Update then
		self.ChatBar:Update(dtScale)
	end
	if self.ChannelsBar and self.ChannelsBar.Update then
		self.ChannelsBar:Update(dtScale)
	end
	if self.MessageLogDisplay and self.MessageLogDisplay.Update then
		self.MessageLogDisplay:Update(dtScale)
	end

	self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency = CurveUtil:Expt(
		self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue,
		dtScale
	)

	self:AnimGuiObjects()
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CONSTRUCTOR
----------------------------------------------------------------

function module.new()
	local object = setmetatable({}, methods)
	object.GuiObject = nil
	object.GuiObjects = {}
	object.ChatBar = nil
	object.ChannelsBar = nil
	object.MessageLogDisplay = nil
	object.Channels = {}
	object.CurrentChannel = nil
	object.Visible = true
	object.CoreGuiEnabled = true
	object.AnimParams = {}
	object:InitializeAnimParams()
	return object
end

local function findExistingRootGui()
	-- Prefer the root supplied by the executor. Do not create a second root.
	local ancestor = script
	while ancestor do
		if ancestor.Name == "NewRobloxGui" then
			return ancestor
		end
		ancestor = ancestor.Parent
	end

	local root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root then
		return root
	end

	-- Compatibility fallback only; this module does not create or clone it.
	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root then
		return root
	end

	return nil
end

local RobloxGui = findExistingRootGui()
local Modules = RobloxGui and RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("Modules") or nil

local function safeRequire(moduleInstance)
	if not moduleInstance or not moduleInstance:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return nil
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleInstance)
	if ok then
		return result
	end
	warn("[Utility] Could not require " .. moduleInstance:GetFullName() .. ": " .. tostring(result))
	return nil
end

-- The 2018+ Settings UI uses CoreScript LocalizationTables for user-facing
-- labels. Register labels built by this utility explicitly; the global
-- RobloxTranslator listener also catches labels inserted later.
local RobloxTranslator = safeRequire(Modules and Modules:FindFirstChild("RobloxTranslator"))
local function registerLocalizedText(instance)
	if not RobloxTranslator or type(RobloxTranslator.RegisterTextObject) ~= "function" then
		return
	end

	local function registerOne(target)
		if not target then return end
		local isTextObject = false
		pcall(function()
			isTextObject = target:IsA("TextLabel") or target:IsA("TextButton")
		end)
		if not isTextObject then return end

		local insideCoreGui = false
		pcall(function() insideCoreGui = CoreGui:IsAncestorOf(target) end)
		if insideCoreGui then
			pcall(function() RobloxTranslator:RegisterTextObject(target) end)
		end
	end

	-- Utility.Create can receive a prebuilt tree. Register the root and every
	-- text-bearing child so nested tab/menu labels are never missed.
	registerOne(instance)
	pcall(function()
		for _, descendant in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do
			registerOne(descendant)
		end
	end)
end

local tenFootInterfaceEnabled = false
do
	local tenFootModule = Modules and Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface")
	local tenFootInterface = safeRequire(tenFootModule)
	if tenFootInterface and type(tenFootInterface.IsEnabled) == "function" then
		local ok, value = pcall(function() return tenFootInterface:IsEnabled() end)
		tenFootInterfaceEnabled = ok and value == true
	end
end

-- FFlags are not part of the public client API. Keep stable local defaults.
local FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization = false
local FFlagFixInactiveSelectorArrows = true

------------------ HELPERS --------------------
local unpackValues = table.unpack or unpack
local Util = {}

function Util.Create(instanceType)
	return function(data)
		local obj = Instance.new(instanceType)
		local parent = nil
		for key, value in pairs(data or {}) do
			if type(key) == "number" then
				if typeof(value) == "Instance" then
					value.Parent = obj
				end
			elseif key == "Parent" then
				parent = value
			else
				obj[key] = value
			end
		end
		if parent then
			obj.Parent = parent
		end
		registerLocalizedText(obj)
		return obj
	end
end

local onResizedCallbacks = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local noSelectionObject = Util.Create("ImageLabel")({
	Name = "NoSelectionImage",
	Image = "",
	BackgroundTransparency = 1,
})

local function clamp(low, high, value)
	return math.max(low, math.min(high, value))
end

local function clampVector2(low, high, input)
	return Vector2.new(
		clamp(low.X, high.X, input.X),
		clamp(low.Y, high.Y, input.Y)
	)
end

local function getViewportSize()
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not camera then
		return DEFAULT_VIEWPORT_SIZE
	end

	local size = camera.ViewportSize
	if size.X &lt;= 1 or size.Y &lt;= 1 then
		return DEFAULT_VIEWPORT_SIZE
	end
	return size
end

local function isPortrait()
	local viewport = getViewportSize()
	return viewport.Y &gt; viewport.X
end

local function isSmallTouchScreen()
	local viewport = getViewportSize()
	return UserInputService.TouchEnabled and (viewport.Y &lt; 500 or viewport.X &lt; 700)
end

local function isTenFootInterface()
	return tenFootInterfaceEnabled
end

local function usesSelectedObject()
	if VRService.VREnabled then
		return false
	end
	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.GamepadEnabled then
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local function getSelectedObject()
	local ok, selected = pcall(function()
		return GuiService.SelectedObject
	end)
	return ok and selected or nil
end

local function setSelectedObject(object)
	pcall(function()
		GuiService.SelectedObject = object
	end)
end

local function bindAction(name, callback, createTouchButton, ...)
	local inputs = { ... }
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(
			name,
			callback,
			createTouchButton == true,
			ACTION_PRIORITY,
			unpackValues(inputs)
		)
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("[Utility] Failed to bind action '" .. tostring(name) .. "': " .. tostring(err))
	end
	return ok
end

local function unbindAction(name)
	pcall(function()
		ContextActionService:UnbindAction(name)
	end)
end

local function getPanel3D()
	local vrFolder = Modules and Modules:FindFirstChild("VR")
	return safeRequire(vrFolder and vrFolder:FindFirstChild("Panel3D"))
end

local function setSelectionTuple(name, selections)
	-- These selection-group helpers are legacy/deprecated and may be absent.
	pcall(function()
		GuiService:RemoveSelectionGroup(name)
	end)
	pcall(function()
		GuiService:AddSelectionTuple(name, unpackValues(selections))
	end)
end

local function isPosOverGui(position, gui)
	if not gui or not gui:IsA("GuiObject") then
		return false
	end
	local topLeft = gui.AbsolutePosition
	local size = gui.AbsoluteSize
	return position.X &gt; topLeft.X and position.X &lt; topLeft.X + size.X
		and position.Y &gt; topLeft.Y and position.Y &lt; topLeft.Y + size.Y
end

local function isPosOverGuiWithClipping(position, gui)
	if not isPosOverGui(position, gui) then
		return false
	end

	local check = gui
	while check and check ~= CoreGui do
		if check:IsA("GuiObject") then
			if not check.Visible then
				return false
			end
			if check.ClipsDescendants and not isPosOverGui(position, check) then
				return false
			end
		end
		check = check.Parent
	end
	return true
end

local function areGuisIntersecting(a, b)
	if not a or not b or not a:IsA("GuiObject") or not b:IsA("GuiObject") then
		return true
	end
	local aPos, aSize = a.AbsolutePosition, a.AbsoluteSize
	local bPos, bSize = b.AbsolutePosition, b.AbsoluteSize
	return aPos.X &lt; bPos.X + bSize.X and aPos.X + aSize.X &gt; bPos.X
		and aPos.Y &lt; bPos.Y + bSize.Y and aPos.Y + aSize.Y &gt; bPos.Y
end

local function isGuiVisible(gui)
	if not gui or not gui:IsA("GuiObject") then
		return false
	end
	local check = gui
	while check and check ~= CoreGui do
		if check:IsA("GuiObject") then
			if not check.Visible then
				return false
			end
			if check.ClipsDescendants and not areGuisIntersecting(check, gui) then
				return false
			end
		end
		check = check.Parent
	end
	return true
end

local function addHoverState(button, instance, onNormalButtonState, onHoverButtonState)
	local function normal()
		if not FFlagFixInactiveSelectorArrows or button.Active then
			onNormalButtonState(instance)
		end
	end
	local function hover()
		if not FFlagFixInactiveSelectorArrows or button.Active then
			onHoverButtonState(instance)
		end
	end
	button.MouseEnter:Connect(hover)
	button.SelectionGained:Connect(hover)
	button.MouseLeave:Connect(normal)
	button.SelectionLost:Connect(normal)
	normal()
end

local function addOnResizedCallback(key, callback)
	onResizedCallbacks[key] = callback
	callback(getViewportSize(), isPortrait())
end

local function fireResizeCallbacks()
	local size = getViewportSize()
	local portrait = size.Y &gt; size.X
	for key, callback in pairs(onResizedCallbacks) do
		if key and (typeof(key) ~= "Instance" or key.Parent ~= nil) then
			local ok, err = pcall(callback, size, portrait)
			if not ok then
				warn("[Utility] Resize callback failed: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		else
			onResizedCallbacks[key] = nil
		end
	end
end

local cameraViewportConnection
local function watchCamera(camera)
	if cameraViewportConnection then
		cameraViewportConnection:Disconnect()
		cameraViewportConnection = nil
	end
	if camera then
		cameraViewportConnection = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(fireResizeCallbacks)
	end
	fireResizeCallbacks()
end

if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
	watchCamera(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
end
Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
	watchCamera(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
end)

	[Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad3] = true,
	[Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad4] = true,
