	[Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad7] = true,
	[Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad8] = true,
}

------------------ TWEENING --------------------
local function Linear(t, beginning, change, duration)
	if duration &lt;= 0 or t &gt;= duration then
		return beginning + change
	end
	return change * t / duration + beginning
end

local function EaseOutQuad(t, beginning, change, duration)
	if duration &lt;= 0 or t &gt;= duration then
		return beginning + change
	end
	t = t / duration
	return beginning - change * t * (t - 2)
end

local function EaseInOutQuad(t, beginning, change, duration)
	if duration &lt;= 0 or t &gt;= duration then
		return beginning + change
	end
	t = t / duration
	if t &lt; 0.5 then
		return 2 * change * t * t + beginning
	end
	return beginning + change * (2 * (2 - t) * t - 1)
end

local function PropertyTweener(instance, property, startValue, finalValue, duration, easingFunc, callback)
	local tween = {}
	duration = math.max(0, tonumber(duration) or 0)
	easingFunc = easingFunc or Linear
	tween.StartTime = os.clock()
	tween.EndTime = tween.StartTime + duration
	tween.Cancelled = false

	local finished = false
	local percentComplete = 0

	local function setValue(value)
		if not instance then
			return false
		end
		local ok = pcall(function()
			instance[property] = value
		end)
		return ok
	end

	local function finalize()
		if finished then
			return
		end
		setValue(easingFunc(duration, startValue, finalValue - startValue, duration))
		finished = true
		percentComplete = 1
		if callback then
			local ok, err = pcall(callback)
			if not ok then
				warn("[Utility] Tween callback failed: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end

	setValue(easingFunc(0, startValue, finalValue - startValue, duration))

	if duration == 0 then
		finalize()
	else
		task.spawn(function()
			while not tween.Cancelled and not finished do
				local elapsed = os.clock() - tween.StartTime
				if elapsed &gt;= duration then
					break
				end
				if not setValue(easingFunc(elapsed, startValue, finalValue - startValue, duration)) then
					tween.Cancelled = true
					return
				end
				percentComplete = clamp(0, 1, elapsed / duration)
				RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
			end
			if not tween.Cancelled and not finished then
				finalize()
			end
		end)
	end

	function tween:GetFinal()
		return finalValue
	end
	function tween:GetPercentComplete()
		return percentComplete
	end
	function tween:IsFinished()
		return finished
	end
	function tween:Finish()
		if not finished then
			self.Cancelled = true
			finalize()
		end
	end
	function tween:Cancel()
		self.Cancelled = true
	end
	return tween
end

------------------ SIGNAL --------------------
local function CreateSignal()
	local signal = {}
	local event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local lastArguments
	local lastArgumentCount = 0

	function signal:fire(...)
		lastArguments = { ... }
		lastArgumentCount = select("#", ...)
		event:Fire()
	end
	function signal:connect(callback)
		assert(type(callback) == "function", "connect(callback) expects a function")
		return event.Event:Connect(function()
			if lastArguments then
				callback(unpackValues(lastArguments, 1, lastArgumentCount))
			end
		end)
	end
	function signal:wait()
		event.Event:Wait()
		if not lastArguments then
			error("Signal fired without argument data")
		end
		return unpackValues(lastArguments, 1, lastArgumentCount)
	end
	function signal:Destroy()
		event:Destroy()
	end
	return signal
end

------------------ BUTTON HELPERS --------------------
local function MakeDefaultButton(name, size, clickFunction, pageRef, hubRef)
	local selectionOverride = Util.Create("ImageLabel")({
		Image = "",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	})

	local button = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
		Name = name .. "Button",
		Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuButton.png",
		ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice,
		SliceCenter = Rect.new(8, 6, 46, 44),
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = size,
		ZIndex = 2,
		SelectionImageObject = selectionOverride,
	})

	local enabled = Util.Create("BoolValue")({
		Name = "Enabled",
		Parent = button,
		Value = true,
	})

	if clickFunction then
		button.Activated:Connect(function()
			clickFunction(gamepadSet[UserInputService:GetLastInputType()] or false)
		end)
	end

	local function isPointerInput(inputObject)
		return inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
			or inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
	end

	local rowRef
	local function setRowRef(ref)
		rowRef = ref
	end

	local function selectButton()
		local hub = hubRef or (pageRef and pageRef.HubRef)
		if hub and hub.Active == false then
			return
		end
		button.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuButtonSelected.png"
		local scrollTarget = rowRef or button
		if hub and type(hub.ScrollToFrame) == "function" then
			pcall(function()
				hub:ScrollToFrame(scrollTarget)
			end)
		end
	end

	local function deselectButton()
		if button:GetAttribute("YekuKeepBlue") == true then
			button.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuButtonSelected.png"
		else
			button.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuButton.png"
		end
	end

	button.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject)
		if button.Selectable and isPointerInput(inputObject) then
			selectButton()
		end
	end)
	button.InputEnded:Connect(function(inputObject)
		if button.Selectable and getSelectedObject() ~= button and isPointerInput(inputObject) then
			deselectButton()
		end
	end)
	button.SelectionGained:Connect(selectButton)
	button.SelectionLost:Connect(deselectButton)

	GuiService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SelectedObject"):Connect(function()
		if not usesSelectedObject() then
			return
		end
		if getSelectedObject() ~= button then
			deselectButton()
		elseif button.Selectable then
			selectButton()
		end
	end)

	return button, setRowRef
end

local function MakeButton(name, text, size, clickFunction, pageRef, hubRef)
	local button, setRowRef = MakeDefaultButton(name, size, clickFunction, pageRef, hubRef)
	local textLabel = Util.Create("TextLabel")({
		Name = name .. "TextLabel",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -8),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
		Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
		TextSize = 24,
		Text = tostring(text or ""),
		TextScaled = true,
		TextWrapped = true,
		ZIndex = 2,
		Parent = button,
	})
	local constraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
	constraint.Parent = textLabel

	if isSmallTouchScreen() then
		textLabel.TextSize = 18
	elseif isTenFootInterface() then
		textLabel.TextSize = 36
	end
	constraint.MaxTextSize = textLabel.TextSize
	return button, textLabel, setRowRef
end

local function MakeImageButton(name, image, size, imageSize, clickFunction, pageRef, hubRef)
	local button, setRowRef = MakeDefaultButton(name, size, clickFunction, pageRef, hubRef)
	local imageLabel = Util.Create("ImageLabel")({
		Name = name .. "ImageLabel",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = imageSize,
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Image = image,
		ZIndex = 2,
		Parent = button,
	})
	return button, imageLabel, setRowRef
end

local function AddButtonRow(pageToAddTo, name, text, size, clickFunction, hubRef)
	local button, textLabel, setRowRef = MakeButton(name, text, size, clickFunction, pageToAddTo, hubRef)
	local row = Util.Create("Frame")({
		Name = name .. "Row",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, size.Y.Scale, size.Y.Offset),
		Parent = pageToAddTo.Page,
	})
	button.Parent = row
	button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
	button.Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 0)
	setRowRef(row)
	return row, button, textLabel, setRowRef
end

------------------ DROP-DOWN --------------------
local function CreateDropDown(dropDownStringTable, startPosition, settingsHub)
	local DEFAULT_DROPDOWN_TEXT = "Choose One"
	local SCROLLING_FRAME_PIXEL_OFFSET = 25
	local SELECTION_TEXT_COLOR_NORMAL = Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178)
	local SELECTION_TEXT_COLOR_NORMAL_VR = Color3.fromRGB(229, 229, 229)
	local SELECTION_TEXT_COLOR_HIGHLIGHTED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

	if type(dropDownStringTable) ~= "table" then
		error("CreateDropDown expects a table as its first argument", 2)
	end

	local this = { CurrentIndex = 0, Selections = {}, SelectionInfo = {} }
	local indexChangedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	indexChangedEvent.Name = "IndexChanged"
	this.IndexChanged = indexChangedEvent.Event

	local interactable = true
	local active = false
	local lastSelectedObject
	local lastStringTable = dropDownStringTable
	local actionId = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
	local buttonEnabled
	local selectionChangedConnection
	local fullscreenParent = RobloxGui
	local vrConnection

	local DropDownFullscreenFrame = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
		Name = "DropDownFullscreenFrame",
		BackgroundTransparency = DROPDOWN_BG_TRANSPARENCY,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
		ZIndex = 10,
		Active = true,
		Visible = false,
		Selectable = false,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Parent = fullscreenParent,
	})

	local function updateParentForVR()
		if VRService.VREnabled then
			local panel3D = getPanel3D()
			local ok, panelGui = pcall(function()
				return panel3D and panel3D.Get("SettingsMenu"):GetGUI()
			end)
			if ok and panelGui then
				fullscreenParent = panelGui
				DropDownFullscreenFrame.Parent = panelGui
				DropDownFullscreenFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			else
				fullscreenParent = RobloxGui
				DropDownFullscreenFrame.Parent = RobloxGui
			end
		else
			fullscreenParent = RobloxGui
			DropDownFullscreenFrame.Parent = RobloxGui
			DropDownFullscreenFrame.BackgroundTransparency = DROPDOWN_BG_TRANSPARENCY
		end
	end

	local DropDownSelectionFrame = Util.Create("ImageLabel")({
		Name = "DropDownSelectionFrame",
		Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuButton.png",
		ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice,
		SliceCenter = Rect.new(8, 6, 46, 44),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0.9, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		ZIndex = 10,
		Parent = DropDownFullscreenFrame,
	})

	local DropDownScrollingFrame = Util.Create("ScrollingFrame")({
		Name = "DropDownScrollingFrame",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -SCROLLING_FRAME_PIXEL_OFFSET),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10),
		ZIndex = 10,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		ScrollBarThickness = 8,
		Parent = DropDownSelectionFrame,
	})

	local function setHubActive(value)
		if settingsHub and type(settingsHub.SetActive) == "function" then
			pcall(function() settingsHub:SetActive(value) end)
		end
	end

	local function hideDropDownSelection(actionName, inputState)
		if actionName ~= nil and inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
		end
		if not active and not DropDownFullscreenFrame.Visible then
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
		end

		if this.DropDownFrame then
			this.DropDownFrame.Selectable = interactable
		end
		setHubActive(true)
		if DropDownFullscreenFrame.Visible and usesSelectedObject() then
			setSelectedObject(lastSelectedObject)
		end
		DropDownFullscreenFrame.Visible = false
		active = false
		if selectionChangedConnection then
			selectionChangedConnection:Disconnect()
			selectionChangedConnection = nil
		end
		unbindAction(actionId .. "Action")
		unbindAction(actionId .. "FreezeAction")
		if buttonEnabled then
			buttonEnabled.Value = interactable
		end
		if VRService.VREnabled then
			local panel3D = getPanel3D()
			pcall(function()
				panel3D:Get("SettingsMenu"):SetSubpanelDepth(DropDownFullscreenFrame, 0)
			end)
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end

	local function openDropDown()
		if not interactable or active or #this.Selections == 0 then
			return
		end
		this.DropDownFrame.Selectable = false
		active = true
		DropDownFullscreenFrame.Visible = true
		if VRService.VREnabled then
			local panel3D = getPanel3D()
			pcall(function()
				panel3D:Get("SettingsMenu"):SetSubpanelDepth(DropDownFullscreenFrame, 0.5)
			end)
		end

		if not this.CurrentIndex or this.CurrentIndex &lt; 1 then
			this.CurrentIndex = 1
		end
		lastSelectedObject = this.DropDownFrame
		setSelectedObject(this.Selections[this.CurrentIndex])

		if selectionChangedConnection then
			selectionChangedConnection:Disconnect()
		end
		selectionChangedConnection = GuiService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SelectedObject"):Connect(function()
			local selected = getSelectedObject()
			for _, label in ipairs(this.Selections) do
				label.TextColor3 = selected == label and SELECTION_TEXT_COLOR_HIGHLIGHTED
					or (VRService.VREnabled and SELECTION_TEXT_COLOR_NORMAL_VR or SELECTION_TEXT_COLOR_NORMAL)
			end
		end)

		bindAction(actionId .. "FreezeAction", function()
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		end, false, Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard, Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1)
		bindAction(actionId .. "Action", hideDropDownSelection, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB, Enum.KeyCode.Escape)
		setHubActive(false)
		if buttonEnabled then
			buttonEnabled.Value = false
		end
	end

	local dropDownFrameSize = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 50)
	this.DropDownFrame = MakeButton("DropDownFrame", DEFAULT_DROPDOWN_TEXT, dropDownFrameSize, openDropDown, nil, settingsHub)
	this.DropDownFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
	this.DropDownFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
	buttonEnabled = this.DropDownFrame:FindFirstChild("Enabled")

	local selectedTextLabel = this.DropDownFrame:FindFirstChild("DropDownFrameTextLabel")
	selectedTextLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 0)
	selectedTextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 1, -8)
	selectedTextLabel.ClipsDescendants = true
	selectedTextLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

	this.DropDownImage = Util.Create("ImageLabel")({
		Name = "DropDownImage",
		Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/DropDown/DropDown.png",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 10),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0),
		ZIndex = 2,
		Parent = this.DropDownFrame,
	})

	local function setSelection(index)
		local selection = this.Selections[index]
		if not selection then
			return false
		end
		selectedTextLabel.Text = selection.Text
		this.CurrentIndex = index
		indexChangedEvent:Fire(index)
		return true
	end

	local function setSelectionByValue(value)
		for index, selection in ipairs(this.Selections) do
			if selection.Text == value then
				return setSelection(index)
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function setIsFaded(faded)
		local transparency = faded and 0.5 or 0
		selectedTextLabel.TextTransparency = transparency
		this.DropDownFrame.ImageTransparency = transparency
		this.DropDownImage.ImageTransparency = transparency
	end

	function this:SetSelectionIndex(newIndex)
		setSelection(tonumber(newIndex))
	end
	function this:SetSelectionByValue(value)
		return setSelectionByValue(value)
	end
	function this:ResetSelectionIndex()
		this.CurrentIndex = nil
		selectedTextLabel.Text = DEFAULT_DROPDOWN_TEXT
		hideDropDownSelection()
	end
	function this:GetSelectedIndex()
		return this.CurrentIndex
	end
	function this:SetZIndex(newZIndex)
		this.DropDownFrame.ZIndex = newZIndex
		this.DropDownImage.ZIndex = newZIndex
		selectedTextLabel.ZIndex = newZIndex
	end
	function this:SetInteractable(value)
		interactable = value == true
		this.DropDownFrame.Selectable = interactable
		if not interactable then
			hideDropDownSelection()
			setIsFaded(VRService.VREnabled)
			if not VRService.VREnabled then
				this:SetZIndex(1)
			end
		else
			setIsFaded(false)
			if not VRService.VREnabled then
				this:SetZIndex(2)
			end
		end
		if buttonEnabled then
			buttonEnabled.Value = interactable and not active
		end
	end

	function this:UpdateDropDownList(newList)
		if type(newList) ~= "table" then
			newList = {}
		end
		lastStringTable = newList
		for _, selection in ipairs(this.Selections) do
			selection:Destroy()
		end
		this.Selections = {}
		this.SelectionInfo = {}

		local vrEnabled = VRService.VREnabled
		local font = vrEnabled and Enum.Font.SourceSansBold or Enum.Font.SourceSans
		local textSize = vrEnabled and 36 or 24
		local itemHeight = vrEnabled and 70 or 50
		local itemSpacing = itemHeight + 1
		local dropDownWidth = vrEnabled and 600 or 400

		for index, value in ipairs(newList) do
			local selectionIndex = index
			local selectionOverride = Util.Create("Frame")({
				BackgroundTransparency = 0.7,
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
			})
			local selection = Util.Create("TextButton")({
				Name = "Selection" .. tostring(index),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				AutoButtonColor = false,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, itemHeight),
				Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, (index - 1) * itemSpacing),
				TextColor3 = vrEnabled and SELECTION_TEXT_COLOR_NORMAL_VR or SELECTION_TEXT_COLOR_NORMAL,
				Font = font,
				TextSize = textSize,
				Text = tostring(value),
				ZIndex = 10,
				SelectionImageObject = selectionOverride,
				Parent = DropDownScrollingFrame,
			})
			if selectionIndex == startPosition or (not startPosition and selectionIndex == 1) then
				this.CurrentIndex = selectionIndex
				selectedTextLabel.Text = selection.Text
				selection.TextColor3 = SELECTION_TEXT_COLOR_HIGHLIGHTED
			end

			local function clicked()
				setSelection(selectionIndex)
				hideDropDownSelection()
			end
			selection.Activated:Connect(clicked)
			selection.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				if usesSelectedObject() then
					setSelectedObject(selection)
				end
			end)
			this.Selections[selectionIndex] = selection
			this.SelectionInfo[selection] = { Clicked = clicked }
		end

		setSelectionTuple(actionId, this.Selections)
		DropDownScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #newList * itemSpacing)

		local canvasHeight = DropDownScrollingFrame.CanvasSize.Y.Offset
		if canvasHeight &lt; (DropDownFullscreenFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y - 10) then
			DropDownSelectionFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, dropDownWidth, 0, canvasHeight + SCROLLING_FRAME_PIXEL_OFFSET)
		else
			DropDownSelectionFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, dropDownWidth, 0.9, 0)
		end
		if #this.Selections == 0 then
			this.CurrentIndex = nil
			selectedTextLabel.Text = DEFAULT_DROPDOWN_TEXT
		end
	end

	local enterIsDown = false
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not active or input.UserInputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			return
		end
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Return then
			enterIsDown = true
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if not active or input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Return or not enterIsDown then
			return
		end
		enterIsDown = false
		local selected = getSelectedObject()
		if selected == this.DropDownFrame then
			openDropDown()
		elseif this.SelectionInfo[selected] then
			this.SelectionInfo[selected].Clicked()
		end
	end)

	vrConnection = VRService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled"):Connect(updateParentForVR)
	updateParentForVR()
	this:UpdateDropDownList(dropDownStringTable)
	DropDownFullscreenFrame.Activated:Connect(function() hideDropDownSelection() end)

	if settingsHub and settingsHub.PoppedMenu and settingsHub.PoppedMenu.Connect then
		settingsHub.PoppedMenu:Connect(function(poppedMenu)
			if poppedMenu == DropDownFullscreenFrame then
				hideDropDownSelection()
			end
		end)
	end

	return this
end

------------------ SELECTOR --------------------
local function CreateSelector(selectionStringTable, startPosition)
	if type(selectionStringTable) ~= "table" then
		error("CreateSelector expects a table as its first argument", 2)
	end

	local this = { HubRef = nil, CurrentIndex = 0, Selections = {} }
	local indexChangedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	indexChangedEvent.Name = "IndexChanged"
	this.IndexChanged = indexChangedEvent.Event

	local interactable = true
	local lastInputDirection = 0
	local tweenTime = 0.15
	local visibleLabels = {}
	local isAutoSelectButton = {}
	local isInTree = true
	local guiServiceConnection

	this.SelectorFrame = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
		Name = "Selector",
		Image = "",
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 50),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		ZIndex = 2,
		SelectionImageObject = noSelectionObject,
	})

	local leftButton = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
		Name = "LeftButton",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50),
		Image = "",
		ZIndex = 3,
		Selectable = false,
		SelectionImageObject = noSelectionObject,
		Parent = this.SelectorFrame,
	})
	local rightButton = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
		Name = "RightButton",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50),
		Image = "",
		ZIndex = 3,
		Selectable = false,
		SelectionImageObject = noSelectionObject,
		Parent = this.SelectorFrame,
	})
	local leftButtonImage = Util.Create("ImageLabel")({
		Name = "LeftButtonImage",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 30),
		Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/Left.png",
		ImageColor3 = ARROW_COLOR,
		ZIndex = 4,
		Parent = leftButton,
	})
	local rightButtonImage = Util.Create("ImageLabel")({
		Name = "RightButtonImage",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 30),
		Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/Right.png",
		ImageColor3 = ARROW_COLOR,
		ZIndex = 4,
		Parent = rightButton,
	})

	if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		addHoverState(leftButton, leftButtonImage,
			function(instance) instance.ImageColor3 = ARROW_COLOR end,
			function(instance) instance.ImageColor3 = ARROW_COLOR_HOVER end)
		addHoverState(rightButton, rightButtonImage,
			function(instance) instance.ImageColor3 = ARROW_COLOR end,
			function(instance) instance.ImageColor3 = ARROW_COLOR_HOVER end)
	end

	local autoSelectButton = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
		Name = "AutoSelectButton",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Image = "",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, leftButton.Size.X.Offset, 0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -leftButton.Size.X.Offset * 2, 1, 0),
		Parent = this.SelectorFrame,
		ZIndex = 2,
		SelectionImageObject = noSelectionObject,
	})
	isAutoSelectButton[autoSelectButton] = true

	local function setSelection(index, direction)
		if #this.Selections == 0 then
			this.CurrentIndex = 0
			return
		end
		index = clamp(1, #this.Selections, math.floor(tonumber(index) or 1))
		direction = direction or 1
		for i, label in ipairs(this.Selections) do
			local isSelected = i == index
			local targetPosition = UDim2.new(0, leftButton.Size.X.Offset, 0, 0)
			if visibleLabels[label] then
				targetPosition = UDim2.new(0, leftButton.Size.X.Offset * -direction * 3, 0, 0)
			else
				targetPosition = UDim2.new(0, leftButton.Size.X.Offset * direction * 3, 0, 0)
			end
			if targetPosition.X.Offset &lt; 0 then
				targetPosition = UDim2.new(0, targetPosition.X.Offset + label.AbsoluteSize.X / 4, 0, 0)
			end

			if isSelected then
				visibleLabels[label] = true
				label.Position = targetPosition
				label.Visible = true
				PropertyTweener(label, "TextTransparency", 1, 0, tweenTime * 1.1, EaseOutQuad)
				if label:IsDescendantOf(game) then
					label:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(0, leftButton.Size.X.Offset, 0, 0), Enum.EasingDirection.In,
						Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, tweenTime, true)
				else
					label.Position = UDim2.new(0, leftButton.Size.X.Offset, 0, 0)
				end
				this.CurrentIndex = i
				indexChangedEvent:Fire(i)
			elseif visibleLabels[label] then
				visibleLabels[label] = false
				PropertyTweener(label, "TextTransparency", 0, 1, tweenTime * 1.1, EaseOutQuad)
				if label:IsDescendantOf(game) then
					label:TweenPosition(targetPosition, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, tweenTime * 0.9, true)
				else
					label.Position = targetPosition
					label.Visible = false
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function stepFunc(inputObject, step)
		if not interactable or #this.Selections &lt;= 1 then
			return
		end
		if inputObject and inputObject.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			and inputObject.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			and not tostring(inputObject.UserInputType):match("Gamepad")
			and inputObject.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			return
		end
		if usesSelectedObject() then
			setSelectedObject(this.SelectorFrame)
		end
		local newIndex = this.CurrentIndex + step
		if newIndex &gt; #this.Selections then
			newIndex = 1
		elseif newIndex &lt; 1 then
			newIndex = #this.Selections
		end
		setSelection(newIndex, step &gt; 0 and 1 or -1)
	end

	function this:SetSelectionIndex(newIndex)
		newIndex = tonumber(newIndex)
		if not newIndex or #this.Selections == 0 then
			return
		end
		setSelection(newIndex, newIndex &gt;= this.CurrentIndex and 1 or -1)
	end
	function this:GetSelectedIndex()
		return this.CurrentIndex
	end
	function this:SetZIndex(newZIndex)
		leftButton.ZIndex = newZIndex
		rightButton.ZIndex = newZIndex
		leftButtonImage.ZIndex = newZIndex + 1
		rightButtonImage.ZIndex = newZIndex + 1
		for _, label in ipairs(this.Selections) do
			label.ZIndex = newZIndex
		end
	end
	function this:SetInteractable(value)
		interactable = value == true
		this.SelectorFrame.Selectable = interactable
		leftButton.Active = interactable
		rightButton.Active = interactable
		leftButtonImage.ImageColor3 = interactable and ARROW_COLOR or ARROW_COLOR_INACTIVE
		rightButtonImage.ImageColor3 = interactable and ARROW_COLOR or ARROW_COLOR_INACTIVE
		for _, label in ipairs(this.Selections) do
			label.TextColor3 = interactable and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(49, 49, 49)
		end
	end
	function this:UpdateOptions(options)
		for _, label in ipairs(this.Selections) do
			label:Destroy()
		end
		this.Selections = {}
		visibleLabels = {}
		if type(options) ~= "table" then
			options = {}
		end

		for index, value in ipairs(options) do
			local label = Util.Create("TextLabel")({
				Name = "Selection" .. tostring(index),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -leftButton.Size.X.Offset * 2, 1, 0),
				Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
				TextColor3 = interactable and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(49, 49, 49),
				TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
				TextTransparency = 0.5,
				Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans,
				TextSize = isTenFootInterface() and 36 or 24,
				Text = tostring(value),
				ZIndex = 2,
				Visible = false,
				Parent = this.SelectorFrame,
			})
			this.Selections[index] = label
			visibleLabels[label] = false
		end

		local initialIndex = tonumber(startPosition) or 1
		if #this.Selections &gt; 0 then
			initialIndex = clamp(1, #this.Selections, initialIndex)
			for index, label in ipairs(this.Selections) do
				if index == initialIndex then
					label.Visible = true
					label.Position = UDim2.new(0, leftButton.Size.X.Offset, 0, 0)
					label.TextTransparency = 0
					visibleLabels[label] = true
				end
			end
			this.CurrentIndex = initialIndex
		else
			this.CurrentIndex = 0
		end
		local hasMultiple = #this.Selections &gt; 1
		leftButton.Visible = hasMultiple
		rightButton.Visible = hasMultiple
		autoSelectButton.Active = interactable and hasMultiple
	end

	autoSelectButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not interactable or #this.Selections &lt;= 1 then
			return
		end
		local nextIndex = this.CurrentIndex + 1
		if nextIndex &gt; #this.Selections then
			nextIndex = 1
		end
		setSelection(nextIndex, 1)
		if usesSelectedObject() then
			setSelectedObject(this.SelectorFrame)
		end
	end)

	local function updateVRSelection()
		local enabled = VRService.VREnabled
		leftButton.Selectable = enabled and interactable
		rightButton.Selectable = enabled and interactable
		autoSelectButton.Selectable = enabled and interactable
	end
	VRService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled"):Connect(updateVRSelection)
	updateVRSelection()

	leftButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
			stepFunc(nil, -1)
		end
	end)
	rightButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
			stepFunc(nil, 1)
		end
	end)
	leftButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			stepFunc(input, -1)
		end
	end)
	rightButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			stepFunc(input, 1)
		end
	end)

	local function connectToGuiService()
		if guiServiceConnection then
			guiServiceConnection:Disconnect()
		end
		guiServiceConnection = GuiService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SelectedObject"):Connect(function()
			if #this.Selections == 0 then
				return
			end
			local selected = getSelectedObject()
			local current = this.Selections[this.CurrentIndex]
			if not current then
				return
			end
			if selected == this.SelectorFrame or (selected and isAutoSelectButton[selected]) then
				current.TextTransparency = 0
			else
				current.TextTransparency = 0.5
			end
		end)
	end

	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not interactable or not isInTree or #this.Selections == 0 then
			return
		end
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			return
		end
		if getSelectedObject() ~= this.SelectorFrame then
			return
		end
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Left or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A then
			stepFunc(input, -1)
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Right or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D then
			stepFunc(input, 1)
		end
	end)

	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not interactable or not isInTree or input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 then
			if not isInTree then lastInputDirection = 0 end
			return
		end
		if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1 then
			return
		end
		local selected = getSelectedObject()
		if not selected or not selected:IsDescendantOf(this.SelectorFrame.Parent or this.SelectorFrame) then
			return
		end
		if input.Position.X &gt; CONTROLLER_THUMBSTICK_DEADZONE and input.Delta.X &gt; 0 and lastInputDirection ~= 1 then
			lastInputDirection = 1
			stepFunc(input, 1)
		elseif input.Position.X &lt; -CONTROLLER_THUMBSTICK_DEADZONE and input.Delta.X &lt; 0 and lastInputDirection ~= -1 then
			lastInputDirection = -1
			stepFunc(input, -1)
		elseif math.abs(input.Position.X) &lt; CONTROLLER_THUMBSTICK_DEADZONE then
			lastInputDirection = 0
		end
	end)

	this.SelectorFrame.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
		isInTree = parent ~= nil
		if not isInTree and guiServiceConnection then
			guiServiceConnection:Disconnect()
			guiServiceConnection = nil
		elseif isInTree then
			connectToGuiService()
		end
	end)

	addOnResizedCallback(this.SelectorFrame, function(viewportSize, portrait)
		local textSize = portrait and 16 or (isTenFootInterface() and 36 or 24)
		for _, label in ipairs(this.Selections) do
			label.TextSize = textSize
		end
	end)

	this:UpdateOptions(selectionStringTable)
	connectToGuiService()
	return this
end

------------------ ALERT --------------------
local function ShowAlert(alertMessage, okButtonText, settingsHub, okPressedFunc, hasBackground)
	local parent = RobloxGui
	if not parent then
		warn("[Utility] ShowAlert skipped: NewRobloxGui root was not found")
		return
	end
	if parent:FindFirstChild("AlertViewBacking") then
		return
	end

	local alertBacking
	local vrConnection
	local removeActionId = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)

	local function updateParentForVR()
		if VRService.VREnabled then
			local panel3D = getPanel3D()
			local ok, gui = pcall(function()
				return panel3D and panel3D.Get("SettingsMenu"):GetGUI()
			end)
			if ok and gui then
				parent = gui
			else
				parent = RobloxGui
			end
		else
			parent = RobloxGui
		end
		if alertBacking and alertBacking.Parent then
			alertBacking.Parent = parent
			if VRService.VREnabled then
				local panel3D = getPanel3D()
				pcall(function() panel3D:Get("SettingsMenu"):SetSubpanelDepth(alertBacking, 0.5) end)
			end
		end
	end

	alertBacking = Util.Create("ImageLabel")({
		Name = "AlertViewBacking",
		Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuButton.png",
		ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice,
		SliceCenter = Rect.new(8, 6, 46, 44),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		ImageTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 400, 0, 350),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -200, 0.5, -175),
		ZIndex = 9,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	updateParentForVR()

	if hasBackground or VRService.VREnabled then
		alertBacking.ImageTransparency = 0
	else
		alertBacking.Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0, 350)
		alertBacking.Position = UDim2.new(0.1, 0, 0.1, 0)
	end

	local viewport = getViewportSize()
	if viewport.Y &lt;= alertBacking.Size.Y.Offset then
		alertBacking.Size = UDim2.new(alertBacking.Size.X.Scale, alertBacking.Size.X.Offset,
			alertBacking.Size.Y.Scale, viewport.Y)
		alertBacking.Position = UDim2.new(alertBacking.Position.X.Scale,
			-alertBacking.Size.X.Offset / 2, 0.5, -alertBacking.Size.Y.Offset / 2)
	end

	Util.Create("TextLabel")({
		Name = "AlertViewMessage",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(0.95, 0, 0.6, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.025, 0, 0.05, 0),
		Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
		TextSize = 36,
		Text = tostring(alertMessage or ""),
		TextWrapped = true,
		TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
		ZIndex = 10,
		Parent = alertBacking,
	})

	local buttonSize = hasBackground and UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 60) or UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
	local buttonPosition = hasBackground and UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.65, 0) or UDim2.new(0.5, -100, 0.65, 0)
	local button, buttonText = MakeButton("AlertViewButton", okButtonText or "OK", buttonSize, function()
		-- The alert is dismissed only by an activation/valid key event.
		if not alertBacking then return end
		if VRService.VREnabled then
			local panel3D = getPanel3D()
			pcall(function() panel3D:Get("SettingsMenu"):SetSubpanelDepth(alertBacking, 0) end)
		end
		alertBacking:Destroy()
		alertBacking = nil
		unbindAction(removeActionId)
		setSelectedObject(nil)
		if vrConnection then vrConnection:Disconnect() end
		if settingsHub and type(settingsHub.ShowBar) == "function" then
			pcall(function() settingsHub:ShowBar() end)
		end
		if okPressedFunc then
			local ok, err = pcall(okPressedFunc)
			if not ok then warn("[Utility] Alert callback failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
		end
	end)
	button.Position = buttonPosition
	button:SetAttribute("YekuKeepBlue", true)
	button.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuButtonSelected.png"
	button.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	button.NextSelectionLeft = button
	button.NextSelectionRight = button
	button.NextSelectionUp = button
	button.NextSelectionDown = button
	button.ZIndex = 9
	buttonText.ZIndex = 9
	button.Parent = alertBacking

	if usesSelectedObject() then
		setSelectedObject(button)
	end
	bindAction(removeActionId, function(_, inputState)
		if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			-- Let the same button path perform cleanup consistently.
			button:Activate()
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end, false, Enum.KeyCode.Escape, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA)

	vrConnection = VRService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled"):Connect(updateParentForVR)
	if settingsHub and not VRService.VREnabled then
		if type(settingsHub.HideBar) == "function" then pcall(function() settingsHub:HideBar() end) end
		local pages = settingsHub.Pages
		if pages and pages.CurrentPage and type(pages.CurrentPage.Hide) == "function" then
			pcall(function() pages.CurrentPage:Hide(1, 1) end)
		end
	end
end

------------------ SLIDER --------------------
local function CreateNewSlider(numOfSteps, startStep, minStep)
	local steps = math.floor(tonumber(numOfSteps) or 0)
	if steps &lt;= 0 then
		error("CreateNewSlider expects numOfSteps to be a positive integer", 2)
	end

	local this = {}
	local spacing = 4
	local currentStep = clamp(0, steps, tonumber(startStep) or 0)
	local minimumStep = clamp(0, steps, tonumber(minStep) or 0)
	currentStep = math.max(currentStep, minimumStep)
	local lastInputDirection = 0
	local timeAtLastInput
	local interactable = true
	local isInTree = true
	local isDraggingPointer = false
	local pointerInputType = nil
	local isBound = false
	local renderStepBindName = "LegacyUtilitySlider_" .. HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
	local valueChangedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	valueChangedEvent.Name = "ValueChanged"
	this.ValueChanged = valueChangedEvent.Event

	this.SliderFrame = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
		Name = "Slider",
		Image = "",
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 50),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		SelectionImageObject = noSelectionObject,
		ZIndex = 2,
	})

	this.StepsContainer = Util.Create("Frame")({
		Name = "StepsContainer",
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0),
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Parent = this.SliderFrame,
	})

	local leftButton = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
		Name = "LeftButton", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50), Image = "", ZIndex = 3,
		Selectable = false, SelectionImageObject = noSelectionObject, Active = true,
		Parent = this.SliderFrame,
	})
	local rightButton = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
		Name = "RightButton", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50), Image = "", ZIndex = 3,
		Selectable = false, SelectionImageObject = noSelectionObject, Active = true,
		Parent = this.SliderFrame,
	})
	local leftButtonImage = Util.Create("ImageLabel")({
		Name = "LeftButtonImage", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30), Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/Less.png",
		ImageColor3 = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and ARROW_COLOR_TOUCH or ARROW_COLOR,
		ZIndex = 4, Parent = leftButton,
	})
	local rightButtonImage = Util.Create("ImageLabel")({
		Name = "RightButtonImage", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30), Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/More.png",
		ImageColor3 = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and ARROW_COLOR_TOUCH or ARROW_COLOR,
		ZIndex = 4, Parent = rightButton,
	})

	if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		addHoverState(leftButton, leftButtonImage,
			function(instance) instance.ImageColor3 = ARROW_COLOR end,
			function(instance) instance.ImageColor3 = ARROW_COLOR_HOVER end)
		addHoverState(rightButton, rightButtonImage,
			function(instance) instance.ImageColor3 = ARROW_COLOR end,
			function(instance) instance.ImageColor3 = ARROW_COLOR_HOVER end)
	end

	this.Steps = {}
	local stepXScale = 1 / steps
	for index = 1, steps do
		local step = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
			Name = "Step" .. tostring(index),
			BackgroundColor3 = index &lt;= currentStep and SELECTED_COLOR or NON_SELECTED_COLOR,
			BackgroundTransparency = 0.36,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			AutoButtonColor = false,
			Active = false,
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.new((index - 1) * stepXScale, spacing / 2, 0.5, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(stepXScale, -spacing, 24 / 50, 0),
			Image = "",
			ZIndex = 3,
			Selectable = false,
			ImageTransparency = 0.36,
			Parent = this.StepsContainer,
			SelectionImageObject = noSelectionObject,
		})
		if index == 1 or index == steps then
			step.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			step.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
			step.SliceCenter = Rect.new(3, 3, 32, 21)
			if index == 1 then
				step.Image = index &lt;= currentStep and SELECTED_LEFT_IMAGE or NON_SELECTED_LEFT_IMAGE
			else
				step.Image = index &lt;= currentStep and SELECTED_RIGHT_IMAGE or NON_SELECTED_RIGHT_IMAGE
			end
		end
		this.Steps[index] = step
	end

	local function drawValue()
		for index, step in ipairs(this.Steps) do
			local selected = index &lt;= currentStep
			step.BackgroundColor3 = selected and SELECTED_COLOR or NON_SELECTED_COLOR
			if index == 1 then
				step.Image = selected and SELECTED_LEFT_IMAGE or NON_SELECTED_LEFT_IMAGE
			elseif index == steps then
				step.Image = selected and SELECTED_RIGHT_IMAGE or NON_SELECTED_RIGHT_IMAGE
			end
		end
		leftButton.Visible = currentStep &gt; minimumStep
		rightButton.Visible = currentStep &lt; steps
	end

	local function modifySelection(alpha)
		for index, step in ipairs(this.Steps) do
			if index == 1 or index == steps then
				step.ImageTransparency = alpha
			else
				step.BackgroundTransparency = alpha
			end
		end
	end

	local function setCurrentStep(newValue)
		newValue = tonumber(newValue)
		if not newValue then return end
		newValue = clamp(minimumStep, steps, math.floor(newValue + 0.5))
		if currentStep == newValue then
			drawValue()
			return
		end
		currentStep = newValue
		drawValue()
		timeAtLastInput = os.clock()
		valueChangedEvent:Fire(currentStep)
	end

	local function isActivateEvent(input)
		return input and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA))
	end

	local function mouseDownFunc(input, newValue)
		if not interactable or not isActivateEvent(input) then return end
		if usesSelectedObject() and not VRService.VREnabled then
			setSelectedObject(this.SliderFrame)
		end
		setCurrentStep(newValue)
	end

	function this:SetValue(value)
		setCurrentStep(value)
	end
	function this:GetValue()
		return currentStep
	end
	function this:SetInteractable(value)
		interactable = value == true
		lastInputDirection = 0
		isDraggingPointer = false
		pointerInputType = nil
		this.SliderFrame.Active = interactable
		this.SliderFrame.Selectable = interactable
		this.StepsContainer.Active = interactable
		leftButton.Active = interactable
		rightButton.Active = interactable
		for _, step in ipairs(this.Steps) do
			step.Active = interactable
			step.Selectable = VRService.VREnabled and interactable
		end
		if interactable then
			drawValue()
		else
			modifySelection(0.36)
		end
	end
	function this:SetZIndex(newZIndex)
		leftButton.ZIndex = newZIndex
		rightButton.ZIndex = newZIndex
		leftButtonImage.ZIndex = newZIndex + 1
		rightButtonImage.ZIndex = newZIndex + 1
		for _, step in ipairs(this.Steps) do step.ZIndex = newZIndex end
	end
	function this:SetMinStep(newMinStep)
		newMinStep = tonumber(newMinStep)
		if newMinStep and newMinStep &gt;= 0 and newMinStep &lt;= steps then
			minimumStep = math.floor(newMinStep)
			setCurrentStep(math.max(currentStep, minimumStep))
		end
	end

	leftButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then setCurrentStep(currentStep - 1) end
	end)
	rightButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then setCurrentStep(currentStep + 1) end
	end)
	leftButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			mouseDownFunc(input, currentStep - 1)
		end
	end)
	rightButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			mouseDownFunc(input, currentStep + 1)
		end
	end)
	local function setStepFromPointerX(screenX)
		if not interactable then return end
		local track = this.StepsContainer
		local width = track.AbsoluteSize.X
		if width &lt;= 0 then return end
		local fraction = math.clamp(
			(screenX - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / width,
			0,
			0.999999
		)
		setCurrentStep(math.floor(fraction * steps) + 1)
	end

	local function beginPointerDrag(input, stepIndex)
		if not interactable then return end
		local inputType = input.UserInputType
		if inputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			and inputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			return
		end
		isDraggingPointer = true
		pointerInputType = inputType
		if stepIndex then
			setCurrentStep(stepIndex)
		else
			setStepFromPointerX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end

	for index, step in ipairs(this.Steps) do
		local stepIndex = index
		step.Active = true
		step.Activated:Connect(function()
			if interactable then setCurrentStep(stepIndex) end
		end)
		step.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			beginPointerDrag(input, stepIndex)
		end)
	end

	-- Permit clicking on the gaps between the segmented steps and dragging
	-- along the scale, rather than requiring one exact step button click.
	this.StepsContainer.Active = true
	this.StepsContainer.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		beginPointerDrag(input, nil)
	end)

	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not isDraggingPointer or not interactable then return end
		if pointerInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
			setStepFromPointerX(input.Position.X)
		elseif pointerInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			setStepFromPointerX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)

	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if not isDraggingPointer then return end
		if (pointerInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
			or (pointerInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
				and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			isDraggingPointer = false
			pointerInputType = nil
		end
	end)

	local function updateVRSelection()
		local vrEnabled = VRService.VREnabled
		leftButton.Selectable = vrEnabled and interactable
		rightButton.Selectable = vrEnabled and interactable
		this.SliderFrame.Active = interactable
		this.SliderFrame.Selectable = interactable
		this.StepsContainer.Active = interactable
		for _, step in ipairs(this.Steps) do
			-- Selectable is mode-specific, but Active must stay enabled for
			-- mouse/touch Activated events on desktop and mobile.
			step.Selectable = vrEnabled and interactable
			step.Active = interactable
		end
	end
	VRService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled"):Connect(updateVRSelection)
	updateVRSelection()

	local function handleNavigation(input, isBegin)
		if not interactable or not isInTree then return end
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
		local selected = getSelectedObject()
		local parent = this.SliderFrame.Parent
		if not selected or not parent or not selected:IsDescendantOf(parent) then return end
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Left or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A then
			lastInputDirection = isBegin and -1 or 0
			if isBegin then setCurrentStep(currentStep - 1) end
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Right or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D then
			lastInputDirection = isBegin and 1 or 0
			if isBegin then setCurrentStep(currentStep + 1) end
		end
	end
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) handleNavigation(input, true) end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input) handleNavigation(input, false) end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not interactable or not isInTree then lastInputDirection = 0 return end
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 or input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1 then return end
		local selected = getSelectedObject()
		local parent = this.SliderFrame.Parent
		if not selected or not parent or not selected:IsDescendantOf(parent) then return end
		if input.Position.X &gt; CONTROLLER_THUMBSTICK_DEADZONE and input.Delta.X &gt; 0 and lastInputDirection ~= 1 then
			lastInputDirection = 1
			setCurrentStep(currentStep + 1)
		elseif input.Position.X &lt; -CONTROLLER_THUMBSTICK_DEADZONE and input.Delta.X &lt; 0 and lastInputDirection ~= -1 then
			lastInputDirection = -1
			setCurrentStep(currentStep - 1)
		elseif math.abs(input.Position.X) &lt; CONTROLLER_THUMBSTICK_DEADZONE then
			lastInputDirection = 0
		end
	end)

	local function stepSliderFunc()
		if not interactable or not timeAtLastInput or lastInputDirection == 0 then return end
		if os.clock() - timeAtLastInput &gt;= CONTROLLER_SCROLL_DELTA then
			setCurrentStep(currentStep + lastInputDirection)
		end
	end
	GuiService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SelectedObject"):Connect(function()
		local selected = getSelectedObject()
		local parent = this.SliderFrame.Parent
		local isThisSelected = selected and parent and selected:IsDescendantOf(parent)
		if isThisSelected then
			modifySelection(0)
			if not isBound then
				isBound = true
				timeAtLastInput = os.clock()
				RunService:BindToRenderStep(renderStepBindName, Enum.RenderPriority.Input.Value + 1, stepSliderFunc)
			end
		else
			modifySelection(0.36)
			if isBound then
				isBound = false
				RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(renderStepBindName)
			end
		end
	end)
	this.SliderFrame.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
		isInTree = parent ~= nil
		if not isInTree and isBound then
			isBound = false
			RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(renderStepBindName)
		end
	end)

	drawValue()
	return this
end

------------------ ROWS --------------------
local ROW_HEIGHT = isTenFootInterface() and 90 or 50
local nextPosTable = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function addPageRow(pageToAddTo, rowFrame, rowLabel, valueChanger, extraSpacing, isObject)
	if pageToAddTo and type(pageToAddTo.AddRow) == "function" then
		pageToAddTo:AddRow(rowFrame, rowLabel, valueChanger, extraSpacing, isObject)
	end
end

local function AddNewRow(pageToAddTo, rowDisplayName, selectionType, rowValues, rowDefault, extraSpacing)
	assert(pageToAddTo and pageToAddTo.Page, "AddNewRow expects a page with a Page GUI object")
	local nextRowPositionY = nextPosTable[pageToAddTo] or 0
	local isARealRow = selectionType ~= "TextBox"

	local rowFrame = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
		Name = rowDisplayName .. "Frame",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/VR/rectBackgroundWhite.png",
		ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice,
		SliceCenter = Rect.new(2, 2, 18, 18),
		ImageTransparency = 1,
		Active = false,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ROW_HEIGHT),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, nextRowPositionY + (extraSpacing or 0)),
		ZIndex = 2,
		Selectable = false,
		SelectionImageObject = noSelectionObject,
		Parent = pageToAddTo.Page,
	})
	rowFrame.ImageColor3 = rowFrame.BackgroundColor3

	local rowLabel = Util.Create("TextLabel")({
		Name = rowDisplayName .. "Label",
		Text = isARealRow and rowDisplayName or "",
		Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
		TextSize = 16,
		TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0),
		ZIndex = 2,
		Parent = rowFrame,
	})
	local rowLabelConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
	rowLabelConstraint.Parent = rowLabel
	if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
		rowLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.35, 0, 1, 0)
		rowLabel.TextScaled = true
		rowLabel.TextWrapped = true
		rowLabelConstraint.MaxTextSize = 16
	end

	local function onResized(viewportSize, portrait)
		rowLabel.TextSize = portrait and 16 or (isTenFootInterface() and 36 or 24)
		rowLabelConstraint.MaxTextSize = rowLabel.TextSize
	end
	addOnResizedCallback(rowFrame, onResized)

	local valueChanger
	local valueChangerSelection
	if selectionType == "Slider" then
		valueChanger = CreateNewSlider(rowValues, rowDefault)
		valueChanger.SliderFrame.Parent = rowFrame
		valueChangerSelection = valueChanger.SliderFrame
	elseif selectionType == "Selector" then
		valueChanger = CreateSelector(rowValues, rowDefault)
		valueChanger.SelectorFrame.Parent = rowFrame
		valueChangerSelection = valueChanger.SelectorFrame
	elseif selectionType == "DropDown" then
		valueChanger = CreateDropDown(rowValues, rowDefault, pageToAddTo.HubRef)
		valueChanger.DropDownFrame.Parent = rowFrame
		valueChangerSelection = valueChanger.DropDownFrame
	elseif selectionType == "TextBox" or selectionType == "TextEntry" then
		local isTextEntry = selectionType == "TextEntry"
		local selectionOverride = Util.Create("ImageLabel")({ Image = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
		local box = Util.Create("TextBox")({
			Name = rowDisplayName .. "Input",
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
			Size = isTextEntry and UDim2.new(0.4, -10, 0, 40) or UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, 0),
			Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, extraSpacing or 0),
			Text = rowDisplayName,
			TextColor3 = isTextEntry and Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178) or Color3.fromRGB(49, 49, 49),
			BackgroundTransparency = isTextEntry and 1 or 0.5,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			TextYAlignment = isTextEntry and Enum.TextYAlignment.Center or Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
			TextXAlignment = isTextEntry and Enum.TextXAlignment.Center or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			TextWrapped = not isTextEntry,
			Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans,
			TextSize = 24,
			ZIndex = 2,
			SelectionImageObject = selectionOverride,
			ClearTextOnFocus = false,
			Parent = rowFrame,
		})
		valueChanger = { HubRef = nil }
		valueChangerSelection = box

		box.Focused:Connect(function()
			if usesSelectedObject() then setSelectedObject(box) end
			if box.Text == rowDisplayName then box.Text = "" end
			box.BackgroundTransparency = isTextEntry and 0.8 or 0.1
		end)
		box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
			box.BackgroundTransparency = isTextEntry and 1 or 0.5
			if valueChanger.ValueChangedEvent then
				valueChanger.ValueChangedEvent:Fire(box.Text)
			end
		end)
		box.SelectionGained:Connect(function()
			box.BackgroundTransparency = isTextEntry and 0.8 or 0.1
			if valueChanger.HubRef and type(valueChanger.HubRef.ScrollToFrame) == "function" then
				pcall(function() valueChanger.HubRef:ScrollToFrame(box) end)
			end
		end)
		box.SelectionLost:Connect(function()
			box.BackgroundTransparency = isTextEntry and 1 or 0.5
		end)
		local valueChangedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
		valueChangedEvent.Name = "ValueChanged"
		valueChanger.ValueChangedEvent = valueChangedEvent
		valueChanger.ValueChanged = valueChangedEvent.Event
		function valueChanger:SetZIndex(newZIndex) box.ZIndex = newZIndex end
		function valueChanger:SetInteractable(enabled)
			box.Selectable = enabled == true
			box.TextColor3 = enabled and (isTextEntry and Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178) or Color3.fromRGB(49, 49, 49)) or Color3.fromRGB(49, 49, 49)
			box.ZIndex = enabled and 2 or 1
		end
		function valueChanger:SetValue(value) box.Text = tostring(value or "") end
		function valueChanger:GetValue() return box.Text end
	else
		error("Unsupported row selection type: " .. tostring(selectionType), 2)
	end

	valueChanger.Name = rowDisplayName .. "ValueChanger"
	valueChanger.Selection = valueChangerSelection

	local function selectRow()
		local dropdown = RobloxGui and RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("DropDownFullscreenFrame")
		if dropdown and dropdown.Visible then return end
		if valueChangerSelection and valueChangerSelection.Visible and valueChangerSelection.ZIndex &gt; 1
			and usesSelectedObject() and pageToAddTo.Active then
			setSelectedObject(valueChangerSelection)
		end
	end
	rowFrame.MouseEnter:Connect(selectRow)
	if valueChangerSelection and valueChangerSelection:IsA("GuiObject") then
		valueChangerSelection.SelectionGained:Connect(function()
			if usesSelectedObject() then
				rowFrame.ImageTransparency = VRService.VREnabled and 0.5 or 1
				rowFrame.BackgroundTransparency = VRService.VREnabled and 1 or 0.5
			end
			if valueChanger.HubRef and type(valueChanger.HubRef.ScrollToFrame) == "function" then
				pcall(function() valueChanger.HubRef:ScrollToFrame(rowFrame) end)
			end
		end)
		valueChangerSelection.SelectionLost:Connect(function()
			rowFrame.ImageTransparency = 1
			rowFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		end)
	end

	nextPosTable[pageToAddTo] = nextRowPositionY + ROW_HEIGHT + (extraSpacing or 0)
	addPageRow(pageToAddTo, rowFrame, rowLabel, valueChanger, extraSpacing, false)
	return rowFrame, rowLabel, valueChanger
end

local function AddNewRowObject(pageToAddTo, rowDisplayName, rowObject, extraSpacing)
	assert(pageToAddTo and pageToAddTo.Page, "AddNewRowObject expects a page with a Page GUI object")
	assert(typeof(rowObject) == "Instance" and rowObject:IsA("GuiObject"), "rowObject must be a GuiObject")
	local nextRowPositionY = nextPosTable[pageToAddTo] or 0

	local rowFrame = Util.Create("ImageButton")({
		Name = rowDisplayName .. "Frame",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/VR/rectBackgroundWhite.png",
		ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice,
		SliceCenter = Rect.new(10, 10, 10, 10),
		ImageTransparency = 1,
		Active = false,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ROW_HEIGHT),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, nextRowPositionY + (extraSpacing or 0)),
		ZIndex = 2,
		Selectable = false,
		SelectionImageObject = noSelectionObject,
		Parent = pageToAddTo.Page,
	})
	rowFrame.ImageColor3 = rowFrame.BackgroundColor3
	local rowLabel = Util.Create("TextLabel")({
		Name = rowDisplayName .. "Label",
		Text = rowDisplayName,
		Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
		TextSize = 16,
		TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0),
		ZIndex = 2,
		Parent = rowFrame,
	})
	addOnResizedCallback(rowFrame, function(_, portrait)
		rowLabel.TextSize = portrait and 16 or (isTenFootInterface() and 36 or 24)
	end)

	rowFrame.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if rowFrame.Visible and usesSelectedObject() then setSelectedObject(rowFrame) end
	end)
	rowFrame.SelectionGained:Connect(function()
		rowFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
	end)
	rowFrame.SelectionLost:Connect(function()
		rowFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	end)
	rowObject.SelectionImageObject = noSelectionObject
	rowObject.SelectionGained:Connect(function()
		rowFrame.ImageTransparency = VRService.VREnabled and 0.5 or 1
		rowFrame.BackgroundTransparency = VRService.VREnabled and 1 or 0.5
	end)
	rowObject.SelectionLost:Connect(function()
		rowFrame.ImageTransparency = 1
		rowFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	end)
	rowObject.Parent = rowFrame

	nextPosTable[pageToAddTo] = nextRowPositionY + ROW_HEIGHT + (extraSpacing or 0)
	addPageRow(pageToAddTo, rowFrame, rowLabel, rowObject, extraSpacing, true)
	return rowFrame
end

------------------ PUBLIC API --------------------
local moduleApiTable = {}

function moduleApiTable:Create(instanceType)
	return Util.Create(instanceType)
end

function moduleApiTable:RayPlaneIntersection(ray, planeNormal, pointOnPlane)
	if not ray or not planeNormal or not pointOnPlane then return nil end
	local direction = ray.Direction
	if direction.Magnitude == 0 then return nil end
	local normal = planeNormal.Unit
	direction = direction.Unit
	local denominator = normal:Dot(direction)
	if math.abs(denominator) &lt; 1e-8 then
		return nil
	end
	local distance = normal:Dot(pointOnPlane - ray.Origin) / denominator
	if distance &lt; 0 then
		return nil
	end
	return ray.Origin + direction * distance
end

function moduleApiTable:GetEaseLinear() return Linear end
function moduleApiTable:GetEaseOutQuad() return EaseOutQuad end
function moduleApiTable:GetEaseInOutQuad() return EaseInOutQuad end
function moduleApiTable:CreateNewSlider(numOfSteps, startStep, minStep)
	return CreateNewSlider(numOfSteps, startStep, minStep)
end
function moduleApiTable:CreateNewSelector(selectionStringTable, startPosition)
	return CreateSelector(selectionStringTable, startPosition)
end
function moduleApiTable:CreateNewDropDown(dropDownStringTable, startPosition)
	return CreateDropDown(dropDownStringTable, startPosition, nil)
end
function moduleApiTable:AddNewRow(pageToAddTo, rowDisplayName, selectionType, rowValues, rowDefault, extraSpacing)
	return AddNewRow(pageToAddTo, rowDisplayName, selectionType, rowValues, rowDefault, extraSpacing)
end
function moduleApiTable:AddNewRowObject(pageToAddTo, rowDisplayName, rowObject, extraSpacing)
	return AddNewRowObject(pageToAddTo, rowDisplayName, rowObject, extraSpacing)
end
function moduleApiTable:ShowAlert(alertMessage, okButtonText, settingsHub, okPressedFunc, hasBackground)
	return ShowAlert(alertMessage, okButtonText, settingsHub, okPressedFunc, hasBackground)
end
function moduleApiTable:IsSmallTouchScreen() return isSmallTouchScreen() end
function moduleApiTable:IsPortrait() return isPortrait() end
function moduleApiTable:MakeStyledButton(name, text, size, clickFunc, pageRef, hubRef)
	return MakeButton(name, text, size, clickFunc, pageRef, hubRef)
end
function moduleApiTable:MakeStyledImageButton(name, image, size, imageSize, clickFunc, pageRef, hubRef)
	return MakeImageButton(name, image, size, imageSize, clickFunc, pageRef, hubRef)
end
function moduleApiTable:AddButtonRow(pageToAddTo, name, text, size, clickFunc, hubRef)
	return AddButtonRow(pageToAddTo, name, text, size, clickFunc, hubRef)
end
function moduleApiTable:CreateSignal() return CreateSignal() end
function moduleApiTable:UsesSelectedObject() return usesSelectedObject() end
function moduleApiTable:TweenProperty(instance, property, startValue, finalValue, duration, easingFunc, callback)
	return PropertyTweener(instance, property, startValue, finalValue, duration, easingFunc, callback)
end
function moduleApiTable:OnResized(key, callback)
	assert(key ~= nil and type(callback) == "function", "OnResized expects a key and callback")
	return addOnResizedCallback(key, callback)
end
function moduleApiTable:FireOnResized()
	fireResizeCallbacks()
end

return moduleApiTable

-- Full-source repair: Players tab retained; Players tab uses Hamburger icon; confirmation canvases are viewport-sized and clipper geometry is header/footer-aware.
    Filename: SettingsHub.lua
    Compatibility rewrite for the Yeku legacy CoreGui runtime.

    Preserves the existing NewRobloxGui tree and legacy page modules.
    Confirmation pages are hidden whenever they are no longer current.
    Delayed Hide/Display operations cannot leave Leave or Reset visible.
    L and R open Leave/Reset independently of bottom-bar visibility.

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local VRService = game:GetService("VRService")
local ContentProvider = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

local function getServiceSafely(name)
	local ok, service = pcall(function() return game:GetService(name) end)
	return ok and service or nil
end

local RobloxReplicatedStorage = getServiceSafely("RobloxReplicatedStorage")
local CorePackages = getServiceSafely("CorePackages")

local function findGuiRoot()
	local node = script
	while node do
		if node.Name == "NewRobloxGui" or node.Name == "RobloxGui" then return node end
		node = node.Parent
	end
	return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
		or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
		or CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 10)
		or CoreGui:WaitForChild("RobloxGui", 5)
end

local RobloxGui = findGuiRoot()
assert(RobloxGui, "SettingsHub: could not find the existing CoreGui.NewRobloxGui root")

local function findPath(root, path)
	local node = root
	for _, childName in ipairs(path) do
		if not node then return nil end
		node = node:FindFirstChild(childName)
	end
	return node
end

local function requireSafely(moduleInstance, label)
	if not moduleInstance or not moduleInstance:IsA("ModuleScript") then return nil end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleInstance)
	if not ok then
		warn("[SettingsHub] Could not require " .. tostring(label or moduleInstance:GetFullName()) .. ": " .. tostring(result))
		return nil
	end
	return result
end

local function fireSignal(signalObject, ...)
	if not signalObject then return false end
	local ok, fireMethod = pcall(function() return signalObject.fire or signalObject.Fire end)
	if not ok or type(fireMethod) ~= "function" then
		warn("[SettingsHub] SettingsShowSignal has no fire/Fire method")
		return false
	end
	local fired, err = pcall(fireMethod, signalObject, ...)
	if not fired then warn("[SettingsHub] Could not fire SettingsShowSignal: " .. tostring(err)) end
	return fired
end

local Modules = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("Modules")
assert(Modules, "SettingsHub: NewRobloxGui is missing its existing Modules folder")
local SettingsModules = Modules:FindFirstChild("Settings")
local UtilityModule = (SettingsModules and SettingsModules:FindFirstChild("Utility"))
	or Modules:FindFirstChild("Utility")
	or (script.Parent and script.Parent:FindFirstChild("Utility"))
local utility = requireSafely(UtilityModule, "Settings.Utility")
assert(utility, "SettingsHub: unable to load the existing Utility ModuleScript")

local function getFlag(name, defaultValue)
	local ok, settingsObject = pcall(function() return settings() end)
	if not ok or not settingsObject then return defaultValue end
	local success, value = pcall(function() return settingsObject:GetFFlag(name) end)
	if success and type(value) == "boolean" then return value end
	return defaultValue
end

local function getPlatform()
	local ok, platformValue = pcall(function() return UserInputService:GetPlatform() end)
	return ok and platformValue or nil
end

local platform = getPlatform()
local isTouchDevice = UserInputService.TouchEnabled
local isMobilePlatform = platform == Enum.Platform.IOS or platform == Enum.Platform.Android

local function getTenFootInterfaceEnabled()
	local module = Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface")
	local tenFoot = requireSafely(module, "TenFootInterface")
	if tenFoot and type(tenFoot.IsEnabled) == "function" then
		local ok, value = pcall(tenFoot.IsEnabled, tenFoot)
		if ok then return value == true end
	end
	return false
end

local isTenFootInterface = getTenFootInterfaceEnabled()
local FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization = getFlag("UseNotificationsLocalization", false)
local FFlagSettingsHubBarsRefactor = getFlag("SettingsHubBarsRefactor4", false)
local FFlagEnableNewDevConsole = getFlag("EnableNewDevConsole", false)
local FFlagHelpMenuShowPlaceVersion = getFlag("HelpMenuShowPlaceVersion", false)
local FFlagSettingsHubPlayersHorizontalScroll = getFlag("SettingsHubPlayersHorizontalScroll", false)
local FFlagEnableResponsiveUIFix = getFlag("EnableResponsiveUIFix", false)

local CONTENT_BASE_URL = ""
pcall(function() CONTENT_BASE_URL = ContentProvider.BaseUrl end)
local isTestEnvironment = CONTENT_BASE_URL ~= "" and not string.find(CONTENT_BASE_URL, "www.roblox.com", 1, true)

local function loadModule(path, fallbackPath)
	local instance = findPath(RobloxGui, path)
	if not instance and fallbackPath then instance = findPath(RobloxGui, fallbackPath) end
	return requireSafely(instance, table.concat(path, "."))
end

local VRHub = loadModule({"Modules", "VR", "VRHub"})
local DeveloperConsoleModule = loadModule({"Modules", "DeveloperConsoleModule"})
local DevConsoleMaster = loadModule({"Modules", "DevConsoleMaster"})
local ChatSelector = loadModule({"Modules", "ChatSelector"})
-- PlayerList is an optional peer, not a prerequisite for the Settings menu.
-- Requiring it here synchronously made the menu wait for the entire roster,
-- dropdown, friendship and follow initialization path. Topbar also loads it
-- independently, so require it in the background and attach when ready.
local PlayerlistModule = nil
local SettingsHubInstance = nil
local BackpackScript = loadModule({"Modules", "BackpackScript"})
local FlagSettings = loadModule({"Modules", "Settings", "Pages", "ShareGame", "FlagSettings"})

local shareGameEnabled = false
if FlagSettings and type(FlagSettings.IsShareGamePageEnabledByPlatform) == "function" then
	local ok, enabled = pcall(FlagSettings.IsShareGamePageEnabledByPlatform, platform)
	shareGameEnabled = ok and enabled == true
end

local function tryMethod(object, methodName, ...)
	if not object then return false, "missing object" end
	local okMethod, method = pcall(function() return object[methodName] end)
	if not okMethod or type(method) ~= "function" then return false, "missing method " .. tostring(methodName) end
	return pcall(method, object, ...)
end

-- Start PlayerList independently. SettingsHub is already free to finish building.
task.spawn(function()
	local loaded = loadModule({"Modules", "PlayerlistModule"})
	if not loaded then return end
	PlayerlistModule = loaded
	-- If the menu was opened while PlayerList initialized, apply the temporary
	-- hide now; otherwise normal menu open/close callbacks will control it.
	if SettingsHubInstance and SettingsHubInstance.Visible then
		tryMethod(PlayerlistModule, "HideTemp", "SettingsMenu", true)
	end
end)

local function getProperty(object, property, defaultValue)
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return object[property] end)
	if ok then return value end
	return defaultValue
end

local function getCurrentViewport()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		local ok, viewport = pcall(function() return camera.ViewportSize end)
		if ok and viewport and viewport.X &gt; 1 and viewport.Y &gt; 1 then return viewport end
	end
	return Vector2.new(1280, 720)
end

local function getRootSize()
	local size = getProperty(RobloxGui, "AbsoluteSize", nil)
	if size and size.X &gt; 1 and size.Y &gt; 1 then return size end
	return getCurrentViewport()
end

local function setSelectedObject(object)
	pcall(function() GuiService.SelectedObject = object end)
end
local function getSelectedObject()
	return getProperty(GuiService, "SelectedObject", nil)
end
local function getLastInputType()
	local ok, inputType = pcall(function() return UserInputService:GetLastInputType() end)
	return ok and inputType or Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
end
local function setMouseIconBehavior(behaviorName)
	pcall(function()
		local behaviorEnum = Enum.OverrideMouseIconBehavior
		if behaviorEnum then UserInputService.OverrideMouseIconBehavior = behaviorEnum[behaviorName] end
	end)
end

local function bindAction(actionName, callback, createTouchButton, ...)
	pcall(function() ContextActionService:UnbindAction(actionName) end)
	local inputs = {...}
	local priority = Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(actionName, callback, createTouchButton == true, priority, table.unpack(inputs))
	end)
	if not ok then warn("[SettingsHub] Could not bind action " .. tostring(actionName) .. ": " .. tostring(err)) end
	return ok
end
local function unbindAction(actionName)
	pcall(function() ContextActionService:UnbindAction(actionName) end)
end

local function getServerVersion()
	if not RobloxReplicatedStorage then return "unavailable" end
	local remote = RobloxReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GetServerVersion")
	if not remote then
		local ok, found = pcall(function() return RobloxReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GetServerVersion", 3) end)
		if ok then remote = found end
	end
	if not remote or not remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") then return "unavailable" end
	local ok, result = pcall(function() return remote:InvokeServer() end)
	if ok and result ~= nil then return tostring(result) end
	return "unavailable"
end
local function getClientVersion()
	local ok, result = pcall(function() return version() end)
	if ok and result ~= nil then return tostring(result) end
	return "current client"
end
local function getPlaceVersionText()
	local ok, placeVersion = pcall(function() return game.PlaceVersion end)
	return ok and tostring(placeVersion) or "unavailable"
end

local function isXboxPlatform()
	return tostring(platform):find("XBox", 1, true) ~= nil
end
local function createPage(path)
	return loadModule(path)
end

local function createLocalSignal(signalName)
	local listeners = {}
	local signal = {}
	function signal:Connect(callback)
		assert(type(callback) == "function", "Signal callback must be a function")
		local entry = {Callback = callback, Connected = true}
		local connection = {}
		function connection:Disconnect() entry.Connected = false end
		connection.disconnect = connection.Disconnect
		table.insert(listeners, entry)
		return connection
	end
	signal.connect = signal.Connect
	function signal:Fire(...)
		local args = table.pack(...)
		for _, entry in ipairs(listeners) do
			if entry.Connected then
				local ok, err = pcall(entry.Callback, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
				if not ok then warn("[SettingsHub] " .. tostring(signalName) .. " callback failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
			end
		end
	end
	signal.fire = signal.Fire
	return signal
end

local function createSettingsHub()
	local this = {
		Visible = false,
		Active = true,
		Pages = {CurrentPage = nil, PageTable = {}},
		MenuStack = {},
		TabHeaders = {},
		BottomBarButtons = {},
		ResizedConnection = nil,
		TabConnection = nil,
		SettingsShowSignal = utility:CreateSignal(),
		OpenStateChangedCount = 0,
	}

	local PoppedMenuEvent = createLocalSignal("SettingsHub.PoppedMenu")
	this.PoppedMenu = PoppedMenuEvent
	this.LeaveGamePage = createPage({"Modules", "Settings", "Pages", "LeaveGame"})
	this.ResetCharacterPage = createPage({"Modules", "Settings", "Pages", "ResetCharacter"})
	this.GameSettingsPage = createPage({"Modules", "Settings", "Pages", "GameSettings"})
	this.ReportAbusePage = createPage({"Modules", "Settings", "Pages", "ReportAbuseMenu"})
	this.HelpPage = createPage({"Modules", "Settings", "Pages", "Help"})
	this.PlayersPage = createPage({"Modules", "Settings", "Pages", "Players"})
	this.RecordPage = nil
	if tostring(platform):find("Windows", 1, true) then
		this.RecordPage = createPage({"Modules", "Settings", "Pages", "Record"})
	end
	this.HomePage = createPage({"Modules", "Settings", "Pages", "Home"})

	local pageChangeConnection
	local viewportConnection
	local cameraChangedConnection
	local onScreenSizeChanged
	local updateCanvasForPage
	local clipperRefreshQueued = false
	local updatePageClipperLayout
	local function queuePageClipperRefresh()
		if clipperRefreshQueued then return end
		clipperRefreshQueued = true
		task.defer(function()
			clipperRefreshQueued = false
			if not this.MenuContainer or not this.MenuContainer.Parent then return end
			updatePageClipperLayout()
			if updateCanvasForPage then updateCanvasForPage(this.Pages.CurrentPage) end
		end)
	end
	local bottomBarBound = false
	local resetEnabled = true
	local GetHeaderPosition
	local setVisibilityInternal

	local function isPhoneTouchScreen()
		if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then return false end
		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or RobloxGui.AbsoluteSize
		if not viewport or viewport.X &lt;= 0 or viewport.Y &lt;= 0 then
			local ok, small = pcall(function() return utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() end)
			return ok and small == true
		end
		-- Use the shorter side so a phone stays a phone in landscape; tablets
		-- keep the full Settings footer in either orientation.
		return math.min(viewport.X, viewport.Y) &lt;= 640
	end

	local function isConfirmationPage(page)
		return page ~= nil and (page == this.LeaveGamePage or page == this.ResetCharacterPage)
	end
	local function forceHideConfirmationPage(page)
		if not isConfirmationPage(page) then return end
		local pageGui = page.Page
		if pageGui and pageGui:IsA("GuiObject") then
			pcall(function() pageGui.Visible = false; pageGui.Active = false end)
		end
	end
	local function forceHideInactiveConfirmationPages(currentPage)
		for _, confirmationPage in ipairs({this.LeaveGamePage, this.ResetCharacterPage}) do
			if confirmationPage and confirmationPage ~= currentPage then forceHideConfirmationPage(confirmationPage) end
		end
	end
	local function pageNeedsBottomBar(page)
		if not page or isConfirmationPage(page) then return false end
		if FFlagSettingsHubBarsRefactor then return page.ShouldShowBottomBar == true end
		return true
	end
	local function shouldShowBottomBar(page)
		page = page or this.Pages.CurrentPage
		if not page or isConfirmationPage(page) then return false end
		if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then return not isConfirmationPage(page) end
		if utility:IsPortrait() or utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then return false end
		return pageNeedsBottomBar(page)
	end
	local function shouldShowHubBar(page)
		page = page or this.Pages.CurrentPage
		if not page then return true end
		if isConfirmationPage(page) then return false end
		if FFlagSettingsHubBarsRefactor then return page.ShouldShowBottomBar == true end
		return true
	end
	local function hideTempPlayerList(hide)
		if PlayerlistModule then tryMethod(PlayerlistModule, "HideTemp", "SettingsMenu", hide) end
	end
	local function getChatVisible()
		if ChatSelector then
			local ok, value = tryMethod(ChatSelector, "GetVisibility")
			if ok then return value == true end
		end
		return false
	end
	local function toggleChatVisibility()
		if ChatSelector then
			local ok = tryMethod(ChatSelector, "ToggleVisibility")
			if not ok then tryMethod(ChatSelector, "SetVisibility", not getChatVisible()) end
		end
	end
	local function setMenuOpenState(value)
		pcall(function() GuiService:SetMenuIsOpen(value) end)
	end

	local activeShieldTween = nil
	local function tweenShieldPosition(position, duration, easingDirection, easingStyle, onCompleted)
		local shield = this.Shield
		if not shield or not shield.Parent then
			if onCompleted then pcall(onCompleted) end
			return
		end
		if activeShieldTween then
			pcall(function() activeShieldTween:Cancel() end)
			activeShieldTween = nil
		end
		local ok, tween = pcall(function()
			return TweenService:Create(shield, TweenInfo.new(duration, easingStyle, easingDirection), {Position = position})
		end)
		if not ok or not tween then
			shield.Position = position
			if onCompleted then pcall(onCompleted) end
			return
		end
		activeShieldTween = tween
		if onCompleted then
			local connection
			connection = tween.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
				if connection then connection:Disconnect() end
				if activeShieldTween == tween then activeShieldTween = nil end
				if playbackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then pcall(onCompleted) end
			end)
		else
			tween.Completed:Connect(function()
				if activeShieldTween == tween then activeShieldTween = nil end
			end)
		end
		tween:Play()
	end

	local function addBottomBarButton(name, text, gamepadImage, keyboardImage, position, clickFunc, hotkeys)
		local buttonName = name .. "Button"
		local textName = name .. "Text"
		local size = isTenFootInterface and UDim2.new(0, 320, 0, 120) or UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 70)
		local button, textLabel = utility:MakeStyledButton(buttonName, text, size, clickFunc, nil, this)
		if not button or not textLabel then
			warn("[SettingsHub] Utility:MakeStyledButton did not return a button and label for " .. name)
			return
		end
		this[buttonName] = button
		this[textName] = textLabel
		button.Name = buttonName
		button.Position = position
		button.Parent = this.BottomButtonFrame
		if isTenFootInterface then pcall(function() button.ImageTransparency = 1 end) end
