		textLabel.TextSize = isTenFootInterface and 36 or 24
		textLabel.TextWrapped = true
		local hintLabel
		if isTouchDevice then
			-- Touch footer buttons have no key icon. Center their text in the
			-- actual button instead of inheriting the desktop's right-shifted slot.
			textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.92, 0, 0.9, 0)
			textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.04, 0, 0.05, 0)
			textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		else
			if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
				textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.74, 0, 0.67, 0)
				textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.23, 0, 0.125, 0)
			else
				textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.78, 0, 0.9, 0)
				textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.22, 0, 0, 0)
			end
			local useGamepadIcon = UserInputService:GetGamepadConnected(Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1) or isXboxPlatform()
			hintLabel = utility:Create("ImageLabel") {
				Name = name .. "Hint",
				ZIndex = this.Shield.ZIndex + 2,
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Image = useGamepadIcon and (gamepadImage or "") or (keyboardImage or ""),
				Parent = button,
				AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
				Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 44),
				Position = UDim2.new(0.12, 0, 0.475, 0),
			}
		end
		local function updateHint(inputObject)
			if not hintLabel or not inputObject then return end
			if tostring(inputObject.UserInputType):find("Gamepad", 1, true) then
				hintLabel.Image = gamepadImage or ""
			elseif inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
				hintLabel.Image = keyboardImage or ""
			end
		end
		UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(updateHint)
		local function hotKeyFunc(_, inputState)
			if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and this.Visible then clickFunc() end
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		end
		this.BottomBarButtons[#this.BottomBarButtons + 1] = {buttonName, {hotKeyFunc, hotkeys or {}}}
	end

	local function setBottomBarBindings()
		local shouldDisplay = this.Visible and shouldShowBottomBar(this.Pages.CurrentPage)
		if not shouldDisplay then
			if this.BottomButtonFrame then this.BottomButtonFrame.Visible = false end
			if bottomBarBound then
				for _, buttonInfo in ipairs(this.BottomBarButtons) do unbindAction(buttonInfo[1]) end
				bottomBarBound = false
			end
			return
		end
		if not bottomBarBound then
			for _, buttonInfo in ipairs(this.BottomBarButtons) do
				local actionName = buttonInfo[1]
				local hotkeyInfo = buttonInfo[2]
				if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
					bindAction(actionName, hotkeyInfo[1], false, table.unpack(hotkeyInfo[2]))
				end
			end
			bottomBarBound = true
		end
		if this.BottomButtonFrame then this.BottomButtonFrame.Visible = true end
	end
	local function removeBottomBarBindings(delayBeforeRemoving)
		for _, buttonInfo in ipairs(this.BottomBarButtons) do unbindAction(buttonInfo[1]) end
		bottomBarBound = false
		local openStateAtRequest = this.OpenStateChangedCount
		local function hideFrame()
			if this.OpenStateChangedCount == openStateAtRequest and this.BottomButtonFrame then
				this.BottomButtonFrame.Visible = false
			end
		end
		if delayBeforeRemoving and delayBeforeRemoving &gt; 0 then task.delay(delayBeforeRemoving, hideFrame) else hideFrame() end
	end
	local function enforcePageChrome(page)
		page = page or this.Pages.CurrentPage
		if this.HubBar then this.HubBar.Visible = shouldShowHubBar(page) end
		if this.PageViewClipper then this.PageViewClipper.Visible = true; this.PageViewClipper.ClipsDescendants = true end
		if this.BottomButtonFrame then this.BottomButtonFrame.Visible = this.Visible and shouldShowBottomBar(page) end
		if this.Visible and shouldShowBottomBar(page) then setBottomBarBindings() else removeBottomBarBindings() end
	end
	local function setResetEnabled(value)
		resetEnabled = value == true
		local button = this.ResetCharacterButton
		if not button then return end
		button.Selectable = resetEnabled
		button.Active = resetEnabled
		local enabledValue = button:FindFirstChild("Enabled")
		if enabledValue and enabledValue:IsA("BoolValue") then enabledValue.Value = resetEnabled end
		local hint = button:FindFirstChild("ResetCharacterHint")
		if hint then hint.ImageColor3 = resetEnabled and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100) end
		local label = button:FindFirstChild("ResetCharacterButtonTextLabel")
		if label and label:IsA("TextLabel") then label.TextColor3 = resetEnabled and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100) end
	end

	----------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- TAB HEADER LAYOUT
	----------------------------------------------------------------------
	local canonicalHeaderMetrics = nil
	local function findHeaderTextLabel(headerGui)
		local descendants = headerGui:GetDescendants()
		for _, descendant in ipairs(descendants) do
			if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") then
				local name = string.lower(descendant.Name)
				if string.find(name, "text", 1, true) or string.find(name, "label", 1, true) or string.find(name, "title", 1, true) then
					return descendant
				end
			end
		end
		for _, descendant in ipairs(descendants) do if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") then return descendant end end
		return nil
	end
	local function findHeaderIcon(headerGui)
		for _, descendant in ipairs(headerGui:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("ImageLabel") or descendant:IsA("ImageButton") then
				if string.find(string.lower(descendant.Name), "icon", 1, true) then return descendant end
			end
		end
		for _, descendant in ipairs(headerGui:GetDescendants()) do if descendant:IsA("ImageLabel") then return descendant end end
		return nil
	end
	local function captureHeaderPartMetrics(object, properties)
		if not object then return nil end
		local metrics = {}
		for _, propertyName in ipairs(properties) do
			local ok, value = pcall(function() return object[propertyName] end)
			if ok then metrics[propertyName] = value end
		end
		return metrics
	end
	local function applyHeaderPartMetrics(object, metrics)
		if not object or not metrics then return end
		for propertyName, value in pairs(metrics) do pcall(function() object[propertyName] = value end) end
	end
	local TEXT_HEADER_PROPERTIES = {"Position", "Size", "AnchorPoint", "TextSize", "Font", "TextXAlignment", "TextYAlignment", "TextWrapped", "TextScaled"}
	local ICON_HEADER_PROPERTIES = {"Position", "Size", "AnchorPoint"}
	local function alignTabHeaderMetrics(headerGui)
		if not headerGui or not headerGui:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
		local textLabel = findHeaderTextLabel(headerGui)
		local iconLabel = findHeaderIcon(headerGui)
		local directTextObject
		if not textLabel and (headerGui:IsA("TextButton") or headerGui:IsA("TextLabel")) then directTextObject = headerGui end
		local actualTextObject = textLabel or directTextObject
		if not canonicalHeaderMetrics then
			canonicalHeaderMetrics = {
				Text = captureHeaderPartMetrics(actualTextObject, TEXT_HEADER_PROPERTIES),
				Icon = captureHeaderPartMetrics(iconLabel, ICON_HEADER_PROPERTIES),
			}
		end
		if actualTextObject and canonicalHeaderMetrics.Text then
			applyHeaderPartMetrics(actualTextObject, canonicalHeaderMetrics.Text)
		elseif actualTextObject then
			actualTextObject.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			actualTextObject.Position = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0.5, 0)
			actualTextObject.Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 1, 0)
			pcall(function()
				actualTextObject.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				actualTextObject.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
				actualTextObject.TextScaled = false
				actualTextObject.TextSize = isTenFootInterface and 28 or (utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() and 14 or 20)
			end)
		end
		if iconLabel and canonicalHeaderMetrics.Icon then
			applyHeaderPartMetrics(iconLabel, canonicalHeaderMetrics.Icon)
		elseif iconLabel then
			iconLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			iconLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, 0)
			local iconSize = isTenFootInterface and 44 or 28
			iconLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, iconSize, 0, iconSize)
		end
	end
	this.AlignTabHeaderMetrics = alignTabHeaderMetrics

	local function makeGui()
		local oldShield = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("SettingsClippingShield")
		if oldShield then
			local reusablePages = {this.PlayersPage, this.ResetCharacterPage, this.LeaveGamePage, this.GameSettingsPage, this.ReportAbusePage, this.HelpPage, this.RecordPage, this.HomePage}
			for _, page in pairs(reusablePages) do
				if page then
					local header
					pcall(function() header = page:GetTabHeader() end)
					header = header or page.TabHeader
					for _, gui in pairs({header, page.Page}) do
						if gui then
							local ok, inside = pcall(function() return gui:IsDescendantOf(oldShield) end)
							if ok and inside then
								local detached, detachError = pcall(function() gui.Parent = nil end)
								if not detached then warn("[SettingsHub] Could not preserve cached page GUI " .. tostring(gui.Name) .. ": " .. tostring(detachError)) end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
			oldShield:Destroy()
		end

		this.ClippingShield = utility:Create("Frame") {
			Name = "SettingsClippingShield", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
			BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = true,
			ZIndex = SETTINGS_BASE_ZINDEX, Parent = RobloxGui,
		}
		this.Shield = utility:Create("Frame") {
			Name = "SettingsShield", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Position = SETTINGS_SHIELD_INACTIVE_POSITION,
			BackgroundTransparency = SETTINGS_SHIELD_TRANSPARENCY, BackgroundColor3 = SETTINGS_SHIELD_COLOR,
			BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, Active = true, ZIndex = SETTINGS_BASE_ZINDEX, Parent = this.ClippingShield,
		}
		this.VRShield = utility:Create("Frame") {
			Name = "VRBackground", Parent = this.Shield, BackgroundColor3 = SETTINGS_SHIELD_COLOR,
			BackgroundTransparency = SETTINGS_SHIELD_TRANSPARENCY, Position = UDim2.new(0, -4, 0, 24),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 8, 1, -40), BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false,
		}
		this.VersionContainer = utility:Create("Frame") {
			Name = "VersionContainer", Parent = this.Shield, BackgroundColor3 = SETTINGS_SHIELD_COLOR,
			BackgroundTransparency = SETTINGS_SHIELD_TRANSPARENCY, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, VERSION_BAR_HEIGHT), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
			BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5, Visible = false,
		}
		local placeLabelWidth = FFlagHelpMenuShowPlaceVersion and 0.333 or 0.5
		local clientLabelPosition = FFlagHelpMenuShowPlaceVersion and 0.333 or 0.5
		local clientTextAlignment = FFlagHelpMenuShowPlaceVersion and Enum.TextXAlignment.Center or Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
		local versionTextSize = isTenFootInterface and 28 or (utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() and 14 or 20)
		this.ServerVersionLabel = utility:Create("TextLabel") {
			Name = "ServerVersionLabel", Parent = this.VersionContainer, Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 3),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextSize = versionTextSize,
			Text = "Server Version: loading...", Size = UDim2.new(placeLabelWidth, -6, 1, -6),
			Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 5,
		}
		task.spawn(function()
			if this.ServerVersionLabel and this.ServerVersionLabel.Parent then this.ServerVersionLabel.Text = "Server Version: " .. getServerVersion() end
		end)
		this.ClientVersionLabel = utility:Create("TextLabel") {
			Name = "ClientVersionLabel", Parent = this.VersionContainer, Position = UDim2.new(clientLabelPosition, 3, 0, 3),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextSize = versionTextSize,
			Text = "Client Version: " .. getClientVersion(), Size = UDim2.new(placeLabelWidth, -6, 1, -6),
			Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans, TextXAlignment = clientTextAlignment, ZIndex = 5,
		}
		if FFlagHelpMenuShowPlaceVersion then
			this.PlaceVersionLabel = utility:Create("TextLabel") {
				Name = "PlaceVersionLabel", Parent = this.VersionContainer, Position = UDim2.new(0.666, 3, 0, 3),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextSize = versionTextSize,
				Text = "Place Version: " .. getPlaceVersionText(), Size = UDim2.new(0.333, -6, 1, -6),
				Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, ZIndex = 5,
			}
			game:GetPropertyChangedSignal("PlaceVersion"):Connect(function()
				if this.PlaceVersionLabel and this.PlaceVersionLabel.Parent then this.PlaceVersionLabel.Text = "Place Version: " .. getPlaceVersionText() end
			end)
		end
		this.EnvironmentLabel = utility:Create("TextLabel") {
			Name = "EnvironmentLabel", Parent = this.VersionContainer, Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 3),
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			TextSize = versionTextSize, Text = CONTENT_BASE_URL, Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 1, -6),
			Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex = 5, Visible = isTestEnvironment,
		}
		this.Modal = utility:Create("TextButton") {
			Name = "Modal", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
			Modal = true, Text = "", Parent = this.Shield, Selectable = false, AutoButtonColor = false,
		}
		this.MenuContainer = utility:Create("Frame") {
			Name = "MenuContainer", ZIndex = this.Shield.ZIndex, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.95, 0, 0.95, 0),
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Parent = this.Shield,
		}
		if FFlagEnableResponsiveUIFix then
			this.MenuListLayout = utility:Create("UIListLayout") {
				Name = "MenuListLayout", FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
				VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
				SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = this.MenuContainer,
			}
		end
		this.MenuAspectRatio = utility:Create("UIAspectRatioConstraint") {
			Name = "MenuAspectRatio", AspectRatio = 800 / 600, AspectType = Enum.AspectType.ScaleWithParentSize,
			DominantAxis = Enum.DominantAxis.Height, Parent = this.MenuContainer,
		}
		this.HubBar = utility:Create("ImageLabel") {
			Name = "HubBar", ZIndex = this.Shield.ZIndex + 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(78, 84, 96), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuBackground.png", ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice,
			SliceCenter = Rect.new(4, 4, 6, 6), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), LayoutOrder = 0, Parent = this.MenuContainer,
		}
		this.HubBarListLayout = utility:Create("UIListLayout") {
			FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
			SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = this.HubBar,
		}
		if utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then
			this.HubBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 32)
			this.HubBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 6)
		elseif isTenFootInterface then
			this.HubBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1200, 0, 100)
			this.HubBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.1, 0)
		else
			this.HubBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 800, 0, 60)
			this.HubBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.05, 0)
		end
		this.PageViewClipper = utility:Create("Frame") {
			Name = "PageViewClipper", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.new(this.HubBar.Size.X.Scale, this.HubBar.Size.X.Offset, 1, -this.HubBar.Size.Y.Offset - this.HubBar.Position.Y.Offset),
			Position = UDim2.new(this.HubBar.Position.X.Scale, this.HubBar.Position.X.Offset, this.HubBar.Position.Y.Scale, this.HubBar.Position.Y.Offset + this.HubBar.Size.Y.Offset + 1),
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), ClipsDescendants = true, LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = this.MenuContainer,
		}
		utility:Create("ImageButton") {
			Name = "InputCapture", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
			Image = "", Active = false, Selectable = false, Parent = this.PageViewClipper,
		}
		this.PageView = utility:Create("ScrollingFrame") {
			Name = "PageView", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), ZIndex = this.Shield.ZIndex, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			BorderSizePixel = 0, Selectable = false, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), ScrollBarThickness = 8,
			Parent = this.PageViewClipper,
		}
		if FFlagSettingsHubPlayersHorizontalScroll then pcall(function() this.PageView.VerticalScrollBarInset = Enum.ScrollBarInset.ScrollBar end) end
		this.PageViewInnerFrame = utility:Create("Frame") {
			Name = "PageViewInnerFrame", Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
			ZIndex = this.Shield.ZIndex, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Selectable = false, Parent = this.PageView,
		}
		this.BottomButtonFrame = utility:Create("Frame") {
			Name = "BottomButtonFrame", Size = this.HubBar.Size, Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), ZIndex = this.Shield.ZIndex + 1,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = 2, Visible = false, Parent = this.MenuContainer,
		}
		this.BottomButtonFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
			if this.Visible and isConfirmationPage(this.Pages.CurrentPage) and this.BottomButtonFrame.Visible then
				this.BottomButtonFrame.Visible = false
				removeBottomBarBindings()
			end
		end)

		local leaveGameFunc = function()
			if not this.LeaveGamePage then setVisibilityInternal(false); return end
			if isConfirmationPage(this.Pages.CurrentPage) then return end
			this:AddToMenuStack(this.Pages.CurrentPage)
			this.HubBar.Visible = false
			removeBottomBarBindings()
			this:SwitchToPage(this.LeaveGamePage, true, 1, true)
		end
		this.OpenLeaveConfirmation = leaveGameFunc
		local inviteToGameFunc = function()
			if RunService:IsStudio() then return end
			local platformService = getServiceSafely("PlatformService")
			if platformService then tryMethod(platformService, "PopupGameInviteUI") end
		end
		local resumeFunc = function() setVisibilityInternal(false) end
		local buttonImageAppend = isTenFootInterface and "@2x" or ""
		if isXboxPlatform() then
			local function createInviteButton()
				addBottomBarButton("InviteToGame", "Send Game Invites",
					"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/XButtonLight" .. buttonImageAppend .. ".png", "",
					UDim2.new(0.5, isTenFootInterface and -160 or -130, 0.5, -25), inviteToGameFunc, {Enum.KeyCode.ButtonX})
				if RunService:IsStudio() then
					local button = this.InviteToGameButton
					if button then
						button.Selectable = false; button.Active = false
						local enabled = button:FindFirstChild("Enabled")
						if enabled and enabled:IsA("BoolValue") then enabled.Value = false end
					end
				end
			end
			task.spawn(function()
				local platformService = getServiceSafely("PlatformService")
				if not platformService then return end
				local ok, creatorId = pcall(function() return platformService:BeginGetPMPCreatorId() end)
				if ok and creatorId == 0 then createInviteButton() end
			end)
		else
			addBottomBarButton("LeaveGame", "Leave Game",
				"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/XButtonLight" .. buttonImageAppend .. ".png",
				"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/LeaveIcon.png",
				UDim2.new(0.5, isTenFootInterface and -160 or -130, 0.5, -19), leaveGameFunc, {Enum.KeyCode.ButtonX})
		end
		local resetCharFunc = function()
			if not resetEnabled or not this.ResetCharacterPage then return end
			if isConfirmationPage(this.Pages.CurrentPage) then return end
			this:AddToMenuStack(this.Pages.CurrentPage)
			this.HubBar.Visible = false
			removeBottomBarBindings()
			this:SwitchToPage(this.ResetCharacterPage, true, 1, true)
		end
		this.OpenResetConfirmation = resetCharFunc
		addBottomBarButton("ResetCharacter", "Reset Character",
			"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/YButtonLight" .. buttonImageAppend .. ".png",
			"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/ResetIcon.png",
			UDim2.new(0.5, isTenFootInterface and -550 or -400, 0.5, -19), resetCharFunc, {Enum.KeyCode.ButtonY})
		addBottomBarButton("Resume", "Resume Game",
			"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/BButtonLight" .. buttonImageAppend .. ".png",
			"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/EscapeIcon.png",
			UDim2.new(0.5, isTenFootInterface and 200 or 140, 0.5, -19), resumeFunc,
			{Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonStart})
		-- Touch footer keeps all three historical controls. Their layout is
		-- recomputed below after viewport sizing, so phones and tablets both work.
		if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
			this.BottomButtonFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			this.BottomButtonFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
			local touchButtons = {
				{ "LeaveGameButton", "LeaveGameText", 0.17 },
				{ "ResetCharacterButton", "ResetCharacterText", 0.50 },
				{ "ResumeButton", "ResumeText", 0.83 },
			}
			for _, entry in ipairs(touchButtons) do
				local button = this[entry[1]]
				local label = this[entry[2]]
				if button then
					button.Visible = true
					button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
					button.Position = UDim2.new(entry[3], 0, 0.5, 0)
					button.Size = UDim2.new(0.31, 0, 0.88, 0)
				end
				if label then
					label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -4)
					label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 2)
					label.TextSize = 14
					label.TextWrapped = true
					label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
				end
			end
			local inviteButton = this.InviteToGameButton
			if inviteButton then inviteButton.Visible = false end
			this.BottomButtonFrame:SetAttribute("YekuTouchFooterRestored", true)
		end

		local function onViewportChanged()
			utility:FireOnResized()
			task.defer(function()
				if not this.MenuContainer or not this.MenuContainer.Parent or not this.PageViewClipper then return end
				if onScreenSizeChanged then onScreenSizeChanged() end
				enforcePageChrome(this.Pages.CurrentPage)
			end)
		end
		local function connectCamera(camera)
			if viewportConnection then viewportConnection:Disconnect(); viewportConnection = nil end
			if camera then viewportConnection = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(onViewportChanged) end
			onViewportChanged()
		end
		connectCamera(workspace.CurrentCamera)
		if cameraChangedConnection then cameraChangedConnection:Disconnect() end
		cameraChangedConnection = workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function() connectCamera(workspace.CurrentCamera) end)
	end


	local function setMenuAspectRatioEnabled(enabled)
		local constraint = this.MenuAspectRatio
		local container = this.MenuContainer
		if not constraint or not container then return end
		pcall(function() constraint.DominantAxis = Enum.DominantAxis.Height end)
		local targetParent = enabled and container or nil
		if constraint.Parent == targetParent then return end
		local ok, err = pcall(function() constraint.Parent = targetParent end)
		if not ok then warn("[SettingsHub] Could not update MenuAspectRatio parent: " .. tostring(err)) end
	end

	----------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- SHARED PAGE CLIPPER LAYOUT
	----------------------------------------------------------------------
	updatePageClipperLayout = function()
		local menu = this.MenuContainer
		local hubBar = this.HubBar
		local clipper = this.PageViewClipper
		local bottomBar = this.BottomButtonFrame
		local pageView = this.PageView
		if not menu or not menu.Parent or not hubBar or not clipper or not bottomBar or not pageView then return false end
		local menuSize = menu.AbsoluteSize
		local menuPosition = menu.AbsolutePosition
		if menuSize.X &lt; 2 or menuSize.Y &lt; 2 then return false end
		local pageTop = 12
		if hubBar.Visible then
			local hubTop = hubBar.AbsolutePosition.Y - menuPosition.Y
			local hubHeight = hubBar.AbsoluteSize.Y
			if hubHeight &lt; 1 then
				hubHeight = hubBar.Size.Y.Offset
				hubTop = menuSize.Y * hubBar.Position.Y.Scale + hubBar.Position.Y.Offset
			end
			pageTop = math.max(pageTop, hubTop + hubHeight + 8)
		end
		local pageBottom = menuSize.Y - 12
		if bottomBar.Visible then
			local footerTop = bottomBar.AbsolutePosition.Y - menuPosition.Y
			local footerHeight = bottomBar.AbsoluteSize.Y
			if footerHeight &gt; 0 then pageBottom = math.min(pageBottom, footerTop - 8) end
		end
		if pageBottom &lt;= pageTop then pageTop = math.max(8, math.min(pageTop, pageBottom - 1)) end
		local pageHeight = math.max(1, math.floor(pageBottom - pageTop))
		local pageWidth = hubBar.AbsoluteSize.X
		if pageWidth &lt; 1 then pageWidth = hubBar.Size.X.Scale * menuSize.X + hubBar.Size.X.Offset end
		pageWidth = math.max(1, math.min(menuSize.X - 16, pageWidth))
		clipper.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		clipper.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, math.floor(pageTop + 0.5))
		clipper.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.floor(pageWidth + 0.5), 0, pageHeight)
		clipper.ClipsDescendants = true
		pageView.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		pageView.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		pageView.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		return true
	end

	onScreenSizeChanged = function()
		if not this.MenuContainer or not this.HubBar or not this.PageViewClipper or not this.PageView or not this.BottomButtonFrame then return end
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- RESTORE ORIGINAL SETTINGS HUB SIZING
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		local viewport = getRootSize()
		local fullScreenSize = viewport.Y
		local portrait = utility:IsPortrait()
		local smallTouch = utility:IsSmallTouchScreen()
		local largestPageSize = isTenFootInterface and 800 or 600
		local minimumPageSize = 150
		local bufferSize
		if isTenFootInterface then
			bufferSize = 0.07 * fullScreenSize
			this.MenuContainer.Size = UDim2.new(0.95, 0, 0.95, 0)
			setMenuAspectRatioEnabled(true)
		elseif smallTouch then
			bufferSize = math.min(10, (1 - 0.99) * fullScreenSize)
			this.MenuContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.99, 0)
			setMenuAspectRatioEnabled(false)
		else
			bufferSize = (1 - 0.95) * fullScreenSize
			this.MenuContainer.Size = UDim2.new(0.95, 0, 0.95, 0)
			setMenuAspectRatioEnabled(true)
		end
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- HEADER SIZE AND POSITION
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		local hubHeight
		if portrait then
			this.MenuContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			setMenuAspectRatioEnabled(false)
			hubHeight = 32
			this.HubBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, hubHeight)
			this.HubBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 10)
		elseif isTenFootInterface then
			hubHeight = 100
			this.HubBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1200, 0, hubHeight)
			this.HubBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.1, 0)
		elseif smallTouch then
			hubHeight = 32
			this.HubBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, hubHeight)
			this.HubBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 6)
		else
			hubHeight = 60
			this.HubBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 800, 0, hubHeight)
			this.HubBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.1, 0)
		end
		local barSize = hubHeight
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- ORIGINAL USABLE PAGE HEIGHT
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		local extraSpace = bufferSize * 2 + barSize * 2
		local usableScreenHeight = fullScreenSize - extraSpace
		local usePageSize
		local centeredLayout = false
		if not portrait then
			if usableScreenHeight &gt; largestPageSize then
				usePageSize = largestPageSize
				centeredLayout = true
			elseif usableScreenHeight &lt; minimumPageSize then
				usePageSize = minimumPageSize
				centeredLayout = true
			else
				usePageSize = usableScreenHeight
			end
		else
			usePageSize = usableScreenHeight
		end
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- RESTORE ORIGINAL HEADER / FOOTER GEOMETRY
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		local barWidth
		if portrait then
			barWidth = math.max(1, viewport.X - 20)
		elseif smallTouch then
			barWidth = math.max(1, viewport.X - 10)
		else
			barWidth = this.HubBar.Size.X.Offset
		end
		this.BottomButtonFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		this.BottomButtonFrame.Size = this.HubBar.Size
		local footerX = UDim2.new(0.5, -barWidth / 2, 0, 0)
		if portrait then
			this.BottomButtonFrame.Position = UDim2.new(footerX.X.Scale, footerX.X.Offset, 1, -(this.HubBar.Position.Y.Offset + barSize))
		elseif centeredLayout then
			this.HubBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -usePageSize / 2 - barSize)
			this.BottomButtonFrame.Position = UDim2.new(footerX.X.Scale, footerX.X.Offset, 0.5, usePageSize / 2)
		else
			this.HubBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, math.max(6, bufferSize - 12))
			this.BottomButtonFrame.Position = UDim2.new(footerX.X.Scale, footerX.X.Offset, 1, -(bufferSize + barSize))
		end
		if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
			this.BottomButtonFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
			this.BottomButtonFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 52)
			this.BottomButtonFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -6)
			this.BottomButtonFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			for _, entry in ipairs({
				{ "LeaveGameButton", "LeaveGameText", 0.17 },
				{ "ResetCharacterButton", "ResetCharacterText", 0.50 },
				{ "ResumeButton", "ResumeText", 0.83 },
			}) do
				local button, label = this[entry[1]], this[entry[2]]
				if button then
					button.Visible = true
					button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
					button.Position = UDim2.new(entry[3], 0, 0.5, 0)
					button.Size = UDim2.new(0.31, 0, 0.88, 0)
				end
				if label then
					label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -4)
					label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 2)
					label.TextSize = 14
					label.TextWrapped = true
				end
			end
		end
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- RESTORE ORIGINAL PAGE CLIPPER HEIGHT
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		local pageHeight = usePageSize + (smallTouch and 36 or 0) - (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 58 or 0)
		this.PageViewClipper.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		this.PageViewClipper.ClipsDescendants = true
		if portrait then
			this.PageViewClipper.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, this.HubBar.Position.Y.Offset + barSize)
		else
			this.PageViewClipper.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -usePageSize / 2)
		end
		this.PageViewClipper.Size = UDim2.new(0, barWidth, 0, math.max(1, math.floor(pageHeight + 0.5)))
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- THE PAGE VIEW FILLS THE RESTORED CLIPPER
		------------------------------------------------------------------
		this.PageView.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		this.PageView.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		this.PageView.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		this.PageView.ClipsDescendants = true
		enforcePageChrome(this.Pages.CurrentPage)

		-- Recalculate from the actual MenuContainer-local bounds once Roblox
		-- has applied the preceding AbsolutePosition/AbsoluteSize changes.
		-- This creates a real gap below HubBar and above the bottom actions,
		-- including on portrait/touch layouts where the old offset math could
		-- place the page clipper underneath the header.
		queuePageClipperRefresh()
	end

	local function scrollPixels(pixels)
		local maxY = math.max(0, this.PageView.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - this.PageView.AbsoluteSize.Y)
		this.PageView.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.clamp(this.PageView.CanvasPosition.Y + pixels, 0, maxY))
	end
	function this:ScrollPixels(pixels) scrollPixels(pixels) end
	function this:ScrollToFrame(frame, forced)
		if not frame or not frame:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
		local inputType = getLastInputType()
		local gamepadLast = tostring(inputType):find("Gamepad", 1, true) ~= nil
		if not (gamepadLast or forced) then return end
		local page = self.Pages.CurrentPage
		if not page or not page.Page then return end
		local top = frame.AbsolutePosition.Y - page.Page.AbsolutePosition.Y
		local bottom = top + frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
		local currentY = self.PageView.CanvasPosition.Y
		if top &lt; currentY then
			self.PageView.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, top))
		elseif bottom - currentY &gt; self.PageViewClipper.AbsoluteSize.Y then
			self.PageView.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, bottom - self.PageViewClipper.AbsoluteSize.Y))
		end
	end

	GetHeaderPosition = function(page)
		if not page then return -1 end
		local header
		pcall(function() header = page:GetTabHeader() end)
		header = header or page.TabHeader
		if not header then return -1 end
		for index, tab in ipairs(this.TabHeaders) do if tab == header then return index end end
		return -1
	end
	local function setZIndex(zIndex, object)
		if not object or not object:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
		object.ZIndex = zIndex
		for _, child in ipairs(object:GetChildren()) do setZIndex(zIndex, child) end
	end
	local function addHeader(header, page)
		if not header or not header:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
		if table.find(this.TabHeaders, header) then return end
		local parented, parentError = pcall(function() header.Parent = this.HubBar end)
		if not parented then
			page.TabPosition = nil
			warn("[SettingsHub] Skipping unusable tab header for " .. tostring(page.Page and page.Page.Name or "page") .. ": " .. tostring(parentError))
			return
		end
		-- Version 1: change only the Players tab image; retain the page itself.
		if page == this.PlayersPage then
			local playersIcon = findHeaderIcon(header)
			if playersIcon then
				pcall(function()
					playersIcon.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarIcons/PlayersTabIcon.png"
				end)
			end
		end
		table.insert(this.TabHeaders, header)
		page.TabPosition = #this.TabHeaders
		local widthScale = 1 / #this.TabHeaders
		for index, tab in ipairs(this.TabHeaders) do
			tab.Size = UDim2.new(widthScale, 0, 1, 0)
			tab.LayoutOrder = index
		end
		alignTabHeaderMetrics(header)
		setZIndex(SETTINGS_BASE_ZINDEX + 1, header)
	end
	local function removeHeader(header)
		if not header then return end
		local removedIndex
		for index, tab in ipairs(this.TabHeaders) do
			if tab == header then removedIndex = index; table.remove(this.TabHeaders, index); break end
		end
		if removedIndex then
			local newWidth = #this.TabHeaders &gt; 0 and (1 / #this.TabHeaders) or 1
			for index, tab in ipairs(this.TabHeaders) do
				tab.Size = UDim2.new(newWidth, 0, 1, 0)
				tab.LayoutOrder = index
			end
		end
		pcall(function() header.Parent = nil end)
	end
	function this:AddPage(page)
		if not page then return false end
		self.Pages.PageTable[page] = true
		local ok, header = pcall(function() return page:GetTabHeader() end)
		if not ok or not header then header = page.TabHeader end
		addHeader(header, page)
		if page.Page and page.TabPosition then page.Page.Position = UDim2.new(page.TabPosition - 1, 0, 0, 0) end
		if type(page.SetHub) == "function" then tryMethod(page, "SetHub", self) end
		return true
	end
	function this:RemovePage(page)
		if not page then return end
		self.Pages.PageTable[page] = nil
		local header
		pcall(function() header = page:GetTabHeader() end)
		header = header or page.TabHeader
		removeHeader(header)
		if self.Pages.CurrentPage == page then self.Pages.CurrentPage = nil end
	end
	function this:HideBar()
		if this.HubBar then this.HubBar.Visible = false end
		if this.PageViewClipper then this.PageViewClipper.Visible = false end
		if this.BottomButtonFrame then this.BottomButtonFrame.Visible = false end
		removeBottomBarBindings()
	end
	function this:ShowBar()
		local currentPage = self.Pages.CurrentPage
		if this.HubBar then this.HubBar.Visible = shouldShowHubBar(currentPage) end
		if this.PageViewClipper then this.PageViewClipper.Visible = true; this.PageViewClipper.ClipsDescendants = true end
		if this.Visible and shouldShowBottomBar(currentPage) then setBottomBarBindings() else removeBottomBarBindings() end
		if this.BottomButtonFrame then this.BottomButtonFrame.Visible = this.Visible and shouldShowBottomBar(currentPage) end
		if onScreenSizeChanged then task.defer(onScreenSizeChanged) end
	end
	local function getPageSize(page)
		local ok, size = tryMethod(page, "GetSize")
		if ok and typeof(size) == "Vector2" then return size end
		if page and page.Page then return Vector2.new(page.Page.AbsoluteSize.X, page.Page.AbsoluteSize.Y) end
		return Vector2.new(0, 0)
	end

	-- Leave/Reset are fixed confirmation screens, not scrollable settings
	-- documents. Earlier code expanded their canvas to page.Page.Size.Y.Offset
	-- (600px), which reintroduced the very scrollbar the page modules removed.
	updateCanvasForPage = function(page)
		if not (this.PageView and this.PageViewInnerFrame) or not page then return end
		local isConfirmation = isConfirmationPage(page)
		if isConfirmation then
			local viewport = this.PageView.AbsoluteSize
			if viewport.X &lt; 1 or viewport.Y &lt; 1 then
				viewport = this.PageViewClipper and this.PageViewClipper.AbsoluteSize or Vector2.new(1, 1)
			end
			this.PageView.ScrollingEnabled = false
			this.PageView.ScrollBarThickness = 0
			this.PageView.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1
			this.PageViewInnerFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			this.PageView.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, math.max(0, math.floor(viewport.X)), 0, math.max(0, math.floor(viewport.Y)))
			this.PageView.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
			return
		end

		this.PageView.ScrollingEnabled = true
		this.PageView.ScrollBarThickness = 8
		this.PageView.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0
		local size = getPageSize(page)
		this.PageView.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, math.max(0, size.X), 0, math.max(0, size.Y))
		if not FFlagSettingsHubPlayersHorizontalScroll then
			if this.PageView.CanvasSize.Y.Offset &gt; this.PageView.AbsoluteSize.Y then
				this.PageViewInnerFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -this.PageView.ScrollBarThickness, 1, 0)
			else
				this.PageViewInnerFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			end
		end
	end

	function this:SwitchToPage(pageToSwitchTo, ignoreStack, direction, skipAnimation)
		if not pageToSwitchTo or self.Pages.PageTable[pageToSwitchTo] == nil then return false end
		local oldPage = self.Pages.CurrentPage
		if oldPage == pageToSwitchTo then
			forceHideInactiveConfirmationPages(pageToSwitchTo)
			enforcePageChrome(pageToSwitchTo)
			if onScreenSizeChanged then task.defer(onScreenSizeChanged) end
			return true
		end
		if direction == nil then
			local oldHeader, newHeader
			if oldPage then pcall(function() oldHeader = oldPage:GetTabHeader() end) end
			pcall(function() newHeader = pageToSwitchTo:GetTabHeader() end)
			oldHeader = oldHeader or (oldPage and oldPage.TabHeader)
			newHeader = newHeader or pageToSwitchTo.TabHeader
			if oldHeader and newHeader then
				direction = oldHeader.AbsolutePosition.X &lt; newHeader.AbsolutePosition.X and 1 or -1
			else
				direction = 1
			end
		end
		if oldPage then
			if pageChangeConnection then pageChangeConnection:Disconnect(); pageChangeConnection = nil end
			oldPage.Active = false
			if not ignoreStack and self.MenuStack[#self.MenuStack] ~= oldPage then table.insert(self.MenuStack, oldPage) end
		end
		for page in pairs(self.Pages.PageTable) do
			if page ~= pageToSwitchTo then tryMethod(page, "Hide", -direction, pageToSwitchTo.TabPosition or 1, skipAnimation) end
		end
		forceHideInactiveConfirmationPages(pageToSwitchTo)
		self.Pages.CurrentPage = pageToSwitchTo
		local isConfirmation = isConfirmationPage(pageToSwitchTo)
		if self.BottomButtonFrame then self.BottomButtonFrame.Visible = self.Visible and shouldShowBottomBar(pageToSwitchTo) end
		if not shouldShowBottomBar(pageToSwitchTo) then removeBottomBarBindings() elseif self.Visible then setBottomBarBindings() end
		local clipped = FFlagSettingsHubBarsRefactor and pageToSwitchTo.IsPageClipped == true
		if FFlagSettingsHubBarsRefactor then
			self.PageViewClipper.ClipsDescendants = true
			self.PageView.ClipsDescendants = clipped
			self.PageViewInnerFrame.ClipsDescendants = clipped
		else
			self.PageViewClipper.ClipsDescendants = true
		end
		self.HubBar.Visible = shouldShowHubBar(pageToSwitchTo)
		self.PageViewClipper.Visible = true
		self.PageView.Visible = true
		self.PageViewInnerFrame.Visible = true
		if isConfirmation then
			self.PageView.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
			self.PageViewInnerFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		end
		local displayOk, displayError = tryMethod(pageToSwitchTo, "Display", self.PageViewInnerFrame, skipAnimation)
		if not displayOk then
			warn("[SettingsHub] Display failed for " .. tostring(pageToSwitchTo.Page and pageToSwitchTo.Page.Name or "page") .. ": " .. tostring(displayError))
		end
		if isConfirmation and pageToSwitchTo.Page and pageToSwitchTo.Page:IsA("GuiObject") then
			local pageGui = pageToSwitchTo.Page
			if pageGui.Parent ~= self.PageViewInnerFrame then
				local parentOk, parentError = pcall(function() pageGui.Parent = self.PageViewInnerFrame end)
				if not parentOk then warn("[SettingsHub] Could not attach confirmation page " .. pageGui.Name .. ": " .. tostring(parentError)) end
			end
			pcall(function() pageGui.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0); pageGui.Visible = true; pageGui.Active = self.Active end)
			self.PageView.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		end
		forceHideInactiveConfirmationPages(pageToSwitchTo)
		pageToSwitchTo.Active = self.Active
		enforcePageChrome(pageToSwitchTo)
		task.defer(function()
			if self.Pages.CurrentPage ~= pageToSwitchTo then return end
			forceHideInactiveConfirmationPages(pageToSwitchTo)
			enforcePageChrome(pageToSwitchTo)
			if onScreenSizeChanged then onScreenSizeChanged() else updatePageClipperLayout() end
		end)
		updateCanvasForPage(pageToSwitchTo)
		queuePageClipperRefresh()
		if pageToSwitchTo.Page and pageToSwitchTo.Page:IsA("GuiObject") then
			pageChangeConnection = pageToSwitchTo.Page:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
				if self.Pages.CurrentPage ~= pageToSwitchTo then return end
				updateCanvasForPage(pageToSwitchTo)
			end)
		end
		-- The clipper can resize after the confirmation page is displayed, so
		-- take one final measurement after layout settles. This still uses the
		-- shared PageView viewport, never the page's 600px content frame.
		if isConfirmation then
			task.defer(function()
				task.wait()
				if self.Pages.CurrentPage == pageToSwitchTo then
					updateCanvasForPage(pageToSwitchTo)
					self.PageView.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
				end
			end)
		end
		return true
	end

	function this:SetActive(active)
		self.Active = active == true
		if self.Pages.CurrentPage then self.Pages.CurrentPage.Active = self.Active end
	end
	function this:AddToMenuStack(item)
		if item and self.MenuStack[#self.MenuStack] ~= item then table.insert(self.MenuStack, item) end
	end
	function this:PopMenu(switchedFromGamepadInput, skipAnimation)
		if #self.MenuStack &gt; 0 then
			local previous = table.remove(self.MenuStack)
			PoppedMenuEvent:Fire(previous)
			if previous and self.Pages.PageTable[previous] then
				self:SwitchToPage(previous, true, 1, skipAnimation)
			elseif #self.MenuStack &gt; 0 and self.Pages.PageTable[self.MenuStack[#self.MenuStack]] then
				self:SwitchToPage(self.MenuStack[#self.MenuStack], true, 1, skipAnimation)
			else
				table.clear(self.MenuStack)
				self:SetVisibility(false, skipAnimation)
			end
		else
			PoppedMenuEvent:Fire()
			self:SetVisibility(false, skipAnimation)
		end
	end
	function this:ClearMenuStack() table.clear(self.MenuStack) end
	function this:ShowShield()
		self.Shield.BackgroundTransparency = VRService.VREnabled and SETTINGS_SHIELD_VR_TRANSPARENCY or SETTINGS_SHIELD_TRANSPARENCY
	end
	function this:HideShield() self.Shield.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end

	local function switchTab(direction, cycle)
		local currentPosition = GetHeaderPosition(this.Pages.CurrentPage)
		if currentPosition &lt; 1 or #this.TabHeaders == 0 then return end
		local newPosition = currentPosition + direction
		if cycle then
			if newPosition &gt; #this.TabHeaders then newPosition = 1 elseif newPosition &lt; 1 then newPosition = #this.TabHeaders end
		end
		local header = this.TabHeaders[newPosition]
		if not header then return end
		for page in pairs(this.Pages.PageTable) do
			local pageHeader
			pcall(function() pageHeader = page:GetTabHeader() end)
			pageHeader = pageHeader or page.TabHeader
			if pageHeader == header then this:SwitchToPage(page, false, direction); return end
		end
	end
	local function switchTabFromBumpers(_, inputState, inputObject)
		if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin or not inputObject then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end
		if inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR1 then switchTab(1, true)
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL1 then switchTab(-1, true) end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end
	local function switchTabFromKeyboard(input)
		if not this.Visible or input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Tab then return end
		local shiftDown = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.RightShift)
		switchTab(shiftDown and -1 or 1, true)
	end
	local function scrollHotkey(_, inputState, inputObject)
		if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end
		if inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.PageUp then scrollPixels(-100)
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.PageDown then scrollPixels(100) end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end
	local function toggleQuickProfilerFromHotkey(_, inputState)
		if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin or not this.Visible then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end
		local controlDown = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.RightControl)
		if controlDown then
			pcall(function()
				local settingsObject = UserSettings()
				local gameSettings = settingsObject.GameSettings
				gameSettings.PerformanceStatsVisible = not gameSettings.PerformanceStatsVisible
			end)
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end
	local function toggleDevConsole(_, inputState)
		if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end
		if FFlagEnableNewDevConsole and DevConsoleMaster then
			tryMethod(DevConsoleMaster, "ToggleVisibility")
		elseif DeveloperConsoleModule then
			local ok, visible = tryMethod(DeveloperConsoleModule, "GetVisibility")
			if ok then tryMethod(DeveloperConsoleModule, "SetVisibility", not visible) end
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end

	----------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- L/R CONFIRMATION HOTKEYS
	----------------------------------------------------------------------
	local function hasFocusedTextBox()
		local focusedTextBox
		pcall(function() focusedTextBox = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() end)
		return focusedTextBox ~= nil
	end
	local function requestConfirmation(openMethodName)
		if not this.Visible or not this.Active then return false end
		if hasFocusedTextBox() then return false end
		if isConfirmationPage(this.Pages.CurrentPage) then return false end
		local callback = this[openMethodName]
		if type(callback) ~= "function" then return false end
		callback()
		return true
	end
	local function makeConfirmationHotkey(openMethodName)
		return function(_, inputState)
			if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end
			if not this.Visible or not this.Active then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end
			if isConfirmationPage(this.Pages.CurrentPage) then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end
			if hasFocusedTextBox() then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end
			requestConfirmation(openMethodName)
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		end
	end
	local function bindConfirmationHotkeys()
		bindAction(LEAVE_KEY_ACTION_NAME, makeConfirmationHotkey("OpenLeaveConfirmation"), false, Enum.KeyCode.L)
		bindAction(RESET_KEY_ACTION_NAME, makeConfirmationHotkey("OpenResetConfirmation"), false, Enum.KeyCode.R)
	end
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
		if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.L and input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.R then return end
		if not this.Visible or not this.Active or hasFocusedTextBox() or isConfirmationPage(this.Pages.CurrentPage) then return end
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.L then requestConfirmation("OpenLeaveConfirmation")
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.R then requestConfirmation("OpenResetConfirmation") end
	end)

	local lastInputUsedToSelectGui = isTenFootInterface
	local inputUsedToSelectGui = {
		[Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1] = true, [Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2] = true,
		[Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad3] = true, [Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad4] = true,
		[Enum.KeyCode.Left] = true, [Enum.KeyCode.Right] = true, [Enum.KeyCode.Up] = true,
		[Enum.KeyCode.Down] = true, [Enum.KeyCode.Tab] = true,
		[Enum.UserInputType.Touch] = false, [Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1] = false,
		[Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2] = false, [Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement] = false,
	}
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		local typeValue = inputUsedToSelectGui[input.UserInputType]
		if typeValue ~= nil then lastInputUsedToSelectGui = typeValue
		elseif inputUsedToSelectGui[input.KeyCode] ~= nil then lastInputUsedToSelectGui = inputUsedToSelectGui[input.KeyCode] end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1 or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick2 then
			if input.Position.Magnitude &gt;= 0.25 then lastInputUsedToSelectGui = true end
		elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
			lastInputUsedToSelectGui = false
		end
	end)

	function this:SetVisibility(visible, noAnimation, customStartPage, switchedFromGamepadInput)
		if self.Visible == (visible == true) then
			forceHideInactiveConfirmationPages(self.Pages.CurrentPage)
			enforcePageChrome(self.Pages.CurrentPage)
			return
		end
		setVisibilityInternal(visible, noAnimation, customStartPage, switchedFromGamepadInput)
	end
	function this:GetVisibility() return self.Visible end
	function this:ToggleVisibility(switchedFromGamepadInput)
		setVisibilityInternal(not self.Visible, nil, nil, switchedFromGamepadInput)
	end

	setVisibilityInternal = function(visible, noAnimation, customStartPage, switchedFromGamepadInput)
		local self = this
		visible = visible == true
		if self.Visible == visible then return end
		self.OpenStateChangedCount += 1
		self.Visible = visible
		if self.ResizedConnection then self.ResizedConnection:Disconnect(); self.ResizedConnection = nil end
		if self.TabConnection then self.TabConnection:Disconnect(); self.TabConnection = nil end
		if self.Modal then self.Modal.Visible = visible end
		if visible then
			self.TabConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(switchTabFromKeyboard)
			self.ResizedConnection = RobloxGui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
				if onScreenSizeChanged then onScreenSizeChanged() end
			end)
			onScreenSizeChanged()
			fireSignal(self.SettingsShowSignal, true)
			setMenuOpenState(true)
			self:ShowShield()
			self.Shield.Visible = true
			if noAnimation then
				if activeShieldTween then pcall(function() activeShieldTween:Cancel() end); activeShieldTween = nil end
				self.Shield.Position = SETTINGS_SHIELD_ACTIVE_POSITION
			else
				tweenShieldPosition(SETTINGS_SHIELD_ACTIVE_POSITION, SETTINGS_OPEN_TWEEN_DURATION, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
			end
			local function sinkCharacterActions() return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end
			bindAction("RbxSettingsHubStopCharacter", sinkCharacterActions, false,
				Enum.KeyCode.W, Enum.KeyCode.A, Enum.KeyCode.S, Enum.KeyCode.D, Enum.KeyCode.Space,
				Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift, Enum.KeyCode.RightShift, Enum.KeyCode.Tab,
				Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1, Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2, Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad3, Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad4)
			bindAction("RbxSettingsHubSwitchTab", switchTabFromBumpers, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR1, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL1)
			bindAction("RbxSettingsScrollHotkey", scrollHotkey, false, Enum.KeyCode.PageUp, Enum.KeyCode.PageDown)
			bindAction(DEV_CONSOLE_ACTION_NAME, toggleDevConsole, false, Enum.KeyCode.F9)
			bindAction(QUICK_PROFILER_ACTION_NAME, toggleQuickProfilerFromHotkey, false, Enum.KeyCode.F7)
			local page = customStartPage
			if not page then
				if not isTenFootInterface and self.PlayersPage then
					page = self.PlayersPage
				elseif self.HomePage then
					page = self.HomePage
				else
					page = self.GameSettingsPage
				end
			end
			if page and self.Pages.PageTable[page] then
				self:SwitchToPage(page, true, 1, true)
			else
				warn("[SettingsHub] No registered page is available for the requested open page")
			end
			bindConfirmationHotkeys()
			enforcePageChrome(self.Pages.CurrentPage)
			hideTempPlayerList(true)
			if getChatVisible() then self.ChatWasVisible = true; toggleChatVisibility() end
			if BackpackScript then
				local isOpen = getProperty(BackpackScript, "IsOpen", false)
				if isOpen then tryMethod(BackpackScript, "OpenClose") end
			end
		else
			forceHideConfirmationPage(self.LeaveGamePage)
			forceHideConfirmationPage(self.ResetCharacterPage)
			if self.LeaveGamePage then
				tryMethod(self.LeaveGamePage, "Hide", -1, self.LeaveGamePage.TabPosition or 1, true)
				forceHideConfirmationPage(self.LeaveGamePage)
			end
			if self.ResetCharacterPage then
				tryMethod(self.ResetCharacterPage, "Hide", -1, self.ResetCharacterPage.TabPosition or 1, true)
				forceHideConfirmationPage(self.ResetCharacterPage)
			end
			if self.BottomButtonFrame then self.BottomButtonFrame.Visible = false end
			fireSignal(self.SettingsShowSignal, false)
			if noAnimation then
				if activeShieldTween then pcall(function() activeShieldTween:Cancel() end); activeShieldTween = nil end
				self.Shield.Position = SETTINGS_SHIELD_INACTIVE_POSITION
				self.Shield.Visible = false
				setMenuOpenState(false)
			else
				tweenShieldPosition(SETTINGS_SHIELD_INACTIVE_POSITION, SETTINGS_CLOSE_TWEEN_DURATION, Enum.EasingDirection.In, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, function()
					if not self.Visible and self.Shield and self.Shield.Parent then
						self.Shield.Visible = false
						setMenuOpenState(false)
					end
				end)
			end
			hideTempPlayerList(false)
			if self.ChatWasVisible then toggleChatVisibility(); self.ChatWasVisible = false end
			if not VRService.VREnabled then setMouseIconBehavior("None") end
			table.clear(self.MenuStack)
			unbindAction(LEAVE_KEY_ACTION_NAME)
			unbindAction(RESET_KEY_ACTION_NAME)
			unbindAction("RbxSettingsHubSwitchTab")
			unbindAction("RbxSettingsHubStopCharacter")
			unbindAction("RbxSettingsScrollHotkey")
			unbindAction(DEV_CONSOLE_ACTION_NAME)
			unbindAction(QUICK_PROFILER_ACTION_NAME)
			removeBottomBarBindings(0.3)
			setSelectedObject(nil)
		end
	end

	local function onVREnabled()
		if VRService.VREnabled then
			local panelModule = findPath(RobloxGui, {"Modules", "VR", "Panel3D"})
			local Panel3D = requireSafely(panelModule, "VR.Panel3D")
			if Panel3D and type(Panel3D.Get) == "function" then
				local ok, panel = pcall(Panel3D.Get, "SettingsMenu")
				if ok and panel then
					tryMethod(panel, "ResizeStuds", 4, 4, 250)
					pcall(function() panel:SetType(Panel3D.Type.Standard) end)
					tryMethod(panel, "SetVisible", false)
					tryMethod(panel, "SetCanFade", false)
					pcall(function() this.ClippingShield.Parent = panel:GetGUI() end)
					this.VRShield.Visible = true
					this:HideShield()
					this.VRPanel = panel
				end
			end
		else
			if this.ClippingShield and this.ClippingShield.Parent then this.ClippingShield.Parent = RobloxGui end
			if this.VRShield then this.VRShield.Visible = false end
			if this.Shield then this:ShowShield() end
			if this.VRPanel then tryMethod(this.VRPanel, "SetVisible", false) end
			this.VRPanel = nil
		end
	end

	makeGui()
	onScreenSizeChanged()
	-- Keep the clipper bound to the real header/footer geometry across resize,
	-- safe-area changes, orientation changes, and topbar visibility changes.
	for _, object in ipairs({this.MenuContainer, this.HubBar, this.BottomButtonFrame}) do
		if object then
			for _, propertyName in ipairs({"AbsoluteSize", "AbsolutePosition", "Visible"}) do
				pcall(function()
					object:GetPropertyChangedSignal(propertyName):Connect(queuePageClipperRefresh)
				end)
			end
		end
	end
	VRService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled"):Connect(onVREnabled)
	onVREnabled()
	local function setHubForPage(page) if page then this:AddPage(page) end end
	setHubForPage(this.PlayersPage)
	setHubForPage(this.ResetCharacterPage)
	setHubForPage(this.LeaveGamePage)
	setHubForPage(this.GameSettingsPage)
	setHubForPage(this.ReportAbusePage)
	setHubForPage(this.HelpPage)
	setHubForPage(this.RecordPage)
	if not this.PlayersPage and this.HomePage then setHubForPage(this.HomePage) end

	for _, confirmationPage in ipairs({this.LeaveGamePage, this.ResetCharacterPage}) do
		local confirmationGui = confirmationPage and confirmationPage.Page
		if confirmationGui and confirmationGui:IsA("GuiObject") then
			confirmationGui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
				if this.Pages.CurrentPage ~= confirmationPage and confirmationGui.Visible then forceHideConfirmationPage(confirmationPage) end
			end)
		end
	end

	if shareGameEnabled and CorePackages and SettingsModules then
		local function packagesLoaded()
			for _, name in ipairs({"Roact", "Rodux", "RoactRodux"}) do if not CorePackages:FindFirstChild(name) then return false end end
			return true
		end
		if packagesLoaded() then
			local shareGameMaster = loadModule({"Modules", "Settings", "ShareGameMaster"})
			if shareGameMaster and type(shareGameMaster.createApp) == "function" then
				local ok, app = pcall(shareGameMaster.createApp, this.PageViewClipper)
				if ok and app then
					this.ShareGameApp = app
					local placeholder = createPage({"Modules", "Settings", "Pages", "ShareGamePlaceholderPage"})
					if placeholder then
						tryMethod(placeholder, "ConnectHubToApp", this, app)
						setHubForPage(placeholder)
						this.ShareGamePage = placeholder
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local StarterGui = getServiceSafely("StarterGui")
	if StarterGui then
		pcall(function()
			StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("ResetButtonCallback", function(callback)
				local isBindableEvent = typeof(callback) == "Instance" and callback:IsA("BindableEvent")
				if isBindableEvent or type(callback) == "boolean" then
					if this.ResetCharacterPage then tryMethod(this.ResetCharacterPage, "SetResetCallback", callback) end
				else
					warn("[SettingsHub] ResetButtonCallback must be a BindableEvent or a boolean")
				end
				if callback == false then setResetEnabled(false)
				elseif not resetEnabled and (isBindableEvent or callback == true) then setResetEnabled(true) end
			end)
		end)
	end

	local function clearHubStack() table.clear(this.MenuStack) end
	pcall(function() ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(CLOSE_MENU_ACTION_NAME) end)
	local escapeBound, escapeBindError = pcall(function()
		ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(CLOSE_MENU_ACTION_NAME, function(_, inputState)
			if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end
			local focusedTextBox
			pcall(function() focusedTextBox = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() end)
			if focusedTextBox then pcall(function() focusedTextBox:ReleaseFocus(false) end); return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end
			if not this.Active then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end
			if this.Visible then this:PopMenu(false, false)
			else clearHubStack(); this:SetVisibility(true, false, nil, false) end
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		end, false, Enum.KeyCode.Escape)
	end)
	if not escapeBound then
		warn("[SettingsHub] Could not bind Escape as a core action: " .. tostring(escapeBindError))
		local fallbackBound, fallbackError = pcall(function()
			ContextActionService:UnbindAction(CLOSE_MENU_ACTION_NAME)
			ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(CLOSE_MENU_ACTION_NAME, function(_, inputState)
				if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end
				local focusedTextBox
				pcall(function() focusedTextBox = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() end)
				if focusedTextBox then pcall(function() focusedTextBox:ReleaseFocus(false) end); return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end
				if not this.Active then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end
				if this.Visible then this:PopMenu(false, false)
				else clearHubStack(); this:SetVisibility(true, false, nil, false) end
				return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
			end, false, 10000, Enum.KeyCode.Escape)
		end)
		if fallbackBound then print("[SettingsHub] Escape is bound using the high-priority legacy fallback.")
		else warn("[SettingsHub] Escape fallback binding failed: " .. tostring(fallbackError)) end
	else
		print("[SettingsHub] Escape is bound to the legacy SettingsHub core action.")
	end

	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not this.Visible or not this.Active then return end
		local key = input.KeyCode
		if key == Enum.KeyCode.Left or key == Enum.KeyCode.Right or key == Enum.KeyCode.Up or key == Enum.KeyCode.Down then
			if not getSelectedObject() and this.Pages.CurrentPage then tryMethod(this.Pages.CurrentPage, "SelectARow") end
		end
	end)
	local showLeaveConfirmation = getProperty(GuiService, "ShowLeaveConfirmation", nil)
	if showLeaveConfirmation and typeof(showLeaveConfirmation) == "RBXScriptSignal" then
		showLeaveConfirmation:Connect(function()
			if #this.MenuStack == 0 then
				this:SetVisibility(true)
				local page = this.PlayersPage or this.GameSettingsPage
				if page then this:SwitchToPage(page, true, 1) end
			else
				this:PopMenu(false, false)
			end
		end)
	end
	if this.Pages.CurrentPage == nil then
		local firstPage = this.PlayersPage or this.HomePage or this.GameSettingsPage or this.HelpPage
		if firstPage then this:SwitchToPage(firstPage, true, 1, true)
		else warn("[SettingsHub] No usable Settings page ModuleScripts were found under NewRobloxGui.Modules.Settings.Pages") end
	end
	return this
end

local moduleApiTable = {
	ModuleName = "SettingsMenu",
	KeepVRTopbarOpen = true,
	VRIsExclusive = true,
	VRClosesNonExclusive = true,
}
SettingsHubInstance = createSettingsHub()
function moduleApiTable:SetVisibility(visible, noAnimation, customStartPage, switchedFromGamepadInput)
	SettingsHubInstance:SetVisibility(visible, noAnimation, customStartPage, switchedFromGamepadInput)
end
function moduleApiTable:ToggleVisibility(switchedFromGamepadInput)
	SettingsHubInstance:ToggleVisibility(switchedFromGamepadInput)
end
function moduleApiTable:SwitchToPage(pageToSwitchTo, ignoreStack)
	SettingsHubInstance:SwitchToPage(pageToSwitchTo, ignoreStack, 1)
end
function moduleApiTable:GetVisibility() return SettingsHubInstance.Visible end
function moduleApiTable:ShowShield() SettingsHubInstance:ShowShield() end
function moduleApiTable:HideShield() SettingsHubInstance:HideShield() end
moduleApiTable.SettingsShowSignal = SettingsHubInstance.SettingsShowSignal
moduleApiTable.Instance = SettingsHubInstance
if VRHub then
	pcall(function() VRHub:RegisterModule(moduleApiTable) end)
	local moduleOpened = getProperty(VRHub, "ModuleOpened", nil)
	local openedSignal
	if moduleOpened and typeof(moduleOpened) == "Instance" and moduleOpened:IsA("BindableEvent") then
		openedSignal = moduleOpened.Event
	elseif typeof(moduleOpened) == "RBXScriptSignal" then
		openedSignal = moduleOpened
	end
	if openedSignal then
		openedSignal:Connect(function(moduleName)
			if moduleName == moduleApiTable.ModuleName then return end
			local ok, otherModule = tryMethod(VRHub, "GetModule", moduleName)
			if ok and otherModule and otherModule.VRIsExclusive then moduleApiTable:SetVisibility(false) end
		end)
	end
end
return moduleApiTable</ProtectedString>
                                    <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-143</int64>
                                    <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                                </Properties>
                            </Item>
                            <Item class="Folder" referent="RBX178">
                                <Properties>
                                    <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                    <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                    <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                                    <Color3 name="IconTint">
                                        <R>0</R>
                                        <G>0</G>
                                        <B>0</B>
                                    </Color3>
                                    <string name="Name">Pages</string>
                                    <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                    <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-35</int64>
                                    <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                                </Properties>
                                <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX179">
                                    <Properties>
                                        <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                        <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                        <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                                        <ContentId name="LinkedSource">
                                            <null />
                                        </ContentId>
                                        <string name="Name">Help</string>
                                        <string name="ScriptGuid">{ed28b7cc-9764-4366-91b2-a701c9639dfa}</string>
                                        <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: Help.lua
    Written by: jeditkacheff
    Version 1.0 - Yeku NewRobloxGui compatibility patch
    Description: Help page for the historical Settings Menu.
]]

----------------- FFLAGS -------------------
local success, result = pcall(function()
	return settings():GetFFlag("UseNotificationsLocalization")
end)
local FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization = success and result == true

-------------- CONSTANTS -------------------
local KEYBOARD_MOUSE_TAG = "KeyboardMouse"
local TOUCH_TAG = "Touch"
local GAMEPAD_TAG = "Gamepad"
local PC_TABLE_SPACING = 4
local XBOX_CONTROLLER_IMAGE_OFFSET = 30
local TEXT_EDGE_DISTANCE = 20

-------------- SERVICES --------------------
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local Settings = UserSettings()
local GameSettings = Settings.GameSettings

-------------- LEGACY GUI ROOT -------------
local RobloxGui = script and script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui")
if not (RobloxGui and RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
	RobloxGui = CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15)
end
assert(RobloxGui and RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui"), "[Help] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing.")

local Modules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules", 15)
assert(Modules, "[Help] NewRobloxGui.Modules is missing.")
local SettingsFolder = Modules:WaitForChild("Settings", 15)
assert(SettingsFolder, "[Help] NewRobloxGui.Modules.Settings is missing.")
local utility = require(SettingsFolder:WaitForChild("Utility", 15))

-- TenFootInterface is optional on desktop. Missing/failed module falls back to desktop layout.
local isTenFootInterface = false
do
	local tenFootModule = Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface")
		or Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface.lua")
	if tenFootModule and tenFootModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		local loadOK, api = pcall(require, tenFootModule)
		if loadOK and type(api) == "table" and type(api.IsEnabled) == "function" then
			local enabledOK, enabled = pcall(function()
				return api:IsEnabled()
			end)
			isTenFootInterface = enabledOK and enabled == true
		else
			warn("[Help] TenFootInterface failed to load; using desktop layout: " .. tostring(api))
		end
	else
		warn("[Help] TenFootInterface is missing; using desktop layout.")
	end
end

local function SafeFFlag(Name, Default)
	local ok, value = pcall(function()
		return settings():GetFFlag(Name)
	end)
	if ok then return value == true end
	return Default == true
end

local PageInstance = nil

local function Initialize()
	local factoryModule = SettingsFolder:WaitForChild("SettingsPageFactory", 15)
	assert(factoryModule, "[Help] SettingsPageFactory is missing.")
	local factory = require(factoryModule)
	assert(type(factory) == "table" and type(factory.CreateNewPage) == "function",
		"[Help] SettingsPageFactory.CreateNewPage is missing.")

	local this = factory:CreateNewPage()
	this.HelpPages = {}
	this.HelpPageContents = {}
	this.ActiveHelpScheme = nil
	local lastInputType = nil

	function this:GetCurrentInputType()
		if lastInputType == nil then
			local platform = UserInputService:GetPlatform()
			if platform == Enum.Platform.XBoxOne or platform == Enum.Platform.WiiU then
				return GAMEPAD_TAG
			elseif platform == Enum.Platform.Windows or platform == Enum.Platform.OSX then
				return KEYBOARD_MOUSE_TAG
			end
			return TOUCH_TAG
		end

		if lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
			or lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
			or lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
			or lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3
			or lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
			return KEYBOARD_MOUSE_TAG
		elseif lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			return TOUCH_TAG
		elseif lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1
			or lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2
			or lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad3
			or lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad4 then
			return GAMEPAD_TAG
		end
		return KEYBOARD_MOUSE_TAG
	end

	local function createPCGroup(title, bindings)
		local group = utility:Create "Frame" {
			Name = "PCGroupFrame" .. tostring(title),
			Size = UDim2.new(1 / 3, -PC_TABLE_SPACING, 0, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		}
		utility:Create "TextLabel" {
			Name = "PCGroupTitle" .. tostring(title),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -9, 0, 30),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Text = title,
			Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
			TextSize = 18,
			TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			ZIndex = 2,
			Parent = group,
		}
		local count = 0
		for _, binding in ipairs(bindings) do
			for actionName, inputName in pairs(binding) do
				local y = 30 + 44 * count
				local row = utility:Create "Frame" {
					Name = "ActionInputBinding" .. tostring(actionName),
					Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42),
					Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y),
					BackgroundTransparency = 0.65,
					BorderSizePixel = 0,
					ZIndex = 2,
					Parent = group,
				}
				local actionLabel = utility:Create "TextLabel" {
					Name = tostring(actionName) .. "Label",
					Size = UDim2.new(0.4, -9, 1, 0),
					Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 0),
					BackgroundTransparency = 1,
					Text = tostring(actionName),
					Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
					TextSize = 18,
					TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
					TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
					TextWrapped = true,
					TextScaled = true,
					ZIndex = 2,
					Parent = row,
				}
				Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", actionLabel).MaxTextSize = 18
				local inputLabel = utility:Create "TextLabel" {
					Name = tostring(inputName) .. "Label",
					Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
					Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 0),
					BackgroundTransparency = 1,
					Text = tostring(inputName),
					Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans,
					TextSize = 18,
					TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
					TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
					TextWrapped = true,
					TextScaled = true,
					ZIndex = 2,
					Parent = row,
				}
				Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", inputLabel).MaxTextSize = 18
				count += 1
			end
		end
		group.Size = UDim2.new(1 / 3, -PC_TABLE_SPACING, 0, 30 + 44 * count)
		return group
	end

	local function createPCHelp(parentFrame)
		local isOSX = UserInputService:GetPlatform() == Enum.Platform.OSX
		local move = createPCGroup("Character Movement", {
			{ ["Move Forward"] = "W/Up Arrow" },
			{ ["Move Backward"] = "S/Down Arrow" },
			{ ["Move Left"] = "A/Left Arrow" },
			{ ["Move Right"] = "D/Right Arrow" },
			{ ["Jump"] = "Space" },
		})
		move.Parent = parentFrame

		local accessories = createPCGroup("Accessories", {
			{ ["Equip Tools"] = "1,2,3..." },
			{ ["Unequip Tools"] = "1,2,3..." },
			{ ["Drop Tool"] = "Backspace" },
			{ ["Use Tool"] = "Left Mouse Button" },
		})
		accessories.Position = UDim2.new(1 / 3, PC_TABLE_SPACING, 0, 0)
		accessories.Parent = parentFrame

		local misc = createPCGroup("Misc", {
			{ ["Screenshot"] = "Print Screen" },
			{ ["Record Video"] = isOSX and "F12/fn + F12" or "F12" },
			{ ["Dev Console"] = isOSX and "F9/fn + F9" or "F9" },
			{ ["Mouselock"] = "Shift" },
			{ ["Graphics Level"] = isOSX and "F10/fn + F10" or "F10" },
			{ ["Fullscreen"] = isOSX and "F11/fn + F11" or "F11" },
			{ ["Perf. Stats"] = isOSX and "Fn+Opt+Cmd+F7" or "Ctrl + Shift + F7" },
		})
		misc.Position = UDim2.new(2 / 3, PC_TABLE_SPACING * 2, 0, 0)
		misc.Parent = parentFrame

		local camera = createPCGroup("Camera Movement", {
			{ ["Rotate"] = "Right Mouse Button" },
			{ ["Zoom In/Out"] = "Mouse Wheel" },
			{ ["Zoom In"] = "I" },
			{ ["Zoom Out"] = "O" },
		})
		camera.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, move.Size.Y.Offset + 50)
		camera.Parent = parentFrame

		local menu = createPCGroup("Menu Items", {
			{ ["Roblox Menu"] = "ESC" },
			{ ["Backpack"] = "~" },
			{ ["Playerlist"] = "TAB" },
			{ ["Chat"] = "/" },
		})
		menu.Position = UDim2.new(1 / 3, PC_TABLE_SPACING, 0, move.Size.Y.Offset + 50)
		menu.Parent = parentFrame
		parentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, menu.Position.Y.Offset + menu.Size.Y.Offset)
	end

	local function createGamepadHelp(parentFrame)
		local platform = UserInputService:GetPlatform()
		local image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/GenericController.png"
		local size = UDim2.new(0, 473, 0, 287)
		local xOffset = 0
		if platform == Enum.Platform.XBoxOne or platform == Enum.Platform.XBox360 then
			image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/XboxController.png"
			size = UDim2.new(0, 745, 0, 452)
			xOffset = XBOX_CONTROLLER_IMAGE_OFFSET
		elseif platform == Enum.Platform.PS4 or platform == Enum.Platform.PS3 then
			image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/PSController.png"
		end

		local controller = utility:Create "ImageLabel" {
			Name = "GamepadImage",
			Size = size,
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -size.X.Offset / 2 + xOffset, 0.5, -size.Y.Offset / 2),
			Image = image,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			ZIndex = 2,
			Parent = parentFrame,
		}
		parentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, size.Y.Offset + 100)
		local labels = {
			{"Switch Tool", "Use Tool"},
			{"Game Menu Toggle", "Roblox Menu"},
			{"Move", "Back"},
			{"Menu Navigation", "Jump"},
			{"Rotate Camera", "Camera Zoom"},
		}
		for index, pair in ipairs(labels) do
			local y = (index - 1) * 0.15
			utility:Create "TextLabel" {
				Name = pair[1] .. "Label",
				Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, y, 0),
				Size = UDim2.new(0, 165, 0, 30),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Text = pair[1],
				TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
				Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
				TextSize = isTenFootInterface and 30 or 20,
				TextWrapped = true,
				ZIndex = 3,
				Parent = controller,
			}
			utility:Create "TextLabel" {
				Name = pair[2] .. "Label",
				Position = UDim2.new(1, -175, y, 0),
				Size = UDim2.new(0, 165, 0, 30),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Text = pair[2],
				TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
				Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
				TextSize = isTenFootInterface and 30 or 20,
				TextWrapped = true,
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
				ZIndex = 3,
				Parent = controller,
			}
		end
	end

	local function createTouchHelp(parentFrame)
		local smallScreen = utility:IsSmallTouchScreen()
		local screenResolution = GuiService:GetScreenResolution()
		local height = screenResolution.Y - (smallScreen and 100 or 350)
		parentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
		local elements = {}

		local function label(Name, Text, Position, Size)
			local item = utility:Create "TextLabel" {
				Name = Name,
				Text = Text,
				Position = Position,
				Size = Size,
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
				TextSize = smallScreen and 14 or 18,
				TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
				TextScaled = true,
				TextWrapped = true,
				ZIndex = 3,
				Parent = parentFrame,
			}
			local constraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
			constraint.MaxTextSize = smallScreen and 14 or 18
			constraint.Parent = item
			return item
		end

		elements.MoveLabel = label("MoveLabel", "Move", UDim2.new(0.06, 0, 0.58, 0), UDim2.new(0, 110, 0, 35))
		elements.JumpLabel = label("JumpLabel", "Jump", UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0.58, 0), UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 35))
		elements.EquipLabel = label("EquipLabel", "Equip/Unequip Tools", UDim2.new(0.5, -70, 0.64, 0), UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 35))
		elements.ZoomLabel = label("ZoomLabel", "Zoom In/Out", UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.02, 0), UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 35))
		elements.RotateLabel = label("RotateLabel", "Rotate Camera", UDim2.new(0.5, -65, 0.02, 0), UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 35))
		elements.UseToolLabel = label("UseToolLabel", "Use Tool", UDim2.new(0.85, -65, 0.02, 0), UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 35))
		return elements
	end

	local function createHelpDisplay(typeOfHelp)
		local frame = utility:Create "Frame" {
			Name = "HelpFrame" .. tostring(typeOfHelp),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		}
		local contents
		if typeOfHelp == KEYBOARD_MOUSE_TAG then
			createPCHelp(frame)
		elseif typeOfHelp == GAMEPAD_TAG then
			createGamepadHelp(frame)
		elseif typeOfHelp == TOUCH_TAG then
			contents = createTouchHelp(frame)
		end
		return frame, contents
	end

	local function updateTouchLayout(scheme)
		this.ActiveHelpScheme = scheme
		local helpElements = this.HelpPageContents[TOUCH_TAG]
		if not helpElements then return end
		local isPortrait = utility:IsPortrait()

		if scheme == Enum.TouchMovementMode.DynamicThumbstick then
			helpElements.MoveLabel.Position = isPortrait and UDim2.new(0.25, -40, 0.75, -50) or UDim2.new(0.15, -40, 0.85, -35)
			helpElements.JumpLabel.Position = isPortrait and UDim2.new(0.75, -40, 0.75, -50) or UDim2.new(0.85, -60, 0.85, -35)
		elseif scheme == Enum.TouchMovementMode.ClickToMove then
			helpElements.JumpLabel.Visible = false
			helpElements.MoveLabel.Position = isPortrait and UDim2.new(0.25, -40, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0.25, -40, 0.5, 40)
		else
			helpElements.MoveLabel.Position = isPortrait and UDim2.new(0.06, 0, 1, -120) or UDim2.new(0.06, 0, 0.58, 0)
			helpElements.JumpLabel.Visible = true
			helpElements.JumpLabel.Position = isPortrait and UDim2.new(0.94, -100, 1, -120) or UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0.58, 0)
		end
	end

	local function displayHelp(currentPage)
		for _, helpPage in pairs(this.HelpPages) do
			if helpPage == currentPage then
				helpPage.Parent = this.Page
				this.Page.Size = helpPage.Size
			else
				helpPage.Parent = nil
			end
		end
		if UserInputService:GetPlatform() == Enum.Platform.XBoxOne and this.HubRef then
			pcall(function()
				this.HubRef.PageViewClipper.ClipsDescendants = false
				this.HubRef.PageView.ClipsDescendants = false
			end)
		end
	end

	local function switchToHelp(typeOfHelp)
		local page = this.HelpPages[typeOfHelp]
		if not page then
			page, this.HelpPageContents[typeOfHelp] = createHelpDisplay(typeOfHelp)
			this.HelpPages[typeOfHelp] = page
		end
		displayHelp(page)
		if typeOfHelp == TOUCH_TAG then
