			updateTouchLayout(GameSettings.TouchMovementMode)
		end
	end

	local function showTypeOfHelp()
		switchToHelp(this:GetCurrentInputType())
	end

	local function adjustForScreenLayout(givenSize)
		if this:GetCurrentInputType() == TOUCH_TAG and this.HelpPages[TOUCH_TAG] then
			local size = givenSize or GuiService:GetScreenResolution()
			local ySize = size.Y - (utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() and 100 or 350)
			this.HelpPages[TOUCH_TAG].Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ySize)
			updateTouchLayout(GameSettings.TouchMovementMode)
		end
	end

	-- Tab customization.
	this.TabHeader.Name = "HelpTab"
	this.TabHeader.Icon.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarIcons/HelpTab.png"
	if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
		this.TabHeader.Title.Text = "Help"
	else
		local iconTitle = this.TabHeader.Icon:FindFirstChild("Title")
		if iconTitle then iconTitle.Text = "Help" end
	end

	this.Page.Name = "Help"

	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject)
		local inputType = inputObject.UserInputType
		if inputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Focus and inputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.None then
			lastInputType = inputType
			showTypeOfHelp()
		end
	end)

	utility:OnResized(this, function(newSize)
		if this.HelpPages[TOUCH_TAG] then
			adjustForScreenLayout(newSize)
		end
	end)

	return this
end

-- Public-facing API additions.
PageInstance = Initialize()

PageInstance.Displayed.Event:Connect(function()
	if not PageInstance.HubRef then return end
	local isPortrait = utility:IsPortrait()
	if PageInstance:GetCurrentInputType() == TOUCH_TAG then
		if PageInstance.HubRef.BottomButtonFrame
			and not utility:IsSmallTouchScreen()
			and not isPortrait then
			PageInstance.HubRef.BottomButtonFrame.Visible = false
		end
	end
	if PageInstance.HubRef.VersionContainer then
		PageInstance.HubRef.VersionContainer.Visible = true
	end
end)

PageInstance.Hidden.Event:Connect(function()
	if not PageInstance.HubRef then return end
	pcall(function()
		PageInstance.HubRef.PageViewClipper.ClipsDescendants = true
		PageInstance.HubRef.PageView.ClipsDescendants = true
		PageInstance.HubRef:ShowShield()
	end)
	local isPortrait = utility:IsPortrait()
	if PageInstance:GetCurrentInputType() == TOUCH_TAG then
		if PageInstance.HubRef.BottomButtonFrame
			and not utility:IsSmallTouchScreen()
			and not isPortrait then
			PageInstance.HubRef.BottomButtonFrame.Visible = true
		end
	end
	if PageInstance.HubRef.VersionContainer then
		PageInstance.HubRef.VersionContainer.Visible = false
	end
end)

return PageInstance

    Filename: Home.lua
    Written by: jeditkacheff
    Version 1.0 - Yeku NewRobloxGui compatibility patch
    Description: Home page for the historical Settings Menu.
]]

local BUTTON_OFFSET = 20
local BUTTON_SPACING = 10

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")

-- Resolve the cloned legacy root instead of CoreGui.RobloxGui.
local RobloxGui = script and script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui")
if not (RobloxGui and RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
	RobloxGui = CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15)
end
assert(RobloxGui and RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui"), "[Home] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing.")

local Modules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules", 15)
assert(Modules, "[Home] NewRobloxGui.Modules is missing.")
local SettingsFolder = Modules:WaitForChild("Settings", 15)
assert(SettingsFolder, "[Home] NewRobloxGui.Modules.Settings is missing.")
local utility = require(SettingsFolder:WaitForChild("Utility", 15))

local success, result = pcall(function()
	return settings():GetFFlag("UseNotificationsLocalization")
end)
local FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization = success and result == true

local function Initialize()
	local factoryModule = SettingsFolder:WaitForChild("SettingsPageFactory", 15)
	assert(factoryModule, "[Home] SettingsPageFactory is missing.")
	local settingsPageFactory = require(factoryModule)
	assert(type(settingsPageFactory) == "table" and type(settingsPageFactory.CreateNewPage) == "function",
		"[Home] SettingsPageFactory.CreateNewPage is missing.")

	local this = settingsPageFactory:CreateNewPage()

	this.TabHeader.Name = "HomeTab"
	this.TabHeader.Icon.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarIcons/HomeTab.png"
	this.TabHeader.Icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 30)
	this.TabHeader.Icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0.5, -15)

	if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
		local title = this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("Title")
		if title then title.Text = "Home" end
	else
		local title = this.TabHeader.Icon:FindFirstChild("Title")
		if title then title.Text = "Home" end
	end

	this.TabHeader.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 1, 0)
	this.Page.Name = "Home"

	local function makeButton(Name, Text, Position, Callback)
		local button = utility:MakeStyledButton(
			Name,
			Text,
			UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50),
			Callback
		)
		button.Position = Position
		button.Parent = this.Page
		return button
	end

	local resumeButton = makeButton(
		"ResumeButton",
		"Resume Game",
		UDim2.new(0.5, -100, 0, BUTTON_OFFSET),
		function()
			if this.HubRef then this.HubRef:SetVisibility(false) end
		end
	)
	this.ResumeButton = resumeButton

	local resetButton = makeButton(
		"ResetButton",
		"Reset Character",
		UDim2.new(0.5, -100, 0, BUTTON_OFFSET + 50 + BUTTON_SPACING),
		function()
			if this.HubRef then
				this.HubRef:SwitchToPage(this.HubRef.ResetCharacterPage, false, 1)
			end
		end
	)

	local leaveButton = makeButton(
		"LeaveButton",
		"Leave Game",
		UDim2.new(0.5, -100, 0, BUTTON_OFFSET + 2 * (50 + BUTTON_SPACING)),
		function()
			if this.HubRef then
				this.HubRef:SwitchToPage(this.HubRef.LeaveGamePage, false, 1)
			end
		end
	)

	this.Page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, BUTTON_OFFSET + 3 * 50 + 2 * BUTTON_SPACING)
	return this
end

local PageInstance = Initialize()

PageInstance.Displayed.Event:Connect(function()
	if not utility:UsesSelectedObject() then return end
	GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = PageInstance.ResumeButton
end)

return PageInstance

                                <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX181">
                                    <Properties>
                                        <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                        <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                        <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>

                                            <null />
                                        </ContentId>

                                        <string name="ScriptGuid">{96dbc55a-b2f7-400b-95a6-60636c738596}</string>
                                        <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: Players.lua
    Yeku Legacy NewRobloxGui compatibility build v10.

    Complete replacement ModuleScript based on the supplied Yeku Players.lua
    and the original Roblox CoreScript visual states.

    Friend status presentation:
      - Add Friend: Utility's styled button, including its normal background.
      - Friend: plain, transparent SourceSans status text in muted gray.
      - Request Sent: plain, transparent SourceSans status text in muted gray.
      - Portrait layout: passive Friend/Request Sent icon buttons use the
        original MenuButton slice, Friend icon, and faded request-sent icon.

    Keeps the legacy SettingsPageFactory / Utility paths, player rows, report
    and custom View actions, mobile controls, player refresh, gamepad selection,
    and the custom Invite Friends overlay. No React / CorePackages dependency.
]]

----------- LOCAL VIEW REQUEST EVENT --------------
local function getGuiInspectAndBuyEvent()
	local event = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GuiInspectAndBuy")
	if event then
		if event:IsA("BindableEvent") then return event end
		warn("[Yeku View] ReplicatedStorage.GuiInspectAndBuy exists as " .. event.ClassName .. "; expected BindableEvent.")
		return nil
	end
	event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	event.Name = "GuiInspectAndBuy"
	event.Parent = ReplicatedStorage
	return event
end

local function fireGuiInspectAndBuy(userId, sourceName)
	local id = tonumber(userId)
	if not id or id &lt;= 0 then
		warn("[Yeku View] Invalid UserId from " .. tostring(sourceName) .. ": " .. tostring(userId))
		return false
	end
	local event = getGuiInspectAndBuyEvent()
	if not event then return false end
	print("[Yeku View] " .. tostring(sourceName) .. " dispatching GuiInspectAndBuy(" .. tostring(id) .. ")")
	event:Fire(id)
	return true
end

assert(Modules and Modules:IsA("Folder"), "[Players] NewRobloxGui.Modules is missing.")

assert(SettingsFolder and SettingsFolder:IsA("Folder"), "[Players] NewRobloxGui.Modules.Settings is missing.")
local PagesFolder = SettingsFolder:FindFirstChild("Pages")

local function safeRequire(moduleScript, description)
	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		warn("[Players] Missing ModuleScript: " .. tostring(description))
		return nil
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if not ok then warn("[Players] Failed requiring " .. tostring(description) .. ": " .. tostring(result)); return nil end
	return result
end

local utility = require(utilityModule)
assert(type(utility) == "table", "[Players] Settings.Utility did not return a table.")

-- The 2018+ Players page contains Yeku-created labels and an invite overlay.
-- Register only fixed UI strings; player names and editable search text remain dynamic.
local RobloxTranslator = safeRequire(Modules:FindFirstChild("RobloxTranslator"), "Modules/RobloxTranslator")
local LOCALIZED_PLAYER_UI_TEXT = {
    ["Players"] = true, ["Friend"] = true, ["Request Sent"] = true,
    ["View"] = true, ["Back"] = true, ["Loading username..."] = true,
    ["Online"] = true, ["Offline"] = true,
    ["In Experience"] = true, ["In Studio"] = true,
    ["Add Friend"] = true, ["Reset Character"] = true, ["Leave Game"] = true,
    ["Resume Game"] = true, ["Invite Friends"] = true,
    ["Invite friends to play"] = true, ["Invite"] = true,
    ["Invited..."] = true, ["Unavailable"] = true,
    ["Loading friends..."] = true, ["Search for friends"] = true,
    ["No friends match your search."] = true, ["No friends to invite."] = true,
    ["Couldn't load friends. Try again."] = true,
}
local watchedPlayerUiText = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local function registerPlayerUiText(instance)
    if not RobloxTranslator or not instance then return end
    if not (instance:IsA("TextLabel") or instance:IsA("TextButton")) then return end
    if not watchedPlayerUiText[instance] then
        watchedPlayerUiText[instance] = true
        instance:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            local changedText = instance.Text
            if LOCALIZED_PLAYER_UI_TEXT[changedText] then
                pcall(function() RobloxTranslator:RegisterTextObject(instance, changedText) end)
            end
        end)
    end
    local sourceText = instance.Text
    if LOCALIZED_PLAYER_UI_TEXT[sourceText] then
        pcall(function() RobloxTranslator:RegisterTextObject(instance, sourceText) end)
    end
end
local function watchPlayerUiText(root)
    if not root then return end
    for _, descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do registerPlayerUiText(descendant) end
    root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
        task.defer(function() registerPlayerUiText(descendant) end)
    end)
end
watchPlayerUiText(NewRobloxGui)

local function safeFFlag(name, defaultValue)
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return settings():GetFFlag(name) end)
	if ok then return value == true end
	return defaultValue == true
end
local function tryRequire(root, path)
	local current = root
	for segment in string.gmatch(path, "[^/]+") do
		if not current then return nil end
		current = current:FindFirstChild(segment) or current:FindFirstChild(segment .. ".lua")
	end
	return safeRequire(current, path)
end
local function safeTenFootEnabled()
	local tenFoot = false
	local moduleScript = Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface") or Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface.lua")
	if moduleScript and moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		local ok, api = pcall(require, moduleScript)
		if ok and type(api) == "table" then
			if type(api.IsEnabled) == "function" then
				local enabledOk, enabled = pcall(function() return api:IsEnabled() end)
				tenFoot = enabledOk and enabled == true
			elseif type(api.IsTenFootInterface) == "function" then
				local enabledOk, enabled = pcall(function() return api:IsTenFootInterface() end)
				tenFoot = enabledOk and enabled == true
			end
		elseif not ok then
			warn("[Players] TenFootInterface failed; using non-TV layout: " .. tostring(api))
		end
	elseif moduleScript and moduleScript:IsA("BoolValue") then
		tenFoot = moduleScript.Value
	end
	if not tenFoot then
		local flag = Lighting:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface")
		if flag and flag:IsA("BoolValue") then tenFoot = flag.Value end
	end
	return tenFoot
end

local isTenFootInterface = safeTenFootEnabled()

------------ HELPERS -------------------
local function isPortrait()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not camera then return false end
	local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
	return viewport.X &gt; 0 and viewport.Y &gt; viewport.X
end
local function isSmallTouchScreen()
	if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then return false end
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or NewRobloxGui.AbsoluteSize
	if not viewport or viewport.X &lt;= 0 or viewport.Y &lt;= 0 then return true end
	-- A phone remains a phone in landscape; tablets keep the footer actions.
	return math.min(viewport.X, viewport.Y) &lt;= 640
end
local function getFriendStatus(player)
	local ok, status = pcall(function() return localPlayer:GetFriendStatus(player) end)
	if ok then return status end
	return Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend
end
local function getThumbnail(userId)
	userId = math.max(1, tonumber(userId) or 1)
	if type(SocialUtil.GetFallbackPlayerImageUrl) == "function" then
		local ok, url = pcall(SocialUtil.GetFallbackPlayerImageUrl, userId, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100, Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarThumbnail)
		if ok and type(url) == "string" and url ~= "" then return url end
	end
	local ok, url = pcall(function()
		return PlayersService:GetUserThumbnailAsync(userId, Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarThumbnail, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)
	end)
	if ok and type(url) == "string" and url ~= "" then return url end
	return "rbxthumb://type=AvatarThumbnail&amp;id=" .. tostring(userId) .. "&amp;w=100&amp;h=100"
end
local function getCurrentSelection()
	local selected
	pcall(function() selected = GuiService.SelectedCoreObject end)
	if not selected then pcall(function() selected = GuiService.SelectedObject end) end
	return selected
end
local function setCurrentSelection(object)
	pcall(function() GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = object end)
	pcall(function() GuiService.SelectedObject = object end)
end
local function safeRequestFriendship(player)
	local ok, err = pcall(function() localPlayer:RequestFriendship(player) end)
	if not ok then warn("[Players] RequestFriendship failed for " .. player.Name .. ": " .. tostring(err)) end
	return ok
end
local function safeConnect(signal, callback)
	if not signal then return nil end
	local ok, connection = pcall(function() return signal:Connect(callback) end)
	if ok then return connection end
	return nil
end
local function getUserId(record)
	if not record then return nil end
	return tonumber(record.Id or record.UserId or record.VisitorId)
end

local function Initialize()
	local this = settingsPageFactory:CreateNewPage()
	this.Page.Name = "Players"
	this.Page.ClipsDescendants = true
	if this.PageListLayout then this.PageListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, PLAYER_ROW_SPACING - PLAYER_ROW_HEIGHT) end
	this.TabHeader.Name = "PlayersTab"
	this.TabHeader.Icon.Image = isTenFootInterface and "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarIcons/PlayersTabIcon@2x.png" or "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarIcons/PlayersTabIcon.png"
	if isSmallTouchScreen() then
		this.TabHeader.Icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28)
		this.TabHeader.Icon.Position = UDim2.new(this.TabHeader.Icon.Position.X.Scale, this.TabHeader.Icon.Position.X.Offset, 0.5, -14)
		this.TabHeader.Size = UDim2.new(0, 115, 1, 0)
	elseif isTenFootInterface then
		this.TabHeader.Icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(74, 74)
		this.TabHeader.Icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -43)
		this.TabHeader.Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 1, 0)
	else
		this.TabHeader.Icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(35, 35)
		this.TabHeader.Icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0.5, -18)
		this.TabHeader.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 1, 0)
	end
	if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
		local title = this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("Title")
		if title then title.Text = "Players" end
	else
		local title = this.TabHeader.Icon:FindFirstChild("Title")
		if title then title.Text = "Players" end
	end

	local playerLabelFakeSelection = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	playerLabelFakeSelection.Name = "PlayerLabelFakeSelection"
	playerLabelFakeSelection.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	playerLabelFakeSelection.Image = ""
	playerLabelFakeSelection.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
	local existingPlayerLabels = {}
	local friendStatusConnection, viewportConnection, playerAddedConnection, playerRemovingConnection, cameraChangedConnection
	local touchLayout, shareGameButton, firstFriendSelectionSet, firstReportCandidate, refreshQueued, destroyed = false, nil, false, nil, false, false
	local queueRefresh

	------------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- INVITE OVERLAY
	------------------------------------------------------------------------
	local InviteMenu = {
		Panel=nil, Header=nil, BackButton=nil, SearchFrame=nil, SearchBox=nil, SearchIcon=nil,
		SearchPlaceholder=nil, InviteList=nil, EmptyLabel=nil, Friends={}, Rows={}, InvitedFriendIds={},
		Loading=false, LoadFailed=false, HubVisibilitySnapshot=nil, LayoutConnection=nil,
		SettingsVisibilityConnection=nil, ShieldVisibilityConnection=nil, ClippingVisibilityConnection=nil,
		PageHiddenConnection=nil, VisibilityWatchRunning=false, ActiveOptions=nil,
	}
	local rebuildInviteRows, updateInviteLayout, closeInviteMenu

	local function findHubGuiObject(hub, propertyName, childName)
		local object
		pcall(function()
			local candidate = hub and hub[propertyName]
			if typeof(candidate) == "Instance" then object = candidate end
		end)
		if object then return object end
		local roots = {}
		pcall(function() if hub and typeof(hub.Shield) == "Instance" then table.insert(roots, hub.Shield) end end)
		table.insert(roots, NewRobloxGui)
		for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
			local ok, found = pcall(function() return root:FindFirstChild(childName, true) end)
			if ok and found and found:IsA("GuiObject") then return found end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function getInviteUsername(friend)
		local username = friend and (friend.Username or friend.UserName) or ""
		username = type(username) == "string" and username or ""
		return string.gsub(username, "^@", "")
	end
	local resolvingUsernames = {}
	local function resolveMissingUsernames(friends)
		for _, friend in ipairs(friends) do
			if getInviteUsername(friend) == "" then
				local userId = getUserId(friend)
				local key = userId and tostring(userId)
				if userId and userId &gt; 0 and key and not resolvingUsernames[key] then
					resolvingUsernames[key] = true
					task.spawn(function()
						local ok, username = pcall(function() return PlayersService:GetNameFromUserIdAsync(userId) end)
						if ok and type(username) == "string" and username ~= "" then friend.Username = username end
						resolvingUsernames[key] = nil
						if InviteMenu.Panel and InviteMenu.Panel.Visible and table.find(InviteMenu.Friends, friend) then rebuildInviteRows() end
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local function getInviteStatus(friend)
		if not friend or friend.IsOnline ~= true then return "Offline", OFFLINE_COLOR end
		local locationType = tonumber(friend.LocationType)
		if locationType == 1 or locationType == 4 then return "In Experience", ONLINE_COLOR end
		if locationType == 3 or locationType == 6 then return "In Studio", IN_STUDIO_COLOR end
		return "Online", ONLINE_COLOR
	end
	local function friendMatchesSearch(friend, query)
		query = string.lower(query or "")
		if query == "" then return true end
		return string.find(string.lower(getInviteUsername(friend)), query, 1, true) ~= nil
	end
	local function fetchInviteFriends()
		local result, byId = {}, {}
		local primarySucceeded, primaryComplete, primaryError = false, false, nil
		local okPages, pagesOrError = pcall(function() return PlayersService:GetFriendsAsync(localPlayer.UserId) end)
		local pages
		if okPages and pagesOrError then pages = pagesOrError; primarySucceeded = true else primaryError = pagesOrError end
		if pages then
			while true do
				local okCurrent, currentPage = pcall(function() return pages:GetCurrentPage() end)
				if not okCurrent or type(currentPage) ~= "table" then primaryError = currentPage; break end
				for _, record in ipairs(currentPage) do
					local userId = getUserId(record)
					local key = userId and tostring(userId)
					if userId and not byId[key] then
						local entry = {Id=userId, Username=record.Username or record.UserName or "", IsOnline=record.IsOnline == true, LocationType=record.LocationType, Invited=InviteMenu.InvitedFriendIds[key] == true}
						byId[key] = entry; table.insert(result, entry)
					end
				end
				local finishedOk, finished = pcall(function() return pages.IsFinished end)
				if finishedOk and finished == true then primaryComplete = true; break end
				if not finishedOk then primaryError = finished; break end
				local advanceOk, advanceError = pcall(function() pages:AdvanceToNextPageAsync() end)
				if not advanceOk then primaryError = advanceError; break end
			end
		end
		local onlineOk, onlineFriends = pcall(function() return localPlayer:GetFriendsOnlineAsync(200) end)
		if not onlineOk or type(onlineFriends) ~= "table" then onlineOk, onlineFriends = pcall(function() return localPlayer:GetFriendsOnline(200) end) end
		if onlineOk and type(onlineFriends) == "table" then
			for _, online in ipairs(onlineFriends) do
				local userId = getUserId(online)
				if userId then
					local key = tostring(userId)
					local entry = byId[key]
					if entry then
						entry.IsOnline = online.IsOnline ~= false; entry.LocationType = online.LocationType; entry.PlaceId = online.PlaceId; entry.GameId = online.GameId
					else
						entry = {Id=userId, Username=online.UserName or online.Username or "", IsOnline=online.IsOnline ~= false, LocationType=online.LocationType, PlaceId=online.PlaceId, GameId=online.GameId, Invited=InviteMenu.InvitedFriendIds[key] == true}
						byId[key] = entry; table.insert(result, entry)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		local rank = { ["In Experience"]=1, Online=2, ["In Studio"]=3, Offline=4 }
		table.sort(result, function(a,b)
			local sa, sb = getInviteStatus(a), getInviteStatus(b)
			if rank[sa] ~= rank[sb] then return rank[sa] &lt; rank[sb] end
			return string.lower(getInviteUsername(a)) &lt; string.lower(getInviteUsername(b))
		end)
		if not primarySucceeded then warn("[Players Invite] GetFriendsAsync failed: " .. tostring(primaryError))
		elseif not primaryComplete then warn("[Players Invite] Friend list may be incomplete: " .. tostring(primaryError)) end
		return result, (primarySucceeded and primaryComplete) or #result &gt; 0
	end

	local function makeInviteButton(parent, name, text, size, zIndex)
		local button = Instance.new("ImageButton")
		button.Name=name; button.Size=size; button.BackgroundTransparency=1; button.BorderSizePixel=0
		button.Image=INVITE_BUTTON_IMAGE; button.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Slice; button.SliceCenter=Rect.new(8,6,46,44)
		button.AutoButtonColor=false; button.Active=true; button.Selectable=true; button.ZIndex=zIndex; button.Parent=parent
		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.Name=name .. "TextLabel"; label.BackgroundTransparency=1; label.BorderSizePixel=0
		label.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,-6); label.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,2)
		label.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSansBold; label.TextSize=20; label.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1)
		label.Text=text; label.TextWrapped=false; label.TextScaled=false
		label.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center; label.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		label.ZIndex=zIndex+1; label.Parent=button
		button.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if button.Active then button.Image=INVITE_BUTTON_HOVER_IMAGE end end)
		button.MouseLeave:Connect(function() button.Image=INVITE_BUTTON_IMAGE end)
		return button,label
	end
	local function setInviteButtonUnavailable(button,label)
		if not button or not label or not button.Parent or not label.Parent then return end
		label.Text="Unavailable"; label.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(220,150,150)
		task.delay(1.5,function() if button.Parent and label.Parent and button.Active then label.Text="Invite"; label.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1) end end)
	end
	local function sendInvite(friend, button, label)
		local userId=getUserId(friend)
		if not userId or userId&lt;=0 then setInviteButtonUnavailable(button,label); return end
		local key=tostring(userId)
		if InviteMenu.InvitedFriendIds[key] then return end
		local canCheck,canSend=pcall(function() return SocialService:CanSendGameInviteAsync(localPlayer,userId) end)
		if not canCheck or canSend==false then
			if not canCheck then warn("[Players Invite] CanSendGameInviteAsync failed: " .. tostring(canSend)) end
			setInviteButtonUnavailable(button,label); return
		end
		local options
		local madeOptions,optionsError=pcall(function()
			options=Instance.new("ExperienceInviteOptions"); options.InviteUser=userId
			options.PromptMessage="Invite " .. (getInviteUsername(friend)~="" and getInviteUsername(friend) or "friend") .. " to join?"
		end)
		if not madeOptions or not options then
			warn("[Players Invite] Could not create invite options: " .. tostring(optionsError)); if options then pcall(function() options:Destroy() end) end
			setInviteButtonUnavailable(button,label); return
		end
		InviteMenu.ActiveOptions=options
		local promptOk,promptError=pcall(function() SocialService:PromptGameInvite(localPlayer,options) end)
		if not promptOk then
			InviteMenu.ActiveOptions=nil; pcall(function() options:Destroy() end)
			warn("[Players Invite] PromptGameInvite failed: " .. tostring(promptError)); setInviteButtonUnavailable(button,label); return
		end
		InviteMenu.InvitedFriendIds[key]=true; friend.Invited=true
		button.ImageTransparency=1; button.Active=false; button.Selectable=false
		label.Text="Invited..."; label.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSans; label.TextSize=18; label.TextColor3=INVITED_COLOR
	end
	local function buildInviteRow(friend,index)
		local panel=InviteMenu.Panel
		local baseZ=(panel and panel.ZIndex or 1)+2
		local row=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		row.Name="InviteFriend_" .. tostring(friend.Id); row.BackgroundTransparency=1; row.BorderSizePixel=0
		row.Image=INVITE_ROW_IMAGE; row.ImageTransparency=0.85; row.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Slice; row.SliceCenter=Rect.new(10,10,10,10)
		row.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,INVITE_ROW_HEIGHT); row.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,12+(index-1)*(INVITE_ROW_HEIGHT+INVITE_ROW_GAP)); row.ZIndex=baseZ; row.Parent=InviteMenu.InviteList
		row.MouseEnter:Connect(function() row.ImageTransparency=0.65 end); row.MouseLeave:Connect(function() row.ImageTransparency=0.85 end)
		local avatarBackground=Instance.new("Frame")
		avatarBackground.Name="AvatarBackground"; avatarBackground.BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); avatarBackground.BorderSizePixel=2
		avatarBackground.BorderColor3=Color3.fromRGB(205,205,205); avatarBackground.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(36,36)
		avatarBackground.Position=UDim2.new(0,12,0.5,-18); avatarBackground.ZIndex=baseZ+1; avatarBackground.Parent=row
		local avatar=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		avatar.Name="Icon"; avatar.BackgroundTransparency=1; avatar.Image="rbxasset://textures/ui/Shell/Icons/DefaultProfileIcon.png"
		avatar.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(32,32); avatar.Position=UDim2.new(0,14,0.5,-16); avatar.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit; avatar.ZIndex=baseZ+2; avatar.Parent=row
		task.spawn(function()
			local userId=getUserId(friend); if not userId or userId&lt;=0 then return end
			local ok,url=pcall(function() return PlayersService:GetUserThumbnailAsync(userId,Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarBust,Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100) end)
			if avatar.Parent and row.Parent then avatar.Image=(ok and type(url)=="string" and url~="") and url or ("rbxthumb://type=AvatarBust&amp;id=" .. tostring(userId) .. "&amp;w=100&amp;h=100") end
		end)
		local username=Instance.new("TextLabel")
		username.Name="Username"; username.BackgroundTransparency=1; username.BorderSizePixel=0; username.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSans; username.TextSize=22
		username.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); username.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; username.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		username.TextWrapped=false; username.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		local accountUsername=getInviteUsername(friend); username.Text=accountUsername~="" and accountUsername or "Loading username..."
		username.Size=UDim2.new(1,-180,0,30); username.Position=UDim2.new(0,60,0,6); username.ZIndex=baseZ+2; username.Parent=row
		local statusText,statusColor=getInviteStatus(friend)
		local status=Instance.new("TextLabel")
		status.Name="Status"; status.BackgroundTransparency=1; status.BorderSizePixel=0; status.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSans; status.TextSize=16
		status.TextColor3=statusColor; status.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; status.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		status.Text=statusText; status.Size=UDim2.new(1,-180,0,18); status.Position=UDim2.new(0,60,0,37); status.ZIndex=baseZ+2; status.Parent=row
		local button,label=makeInviteButton(row,"InviteButton","Invite",UDim2.fromOffset(INVITE_BUTTON_WIDTH,INVITE_BUTTON_HEIGHT),baseZ+3)
		button.Position=UDim2.new(1,-(INVITE_BUTTON_WIDTH+12),0.5,-(INVITE_BUTTON_HEIGHT/2)); label.TextSize=18
		if InviteMenu.InvitedFriendIds[tostring(friend.Id)] then
			button.ImageTransparency=1; button.Active=false; button.Selectable=false; label.Text="Invited..."; label.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSans; label.TextSize=18; label.TextColor3=INVITED_COLOR
		else button.Activated:Connect(function() sendInvite(friend,button,label) end) end
		return row
	end
	rebuildInviteRows=function()
		if not InviteMenu.InviteList or not InviteMenu.EmptyLabel then return end
		for _,row in ipairs(InviteMenu.Rows) do if row then pcall(function() row:Destroy() end) end end
		table.clear(InviteMenu.Rows)
		local query=InviteMenu.SearchBox and InviteMenu.SearchBox.Text or ""; local count=0
		for _,friend in ipairs(InviteMenu.Friends) do if friendMatchesSearch(friend,query) then count+=1; table.insert(InviteMenu.Rows,buildInviteRow(friend,count)) end end
		InviteMenu.InviteList.CanvasPosition=Vector2.new(0,0)
		InviteMenu.InviteList.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,24+math.max(0,count*(INVITE_ROW_HEIGHT+INVITE_ROW_GAP)-INVITE_ROW_GAP))
		if count&gt;0 then InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Visible=false else
			if InviteMenu.Loading then InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Text="Loading friends..."
			elseif InviteMenu.LoadFailed then InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Text="Couldn't load friends. Try again."
			elseif #InviteMenu.Friends&gt;0 then InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Text="No friends match your search."
			else InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Text="No friends to invite." end
			InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Visible=true
		end
	end
	updateInviteLayout=function()
		if not InviteMenu.Panel or not InviteMenu.Panel.Parent then return end
		local viewport=NewRobloxGui.AbsoluteSize
		if viewport.X&lt;=0 or viewport.Y&lt;=0 then local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera; viewport=camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280,720) end
		local compact=viewport.X&lt;620
		local width=math.max(240,math.min(800,viewport.X-(compact and 16 or 32)))
		local height=math.max(240,math.min(760,viewport.Y-(compact and 16 or 32)))
		local headerHeight=compact and 94 or 60
		InviteMenu.Panel.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(width,height)
		InviteMenu.BackButton.Size=compact and UDim2.fromOffset(76,42) or UDim2.fromOffset(132,56)
		InviteMenu.BackButton.Position=compact and UDim2.new(0,8,0,4) or UDim2.new(0,8,0,0)
		local title=InviteMenu.Header:FindFirstChild("InviteFriendsTitle")
		if title then
			if compact then title.Size=UDim2.new(1,-94,0,42); title.Position=UDim2.new(0,88,0,3)
			else title.Size=UDim2.new(0,240,0,44); title.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,-120,0,8) end
		end
		if compact then InviteMenu.SearchFrame.Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,34); InviteMenu.SearchFrame.Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,53)
		else InviteMenu.SearchFrame.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(220,34); InviteMenu.SearchFrame.Position=UDim2.new(1,-228,0,15) end
		InviteMenu.InviteList.Position=UDim2.new(0,6,0,headerHeight+5); InviteMenu.InviteList.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,1,-(headerHeight+10))
	end
	local function restoreInviteHubVisibility()
		local snapshot=InviteMenu.HubVisibilitySnapshot
		if snapshot then for _,entry in ipairs(snapshot) do if entry.Object and entry.Visible~=nil and entry.Object.Parent then pcall(function() entry.Object.Visible=entry.Visible end) end end end
		InviteMenu.HubVisibilitySnapshot=nil
	end
	local function createInviteOverlay()
		if InviteMenu.Panel and InviteMenu.Panel.Parent then return true end
		local hub=this.HubRef
		if not hub or not hub.Shield or typeof(hub.Shield)~="Instance" then warn("[Players Invite] SettingsHub.Shield is unavailable."); return false end
		local shield=hub.Shield
		if not shield:IsA("GuiObject") and not shield:IsA("ScreenGui") then warn("[Players Invite] SettingsHub.Shield is not a GUI object."); return false end
		local shieldZ=1; pcall(function() shieldZ=shield.ZIndex end); local baseZ=shieldZ+INVITE_PAGE_ZINDEX_OFFSET
		InviteMenu.Panel=Instance.new("Frame"); InviteMenu.Panel.Name="LegacyInviteFriendsPage"; InviteMenu.Panel.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
		InviteMenu.Panel.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0); InviteMenu.Panel.BackgroundTransparency=1; InviteMenu.Panel.BorderSizePixel=0
		InviteMenu.Panel.ClipsDescendants=false; InviteMenu.Panel.Active=true; InviteMenu.Panel.Visible=false; InviteMenu.Panel.ZIndex=baseZ; InviteMenu.Panel.Parent=shield
		InviteMenu.Header=Instance.new("Frame"); InviteMenu.Header.Name="InviteHeader"; InviteMenu.Header.BackgroundTransparency=1; InviteMenu.Header.BorderSizePixel=0
		InviteMenu.Header.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,60); InviteMenu.Header.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0); InviteMenu.Header.ZIndex=baseZ+1; InviteMenu.Header.Parent=InviteMenu.Panel
		InviteMenu.BackButton=makeInviteButton(InviteMenu.Header,"InviteBackButton","Back",UDim2.fromOffset(132,56),baseZ+4)
		InviteMenu.BackButton.Position=UDim2.new(0,8,0,0); InviteMenu.BackButton.Activated:Connect(function() closeInviteMenu(true) end)
		local title=Instance.new("TextLabel"); title.Name="InviteFriendsTitle"; title.BackgroundTransparency=1; title.BorderSizePixel=0
		title.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSansBold; title.TextSize=27; title.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); title.Text="Invite Friends"
		title.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center; title.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center; title.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(240,44)
		title.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,-120,0,8); title.ZIndex=baseZ+3; title.Parent=InviteMenu.Header
		InviteMenu.SearchFrame=Instance.new("Frame"); InviteMenu.SearchFrame.Name="SearchFrame"; InviteMenu.SearchFrame.BackgroundTransparency=1; InviteMenu.SearchFrame.BorderSizePixel=0
		InviteMenu.SearchFrame.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(220,34); InviteMenu.SearchFrame.Position=UDim2.new(1,-228,0,15); InviteMenu.SearchFrame.ZIndex=baseZ+3; InviteMenu.SearchFrame.Parent=InviteMenu.Header
		local corner=Instance.new("UICorner"); corner.Name="SearchCorner"; corner.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,2); corner.Parent=InviteMenu.SearchFrame
		local border=Instance.new("UIStroke"); border.Name="SearchBorder"; border.Color=Color3.fromRGB(170,170,170); border.Thickness=1; border.Transparency=0; border.Parent=InviteMenu.SearchFrame
		InviteMenu.SearchIcon=Instance.new("ImageLabel"); InviteMenu.SearchIcon.Name="SearchIcon"; InviteMenu.SearchIcon.BackgroundTransparency=1; InviteMenu.SearchIcon.BorderSizePixel=0
		InviteMenu.SearchIcon.Image=INVITE_SEARCH_ICON_IMAGE; InviteMenu.SearchIcon.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit; InviteMenu.SearchIcon.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(20,20)
		InviteMenu.SearchIcon.Position=UDim2.new(0,8,0.5,-10); InviteMenu.SearchIcon.ZIndex=baseZ+4; InviteMenu.SearchIcon.Parent=InviteMenu.SearchFrame
		InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder=Instance.new("TextLabel"); InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.Name="SearchPlaceholder"; InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.BackgroundTransparency=1
		InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSans; InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.TextSize=18; InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(190,190,190)
		InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.Text="Search for friends"; InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.Size=UDim2.new(1,-42,1,0); InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.Position=UDim2.new(0,34,0,0); InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.ZIndex=baseZ+4; InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.Parent=InviteMenu.SearchFrame
		InviteMenu.SearchBox=Instance.new("TextBox"); InviteMenu.SearchBox.Name="SearchBox"; InviteMenu.SearchBox.BackgroundTransparency=1; InviteMenu.SearchBox.BorderSizePixel=0
		InviteMenu.SearchBox.ClearTextOnFocus=false; InviteMenu.SearchBox.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSans; InviteMenu.SearchBox.TextSize=18; InviteMenu.SearchBox.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1)
		InviteMenu.SearchBox.PlaceholderText=""; InviteMenu.SearchBox.Text=""; InviteMenu.SearchBox.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; InviteMenu.SearchBox.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		InviteMenu.SearchBox.Size=UDim2.new(1,-42,1,0); InviteMenu.SearchBox.Position=UDim2.new(0,34,0,0); InviteMenu.SearchBox.ZIndex=baseZ+5; InviteMenu.SearchBox.Parent=InviteMenu.SearchFrame
		InviteMenu.SearchBox.Focused:Connect(function() InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.Visible=false; InviteMenu.SearchIcon.Visible=InviteMenu.SearchBox.Text=="" end)
		InviteMenu.SearchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function() local empty=InviteMenu.SearchBox.Text==""; InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.Visible=empty; InviteMenu.SearchIcon.Visible=empty end)
		InviteMenu.SearchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			local empty=InviteMenu.SearchBox.Text==""; InviteMenu.SearchIcon.Visible=empty; InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.Visible=empty and not InviteMenu.SearchBox:IsFocused()
			if InviteMenu.Panel and InviteMenu.Panel.Visible then rebuildInviteRows() end
		end)
		InviteMenu.InviteList=Instance.new("ScrollingFrame"); InviteMenu.InviteList.Name="InviteList"; InviteMenu.InviteList.BackgroundTransparency=1; InviteMenu.InviteList.BorderSizePixel=0
		InviteMenu.InviteList.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,1,-70); InviteMenu.InviteList.Position=UDim2.new(0,6,0,65); InviteMenu.InviteList.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
		InviteMenu.InviteList.ScrollBarThickness=6; InviteMenu.InviteList.ScrollingDirection=Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y; InviteMenu.InviteList.ElasticBehavior=Enum.ElasticBehavior.WhenScrollable
		InviteMenu.InviteList.ZIndex=baseZ+2; InviteMenu.InviteList.Parent=InviteMenu.Panel
		InviteMenu.EmptyLabel=Instance.new("TextLabel"); InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Name="EmptyLabel"; InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.BackgroundTransparency=1; InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSans
		InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.TextSize=20; InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(190,190,190); InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Text="Loading friends..."
		InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center; InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center; InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,40)
		InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,14); InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Visible=true; InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.ZIndex=baseZ+3; InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Parent=InviteMenu.InviteList
		InviteMenu.LayoutConnection=safeConnect(NewRobloxGui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"),updateInviteLayout)
		if hub.SettingsShowSignal then InviteMenu.SettingsVisibilityConnection=safeConnect(hub.SettingsShowSignal,function(visible) if not visible and InviteMenu.Panel and InviteMenu.Panel.Visible then closeInviteMenu(false) end end) end
		if hub.Shield and hub.Shield:IsA("GuiObject") then
			InviteMenu.ShieldVisibilityConnection=safeConnect(hub.Shield:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"),function() if not hub.Shield.Visible and InviteMenu.Panel and InviteMenu.Panel.Visible then closeInviteMenu(false) end end)
			local clipping=hub.ClippingShield or hub.Shield.Parent
			if clipping and clipping:IsA("GuiObject") then InviteMenu.ClippingVisibilityConnection=safeConnect(clipping:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"),function() if not clipping.Visible and InviteMenu.Panel and InviteMenu.Panel.Visible then closeInviteMenu(false) end end) end
		end
		if this.Hidden and this.Hidden.Event then InviteMenu.PageHiddenConnection=safeConnect(this.Hidden.Event,function() if InviteMenu.Panel and InviteMenu.Panel.Visible then closeInviteMenu(false) end end) end
		updateInviteLayout(); return true
	end
	closeInviteMenu=function(returnToPlayersPage)
		if InviteMenu.Panel then InviteMenu.Panel.Visible=false end
		if InviteMenu.SearchBox then pcall(function() InviteMenu.SearchBox:ReleaseFocus() end) end
		restoreInviteHubVisibility()
		if InviteMenu.SearchBox then pcall(function() InviteMenu.SearchBox.Text="" end) end
		if InviteMenu.SearchIcon then InviteMenu.SearchIcon.Visible=true end
		if InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder then InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.Visible=true end
		if InviteMenu.InviteList then InviteMenu.InviteList.CanvasPosition=Vector2.new(0,0) end
		if returnToPlayersPage then local hub=this.HubRef; if hub and type(hub.SwitchToPage)=="function" then pcall(function() hub:SwitchToPage(this,false,1) end) end end
		local selected=getCurrentSelection()
		if selected and InviteMenu.Panel then local ok,inside=pcall(function() return selected==InviteMenu.Panel or selected:IsDescendantOf(InviteMenu.Panel) end); if ok and inside then setCurrentSelection(nil) end end
	end
	local function startHubVisibilityWatch()
		if InviteMenu.VisibilityWatchRunning then return end
		InviteMenu.VisibilityWatchRunning=true
		task.spawn(function()
			while not destroyed and InviteMenu.Panel and InviteMenu.Panel.Visible do
				task.wait(0.1); if not (InviteMenu.Panel and InviteMenu.Panel.Visible) then break end
				local hub=this.HubRef
				if hub then local hubVisible,shieldVisible; pcall(function() hubVisible=hub.Visible end); pcall(function() if hub.Shield then shieldVisible=hub.Shield.Visible end end)
					if hubVisible==false or (hubVisible==nil and shieldVisible==false) then closeInviteMenu(false); break end
				end
			end
			InviteMenu.VisibilityWatchRunning=false
		end)
	end
	local function openInviteFriends()
		if not createInviteOverlay() or InviteMenu.Panel.Visible then return end
		local hub=this.HubRef
		if hub then
			local snapshot={}
			local candidates={{Object=findHubGuiObject(hub,"HubBar","HubBar")},{Object=findHubGuiObject(hub,"PageViewClipper","PageViewClipper")},{Object=findHubGuiObject(hub,"BottomButtonFrame","BottomButtonFrame")}}
			for _,candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
				local object=candidate.Object
				if object and object:IsA("GuiObject") then
					local exists=false; for _,saved in ipairs(snapshot) do if saved.Object==object then exists=true; break end end
					if not exists then table.insert(snapshot,{Object=object,Visible=object.Visible}) end
				end
			end
			InviteMenu.HubVisibilitySnapshot=snapshot
			for _,saved in ipairs(snapshot) do pcall(function() saved.Object.Visible=false end) end
		end
		InviteMenu.Panel.Visible=true; startHubVisibilityWatch(); InviteMenu.Loading=true; InviteMenu.LoadFailed=false; InviteMenu.Friends={}
		InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Text="Loading friends..."; InviteMenu.EmptyLabel.Visible=true; InviteMenu.SearchBox.Text=""; InviteMenu.SearchIcon.Visible=true; InviteMenu.SearchPlaceholder.Visible=true
		InviteMenu.InviteList.CanvasPosition=Vector2.new(0,0); rebuildInviteRows()
		task.spawn(function()
			local friends,success=fetchInviteFriends(); if destroyed then return end
			InviteMenu.Friends=friends; InviteMenu.Loading=false; InviteMenu.LoadFailed=not success; resolveMissingUsernames(friends)
			if InviteMenu.Panel and InviteMenu.Panel.Visible then rebuildInviteRows() end
		end)
	end

	------------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- PLAYER ROW ACTIONS
	------------------------------------------------------------------------
	local function showPlayerActions(player)
		return player~=nil and player~=localPlayer and player.UserId&gt;0 and localPlayer.UserId&gt;0
	end
	local function findRowButton(row,name)
		if not row then return nil end
		local found=row:FindFirstChild(name,true)
		if found and found:IsA("GuiButton") then return found end
		return nil
	end
	local function clearRowButton(row,name)
		local found=findRowButton(row,name)
		if found then
			local selected=getCurrentSelection()
			if selected then local ok,inside=pcall(function() return selected==found or selected:IsDescendantOf(found) end); if ok and inside then setCurrentSelection(nil) end end
			found:Destroy()
		end
	end
	local function canRequestFriend(status)
		return status==Enum.FriendStatus.Unknown or status==Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend or status==Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestReceived
	end
	local function createReportButton(row,player)
		if not showPlayerActions(player) then return nil end
		local existing=findRowButton(row,"ReportPlayer")
		if existing then existing.Visible=true; existing.Active=true; existing.Selectable=true; existing.ZIndex=4; if not firstReportCandidate then firstReportCandidate=existing end; return existing end
		local reportButton,reportIcon=utility:MakeStyledImageButton("ReportPlayer",REPORT_PLAYER_IMAGE,UDim2.fromOffset(REPORT_BUTTON_WIDTH,REPORT_BUTTON_HEIGHT),UDim2.fromOffset(28,28),function()
			if player.Parent~=PlayersService then return end
			local hub=this.HubRef; local reportPage
			pcall(function() reportPage=hub and hub.ReportAbusePage end)
			if not reportPage and type(ReportAbuseMenu)=="table" then reportPage=ReportAbuseMenu end
			if not reportPage or type(reportPage.ReportPlayer)~="function" then warn("[Players] Report page unavailable."); return end
			if hub then pcall(function() if not reportPage.HubRef then reportPage.HubRef=hub end end) end
			local ok,err=pcall(function() reportPage:ReportPlayer(player) end)
			if not ok then warn("[Players] ReportPlayer failed: " .. tostring(err)); return end
			if hub and hub.Visible and type(hub.SwitchToPage)=="function" then local currentPage; pcall(function() currentPage=hub.Pages and hub.Pages.CurrentPage end); if currentPage~=reportPage then pcall(function() hub:SwitchToPage(reportPage,false,1) end) end end
		end)
		if not reportButton then warn("[Players] Utility.MakeStyledImageButton returned no Report button"); return nil end
		reportButton.Name="ReportPlayer"; reportButton.LayoutOrder=1; reportButton.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(REPORT_BUTTON_WIDTH,REPORT_BUTTON_HEIGHT)
		reportButton.Visible=true; reportButton.Active=true; reportButton.Selectable=true; reportButton.AutoButtonColor=false; reportButton.ZIndex=4
		reportButton.Parent=row:FindFirstChild("RightSideButtons") or row; reportButton.SelectionImageObject=playerLabelFakeSelection
		if reportIcon and reportIcon:IsA("ImageLabel") then reportIcon.Name="ReportPlayerImageLabel"; reportIcon.Image=REPORT_PLAYER_IMAGE; reportIcon.ImageTransparency=0; reportIcon.BackgroundTransparency=1; reportIcon.Visible=true; reportIcon.ZIndex=5; reportIcon.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(28,28); reportIcon.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5); reportIcon.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0) end
		pcall(function() reportButton:SetAttribute("ActionOwnerUserId",player.UserId) end)
		local function updateHighlight() if row.Parent then row.ImageTransparency=getCurrentSelection()==reportButton and FRAME_SELECTED_TRANSPARENCY or FRAME_DEFAULT_TRANSPARENCY end end
		safeConnect(reportButton.SelectionGained,updateHighlight); safeConnect(reportButton.SelectionLost,updateHighlight)
		if not firstReportCandidate then firstReportCandidate=reportButton end
		return reportButton
	end
	local function openPlayerView(userId)
		local id=tonumber(userId)
		if not id or id&lt;=0 then warn("[Players] View clicked with invalid UserId: " .. tostring(userId)); return false end
		local target=PlayersService:GetPlayerByUserId(id)
		if not target or target.Parent~=PlayersService then warn("[Players] View target is not in current player list: " .. tostring(id)); return false end
		local hub=this.HubRef; if hub and hub.Visible then pcall(function() hub:SetVisibility(false) end) end
		task.defer(function() fireGuiInspectAndBuy(id,"Players") end)
		return true
	end
	local function createViewButton(row,player)
		if not player or player.UserId&lt;=0 then return nil end
		local existing=findRowButton(row,"ViewButton")
		if existing then existing.Visible=true; existing.Active=true; existing.Selectable=true; existing.LayoutOrder=2; existing.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(VIEW_BUTTON_WIDTH,VIEW_BUTTON_HEIGHT); pcall(function() existing:SetAttribute("ViewUserId",player.UserId) end); return existing end
		local button=utility:MakeStyledButton("ViewButton","View",UDim2.fromOffset(VIEW_BUTTON_WIDTH,VIEW_BUTTON_HEIGHT),function() end)
		if not button or not button:IsA("GuiButton") then warn("[Players] Utility.MakeStyledButton did not return View button"); return nil end
		button.Name="ViewButton"; button.LayoutOrder=2; button.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(VIEW_BUTTON_WIDTH,VIEW_BUTTON_HEIGHT); button.Visible=true; button.Active=true; button.Selectable=true; button.AutoButtonColor=false; button.ZIndex=4
		button.Parent=row:FindFirstChild("RightSideButtons") or row; button.SelectionImageObject=playerLabelFakeSelection; button:SetAttribute("ViewUserId",player.UserId)
		local label=button:FindFirstChild("ViewButtonTextLabel")
		if label and label:IsA("TextLabel") then label.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSansBold; label.TextSize=24; label.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); label.ZIndex=5 end
		button.Activated:Connect(function() if button.Parent then openPlayerView(button:GetAttribute("ViewUserId")) end end)
		local function updateHighlight() if row.Parent then row.ImageTransparency=getCurrentSelection()==button and FRAME_SELECTED_TRANSPARENCY or FRAME_DEFAULT_TRANSPARENCY end end
		safeConnect(button.SelectionGained,updateHighlight); safeConnect(button.SelectionLost,updateHighlight)
		return button
	end

	-- This follows the original CoreScript's split between styled action buttons
	-- and passive status controls. Passive desktop labels are separate plain
	-- TextButtons, not Utility buttons with their original background restored.
	local function createFriendStatusButton(row,player,status,portrait)
		if not showPlayerActions(player) then return nil end
		local isFriend=status==Enum.FriendStatus.Friend
		local requestSent=status==Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestSent
		local passive=isFriend or requestSent
		local canRequestNow=canRequestFriend(status)
		local statusText=isFriend and "Friend" or (requestSent and "Request Sent" or "Add Friend")
		local styleKey=(portrait and "Portrait" or "Text") .. (passive and "Passive" or "Action")
		local button=findRowButton(row,"FriendStatus")
		if button and button:GetAttribute("YekuFriendStatusStyle")~=styleKey then clearRowButton(row,"FriendStatus"); button=nil end

		local function requestFriend()
			if not player or player.Parent~=PlayersService then return end
			local currentStatus=getFriendStatus(player)
			if not canRequestFriend(currentStatus) then return end
			if PreventFriendingRemovedPlayers and player.Parent~=PlayersService then return end
			if safeRequestFriendship(player) and queueRefresh then task.delay(0.25,queueRefresh); task.delay(1,queueRefresh) end
		end

		if not button then
			if portrait and passive then
				-- Original CoreScript portrait status control: menu-slice button
				-- behind Friend / Request Sent glyphs.
				button=Instance.new("ImageButton")
				button.Name="FriendStatus"; button.BackgroundTransparency=1; button.BorderSizePixel=0
				button.Image=INVITE_BUTTON_IMAGE; button.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Slice; button.SliceCenter=Rect.new(8,6,46,44)
				button.AutoButtonColor=false; button.Active=true; button.Selectable=true
				local icon=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
				icon.Name="FriendStatusImageLabel"; icon.BackgroundTransparency=1; icon.BorderSizePixel=0
				icon.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5); icon.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0); icon.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(28,28); icon.ZIndex=5; icon.Parent=button
			elseif portrait then
				local icon
				button,icon=utility:MakeStyledImageButton("FriendStatus",ADD_FRIEND_IMAGE,UDim2.fromOffset(46,46),UDim2.fromOffset(28,28),requestFriend)
				if not button or not button:IsA("GuiButton") then warn("[Players] Failed to create Add Friend icon button"); return nil end
				if icon and icon:IsA("ImageLabel") then icon.Name="FriendStatusImageLabel"; icon.BackgroundTransparency=1; icon.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5); icon.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0); icon.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(28,28); icon.ZIndex=5 end
			elseif passive then
				-- Important: passive state has no styled background. The actual
				-- gray color is explicit; don't fake gray with a white label.
				button=Instance.new("TextButton")
				button.Name="FriendStatus"; button.BackgroundTransparency=1; button.BorderSizePixel=0
				button.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSans; button.FontSize=Enum.FontSize.Size24; button.TextSize=24
				button.TextColor3=INVITE_PASSIVE_TEXT_COLOR; button.TextTransparency=0; button.TextStrokeTransparency=1
				button.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center; button.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
				button.TextWrapped=false; button.TextScaled=false; button.RichText=false; button.Text=statusText
				button.AutoButtonColor=false; button.Active=true; button.Selectable=true
			else
				-- The Add Friend state alone receives Utility's legacy styling.
				local label
				button,label=utility:MakeStyledButton("FriendStatus","Add Friend",UDim2.fromOffset(FRIEND_BUTTON_WIDTH,FRIEND_BUTTON_HEIGHT),requestFriend)
				if not button or not button:IsA("GuiButton") then warn("[Players] Failed to create Add Friend text button"); return nil end
				if label and label:IsA("TextLabel") then label.Name="FriendStatusTextLabel"; label.Text="Add Friend"; label.ZIndex=5; label.Position=label.Position+UDim2.new(0,0,0,1); label.Visible=true end
			end
		end
		if not button then return nil end
		button.Name="FriendStatus"; button.LayoutOrder=3
		button.Size=portrait and UDim2.fromOffset(46,46) or UDim2.fromOffset(FRIEND_BUTTON_WIDTH,FRIEND_BUTTON_HEIGHT)
		button.Visible=true; button.ZIndex=4; button.Selectable=true; button.AutoButtonColor=false
		button.SelectionImageObject=playerLabelFakeSelection; button.Parent=row:FindFirstChild("RightSideButtons") or row
		button.Active=passive or canRequestNow
		pcall(function() button:SetAttribute("PortraitLayout",portrait==true); button:SetAttribute("ActionOwnerUserId",player.UserId); button:SetAttribute("YekuFriendStatusStyle",styleKey) end)

		if portrait then
			local icon=button:FindFirstChild("FriendStatusImageLabel")
			if icon and icon:IsA("ImageLabel") then
				icon.Image=isFriend and FRIEND_IMAGE or ADD_FRIEND_IMAGE
				icon.ImageTransparency=requestSent and 0.5 or 0
				icon.BackgroundTransparency=1; icon.Visible=true; icon.ZIndex=button.ZIndex+1
				icon.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5); icon.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0); icon.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(28,28)
			end
			if passive then
				button.BackgroundTransparency=1; button.Image=INVITE_BUTTON_IMAGE; button.ImageTransparency=0
				button.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Slice; button.SliceCenter=Rect.new(8,6,46,44)
			end
		elseif passive then
			button.BackgroundTransparency=1; button.Text=statusText
			button.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSans; button.FontSize=Enum.FontSize.Size24; button.TextSize=24
			button.TextColor3=INVITE_PASSIVE_TEXT_COLOR; button.TextTransparency=0; button.TextStrokeTransparency=1
			button.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center; button.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
			button.TextWrapped=false; button.TextScaled=false; button.RichText=false
		else
			local label=button:FindFirstChild("FriendStatusTextLabel")
			if label and label:IsA("TextLabel") then
				label.Text="Add Friend"; label.TextTransparency=0; label.Visible=true; label.ZIndex=button.ZIndex+1
			elseif button:IsA("TextButton") then button.Text="Add Friend" end
		end
		local function updateHighlight() if row.Parent then row.ImageTransparency=getCurrentSelection()==button and FRAME_SELECTED_TRANSPARENCY or FRAME_DEFAULT_TRANSPARENCY end end
		safeConnect(button.SelectionGained,updateHighlight); safeConnect(button.SelectionLost,updateHighlight)
		if UserInputService.GamepadEnabled and canRequestNow and not firstFriendSelectionSet then
			firstFriendSelectionSet=true; task.defer(function() if button.Parent then setCurrentSelection(button) end end)
		end
		return button
	end

	local function refreshRowActions(row,player)
		local right=row:FindFirstChild("RightSideButtons")
		if not right then return end
		if not player or player.Parent~=PlayersService or player.UserId&lt;=0 then clearRowButton(row,"FriendStatus"); clearRowButton(row,"ReportPlayer"); clearRowButton(row,"ViewButton"); right.Visible=false; return end
		right.Visible=true; right.ClipsDescendants=false
		local portrait=isPortrait() and UserInputService.TouchEnabled
		createViewButton(row,player)
		if player==localPlayer then clearRowButton(row,"FriendStatus"); clearRowButton(row,"ReportPlayer"); return end
		createReportButton(row,player)
		createFriendStatusButton(row,player,getFriendStatus(player),portrait)
	end

	local function createPlayerRow(player,index,offset)
		local row=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		row.Name="PlayerLabel" .. player.Name; row.LayoutOrder=index; row.Image="rbxasset://textures/ui/dialog_white.png"
		row.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Slice; row.SliceCenter=Rect.new(10,10,10,10); row.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,PLAYER_ROW_HEIGHT)
		row.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,offset+(index-1)*PLAYER_ROW_SPACING); row.BackgroundTransparency=1; row.ImageTransparency=FRAME_DEFAULT_TRANSPARENCY; row.BorderSizePixel=0; row.ZIndex=2
		local icon=Instance.new("ImageLabel"); icon.Name="Icon"; icon.BackgroundTransparency=1; icon.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(36,36); icon.Position=UDim2.new(0,12,0,13); icon.Image=getThumbnail(player.UserId); icon.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit; icon.ZIndex=3; icon.Parent=row
		local nameLabel=Instance.new("TextLabel"); nameLabel.Name="NameLabel"; nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency=1; nameLabel.BorderSizePixel=0
		nameLabel.Position=UDim2.new(0,60,0,0); nameLabel.Size=UDim2.new(1,-410,1,0); nameLabel.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSans; nameLabel.TextSize=24; nameLabel.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1)
		nameLabel.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; nameLabel.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center; nameLabel.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd; nameLabel.Text=player.Name; nameLabel.ZIndex=3; nameLabel.Parent=row
		pcall(function() nameLabel.Localize=false end)
		local right=Instance.new("Frame"); right.Name="RightSideButtons"; right.BackgroundTransparency=1; right.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0); right.Size=UDim2.new(1,-10,1,0); right.ZIndex=4; right.ClipsDescendants=false; right.Parent=row
		local layout=Instance.new("UIListLayout"); layout.Name="RightSideListLayout"; layout.FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal; layout.HorizontalAlignment=Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
		layout.VerticalAlignment=Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center; layout.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder; layout.Padding=UDim.new(0,RIGHT_BUTTON_PADDING); layout.Parent=right
		row.MouseEnter:Connect(function() row.ImageTransparency=FRAME_SELECTED_TRANSPARENCY end); row.MouseLeave:Connect(function() row.ImageTransparency=FRAME_DEFAULT_TRANSPARENCY end)
		return row
	end

	------------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- MOBILE RESET / LEAVE / RESUME CONTROLS
	------------------------------------------------------------------------
	local mobileButtons={}
	local mobileControlsOffset = 85
	local function setMobileButtonText(label,text)
		if label and label:IsA("TextLabel") then label.Text=text; label.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,-6) end
	end
	local function createMobileButton(name,text,callback)
		local button,label=utility:MakeStyledButton(name,text,UDim2.fromOffset(200,62),callback)
		if not button then return nil end
		button.Name=name; button.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(200,62); button.Visible=false; button.ZIndex=5; button.Parent=this.Page
		if label then label.FontSize=Enum.FontSize.Size24; label.TextSize=24; label.ZIndex=6; setMobileButtonText(label,text) end
		mobileButtons[name]={Button=button,Label=label}; return button
	end
	createMobileButton("ResetButton","Reset Character",function() if this.HubRef and this.HubRef.ResetCharacterPage then this.HubRef:SwitchToPage(this.HubRef.ResetCharacterPage,false,1) end end)
	createMobileButton("LeaveButton","Leave Game",function() if this.HubRef and this.HubRef.LeaveGamePage then this.HubRef:SwitchToPage(this.HubRef.LeaveGamePage,false,1) end end)
	createMobileButton("ResumeButton","Resume Game",function() if this.HubRef then this.HubRef:SetVisibility(false) end end)

	local function ensureInviteFriendsButton()
		if shareGameButton and shareGameButton.Parent then return end
		local row=Instance.new("ImageButton"); row.Name="InviteFriendsButton"; row.BackgroundTransparency=1; row.BorderSizePixel=0
		row.Image="rbxasset://textures/ui/dialog_white.png"; row.ImageTransparency=FRAME_DEFAULT_TRANSPARENCY; row.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Slice; row.SliceCenter=Rect.new(10,10,10,10)
		row.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,60); row.ZIndex=2; row.AutoButtonColor=false
		local icon=Instance.new("ImageLabel"); icon.Name="InviteIcon"; icon.BackgroundTransparency=1; icon.BorderSizePixel=0; icon.Image="rbxassetid://80022950003290"
		icon.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(28,28); icon.Position=UDim2.new(0,14,0.5,-14); icon.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit; icon.ImageColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); icon.ZIndex=3; icon.Parent=row
		local label=Instance.new("TextLabel"); label.Name="InviteFriendsButtonTextLabel"; label.BackgroundTransparency=1; label.BorderSizePixel=0; label.Font=Enum.Font.SourceSans
		label.FontSize=Enum.FontSize.Size24; label.TextSize=24; label.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); label.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; label.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		label.Text="Invite friends to play"; label.Size=UDim2.new(1,-62,1,0); label.Position=UDim2.new(0,54,0,0); label.ZIndex=3; label.Parent=row
		row.MouseEnter:Connect(function() row.ImageTransparency=FRAME_SELECTED_TRANSPARENCY end); row.MouseLeave:Connect(function() row.ImageTransparency=FRAME_DEFAULT_TRANSPARENCY end)
		row.Activated:Connect(openInviteFriends); shareGameButton=row; row.Parent=this.Page
	end

	------------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- REFRESH / LIFECYCLE
	------------------------------------------------------------------------
	local function updateMobileButtons()
		local smallTouch = isSmallTouchScreen()
		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or NewRobloxGui.AbsoluteSize
		local width = viewport and viewport.X or 390
		local hasRoomForThree = width &gt;= 720
		touchLayout = smallTouch
		local reset, leave, resume = mobileButtons.ResetButton, mobileButtons.LeaveButton, mobileButtons.ResumeButton
		local function setButton(record, visible, buttonWidth, buttonHeight, labelSize)
			if not record then return end
			local button = record.Button
			button.Visible = visible
			if visible then
				button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(buttonWidth, buttonHeight)
				if record.Label then
					record.Label.TextSize = labelSize
					record.Label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -6)
				end
			end
		end
		if smallTouch and hasRoomForThree then
			setButton(reset, true, 200, 62, 24)
			setButton(leave, true, 200, 62, 24)
			setButton(resume, true, 200, 62, 24)
			if reset then reset.Button.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -340, 0, 14) end
			if leave then leave.Button.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -100, 0, 14) end
			if resume then resume.Button.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 140, 0, 14) end
			mobileControlsOffset = 85
		elseif smallTouch then
			-- Narrow phones stack all three actions at the top of the Players page.
			-- This avoids clipped side-by-side controls in portrait orientation.
			local buttonWidth = math.max(140, math.min(200, width - 32))
			setButton(reset, true, buttonWidth, 48, 20)
			setButton(leave, true, buttonWidth, 48, 20)
			setButton(resume, true, buttonWidth, 48, 20)
			local x = -buttonWidth / 2
			if reset then reset.Button.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0, 12) end
			if leave then leave.Button.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0, 66) end
			if resume then resume.Button.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0, 120) end
			mobileControlsOffset = 180
		else
			setButton(reset, false, 200, 62, 24)
			setButton(leave, false, 200, 62, 24)
			setButton(resume, false, 200, 62, 24)
			mobileControlsOffset = 85
		end
	end
	local function refresh()
		refreshQueued=false; if destroyed or not this.Page or not this.Page.Parent then return end
		updateMobileButtons(); ensureInviteFriendsButton()
		local sortedPlayers=PlayersService:GetPlayers()
		table.sort(sortedPlayers,function(a,b) local an,bn=string.lower(a.Name),string.lower(b.Name); if an==bn then return a.UserId&lt;b.UserId end; return an&lt;bn end)
		local offset=touchLayout and mobileControlsOffset or 20
		if shareGameButton then shareGameButton.Visible=true; shareGameButton.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,offset); offset+=PLAYER_ROW_SPACING end
		firstFriendSelectionSet=false; firstReportCandidate=nil
		local liveRows={}
		for index,player in ipairs(sortedPlayers) do
			local row=existingPlayerLabels[player]
			if not row or not row.Parent then row=createPlayerRow(player,index,offset); row.Parent=this.Page; existingPlayerLabels[player]=row end
			row.Name="PlayerLabel" .. player.Name; row.LayoutOrder=index+1; row.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,offset+(index-1)*PLAYER_ROW_SPACING); row.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,PLAYER_ROW_HEIGHT)
			local icon=row:FindFirstChild("Icon")
			if icon and icon:IsA("ImageLabel") then local image=getThumbnail(player.UserId); if icon.Image~=image then icon.Image=image end end
			local nameLabel=row:FindFirstChild("NameLabel"); if nameLabel and nameLabel:IsA("TextLabel") then nameLabel.Text=player.Name end
			refreshRowActions(row,player); liveRows[player]=true
		end
		for player,row in pairs(existingPlayerLabels) do
			if not liveRows[player] or player.Parent~=PlayersService then
				local selected=getCurrentSelection()
				if row and selected then local ok,inside=pcall(function() return selected:IsDescendantOf(row) end); if ok and inside then setCurrentSelection(nil) end end
				if row then row:Destroy() end; existingPlayerLabels[player]=nil
			end
		end
		if UserInputService.GamepadEnabled and not firstFriendSelectionSet and firstReportCandidate then setCurrentSelection(firstReportCandidate) end
		local rowsForHeight=math.max(1,#sortedPlayers)
		this.Page.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,math.max(PLAYER_ROW_HEIGHT,offset+PLAYER_ROW_SPACING*rowsForHeight-5))
	end
	queueRefresh=function() if refreshQueued or destroyed then return end; refreshQueued=true; task.defer(refresh) end
	safeConnect(this.Displayed and this.Displayed.Event,refresh)
	local friendStatusSignal; pcall(function() friendStatusSignal=localPlayer.FriendStatusChanged end)
	friendStatusConnection=safeConnect(friendStatusSignal,function(player) if player and existingPlayerLabels[player] then refreshRowActions(existingPlayerLabels[player],player) else queueRefresh() end end)
	playerAddedConnection=PlayersService.PlayerAdded:Connect(queueRefresh)
	playerRemovingConnection=PlayersService.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player) local row=existingPlayerLabels[player]; if row then row:Destroy() end; existingPlayerLabels[player]=nil; queueRefresh() end)
	local function connectViewport()
		if viewportConnection then viewportConnection:Disconnect(); viewportConnection=nil end
		local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
		if camera then viewportConnection=safeConnect(camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"),queueRefresh) end
	end
	cameraChangedConnection=workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function() connectViewport(); queueRefresh() end)
	connectViewport()

	this.Destroy=function()
		destroyed=true
		if friendStatusConnection then friendStatusConnection:Disconnect(); friendStatusConnection=nil end
		if viewportConnection then viewportConnection:Disconnect(); viewportConnection=nil end
		if playerAddedConnection then playerAddedConnection:Disconnect(); playerAddedConnection=nil end
		if playerRemovingConnection then playerRemovingConnection:Disconnect(); playerRemovingConnection=nil end
		if cameraChangedConnection then cameraChangedConnection:Disconnect(); cameraChangedConnection=nil end
		if InviteMenu.LayoutConnection then InviteMenu.LayoutConnection:Disconnect(); InviteMenu.LayoutConnection=nil end
		if InviteMenu.SettingsVisibilityConnection then InviteMenu.SettingsVisibilityConnection:Disconnect(); InviteMenu.SettingsVisibilityConnection=nil end
		if InviteMenu.ShieldVisibilityConnection then InviteMenu.ShieldVisibilityConnection:Disconnect(); InviteMenu.ShieldVisibilityConnection=nil end
		if InviteMenu.ClippingVisibilityConnection then InviteMenu.ClippingVisibilityConnection:Disconnect(); InviteMenu.ClippingVisibilityConnection=nil end
		if InviteMenu.PageHiddenConnection then InviteMenu.PageHiddenConnection:Disconnect(); InviteMenu.PageHiddenConnection=nil end
		restoreInviteHubVisibility()
		for _,row in pairs(existingPlayerLabels) do if row then row:Destroy() end end; table.clear(existingPlayerLabels)
		for _,row in ipairs(InviteMenu.Rows) do if row then row:Destroy() end end
		if InviteMenu.Panel then InviteMenu.Panel:Destroy(); InviteMenu.Panel=nil end
		if shareGameButton then shareGameButton:Destroy(); shareGameButton=nil end
	end
	refresh()
	return this
end

local PageInstance=Initialize()
return PageInstance

                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: Record.lua
    Written by: jeditkacheff
    Version 1.2 - RemoteEvent-only commands / status-event state

    Commands are sent through RecordSomething (RemoteEvent:FireServer or executor-local BindableEvent:Fire).
    The executor bridge owns the native recorder call and publishes client-local
    status events. This page does not call VideoRecording GetCore/SetCore and
    never treats a click as proof that recording actually started.
]]

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local VRService = game:GetService("VRService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Settings = UserSettings()

local RobloxGui = script and script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui")
if not (RobloxGui and RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
	RobloxGui = CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15)
end
assert(RobloxGui and RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui"), "[Record] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing.")

local Modules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules", 15)
assert(Modules, "[Record] NewRobloxGui.Modules is missing.")
local SettingsFolder = Modules:WaitForChild("Settings", 15)
assert(SettingsFolder, "[Record] NewRobloxGui.Modules.Settings is missing.")
local utilityModule = SettingsFolder:WaitForChild("Utility", 15)
assert(utilityModule and utilityModule:IsA("ModuleScript"), "[Record] Settings.Utility is missing.")
local utility = require(utilityModule)

-- SetVerb was a RobloxScriptSecurity GuiButton method and was removed from the
-- client API. Do not probe button.SetVerb. Screenshot requests go through the
-- existing Yeku RemoteEvent; video recording requests use RecordSomething.
local function fireYekuRemoteEvent(eventName)
	local eventObject = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(eventName)
	if not eventObject then
		warn("[Record] ReplicatedStorage." .. eventName .. " is missing; Yeku bridge is unavailable.")
		return false
	end
	if not eventObject:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
		warn("[Record] ReplicatedStorage." .. eventName .. " must be a RemoteEvent, not " .. eventObject.ClassName .. ".")
		return false
	end

	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		eventObject:FireServer()
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("[Record] Could not fire " .. eventName .. ": " .. tostring(err))
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local function findRecordingEvent(name)
	local eventObject = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(name)
	if eventObject and (eventObject:IsA("BindableEvent") or eventObject:IsA("RemoteEvent")) then
		return eventObject
	end
	return nil
end

local function fireRecordSomething(action)
	local eventObject = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RecordSomething")
	if not eventObject then
		eventObject = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("RecordSomething", 3)
	end
	if not eventObject then
		warn("[Record] ReplicatedStorage.RecordSomething bridge is missing; start/end was not sent.")
		return false
	end
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		if eventObject:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			eventObject:FireServer(action)
		elseif eventObject:IsA("BindableEvent") then
			eventObject:Fire(action)
		else
			error("unsupported RecordSomething class: " .. eventObject.ClassName)
		end
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("[Record] Could not send RecordSomething(" .. tostring(action) .. "): " .. tostring(err))
		return false
	end
	print("[Record] Sent RecordSomething(" .. tostring(action) .. ")")
	return true
end


-- TenFootInterface is optional on desktop.
local isTenFootInterface = false
do
	local module = Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface")
		or Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface.lua")
	if module and module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		local ok, api = pcall(require, module)
		if ok and type(api) == "table" and type(api.IsEnabled) == "function" then
			local enabledOK, enabled = pcall(function()
				return api:IsEnabled()
			end)
			isTenFootInterface = enabledOK a
